// === GLOBAL DELETE CONFIRM ===
Future<bool> confirmDelete(BuildContext ctx, String itemName, {String extraWarning = ''}) async {
  return await showDialog<bool>(
    context: ctx,
    barrierDismissible: false,
    builder: (c) => AlertDialog(
      title: Row(children: [Icon(Icons.warning_amber_rounded, color: Colors.red), SizedBox(width:8), Text('Confirm Delete')]),
      content: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        Text('Are you sure you want to delete:', style: TextStyle(fontSize:13)),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        Container(
          width: double.infinity,
          padding: EdgeInsets.all(10),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.red.shade50, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), border: Border.all(color: Colors.red.shade200)),
          child: Text(itemName, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.red.shade800)),
        ),
        if(extraWarning.isNotEmpty)...[
          SizedBox(height:8),
          Text(extraWarning, style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, color: Colors.orange.shade800, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        ],
        SizedBox(height:8),
        Text('This action cannot be undone.', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, color: Colors.grey)),
      ]),
      actions: [
        TextButton(onPressed: ()=> Navigator.pop(c,false), child: Text('CANCEL')),
        ElevatedButton(
          style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.red),
          onPressed: ()=> Navigator.pop(c,true),
          child: Text('DELETE'),
        ),
      ],
    ),
  )??false;
}

class PermissionPage extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<PermissionAlert> perms;
  final List<Student> studs;
  final Function(Student,String) onGrant;
  PermissionPage({required this.perms, required this.studs, required this.onGrant});
  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Permissions OUT (${perms.length})'), backgroundColor: Colors.orange.shade800),
      body: perms.isEmpty? Center(child: Text('No one outside')) : ListView.builder(
        itemCount: perms.length,
        itemBuilder: (_,i){
          var p=perms[i];
          return Card(child: ListTile(title: Text('${p.studentName} - ${p.studentId}'), subtitle: Text('${p.timeOut} Reason:${p.reason} Phone:${p.parentPhone}'), trailing: Text('OUT', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red))));
        }
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(onPressed: (){
        Student? sel; String reason='Medical'; String search='';
        showDialog(context: c, builder: (ctx){
          return StatefulBuilder(builder: (ctx,setS){
            var filteredStuds = studs.where((s){ if(search.isEmpty) return true; return s.name.toLowerCase().contains(search.toLowerCase()) || s.idNo.toLowerCase().contains(search.toLowerCase()); }).toList();
            return AlertDialog(
              title: Text('Grant Permission - Search & Select'),
              content: Container(width: double.maxFinite, child: SingleChildScrollView(child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
                TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Search by Name / ID', prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search), border: OutlineInputBorder(), isDense:true), onChanged: (v)=>setS(()=>search=v)),
                SizedBox(height:8),
                Container(height: 200, decoration: BoxDecoration(border: Border.all(color: Colors.grey.shade300), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)), child: ListView.builder(itemCount: filteredStuds.length, itemBuilder: (c,i){ var s=filteredStuds[i]; bool isSel=sel?.idNo==s.idNo; return ListTile(dense:true, selected: isSel, selectedTileColor: Colors.orange[50], title: Text('${s.name}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12)), subtitle: Text('${s.cls} ${s.stream} | ${s.idNo}', style:TextStyle(fontSize:10)), trailing: isSel? Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: Colors.orange) : null, onTap: ()=>setS(()=>sel=s)); })),
                if(sel!=null) Card(color: Colors.orange[50], child: ListTile(dense:true, title: Text('Selected: ${sel!.name}', style:TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:12)), subtitle: Text('${sel!.cls} ${sel!.stream} - ${sel!.idNo}'))),
                TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Reason', border: OutlineInputBorder(), isDense:true), onChanged: (v)=>reason=v),
              ]))),
              actions: [TextButton(onPressed: ()=>Navigator.pop(ctx), child: Text('Cancel')), ElevatedButton(style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.orange), onPressed: (){ if(sel!=null){ onGrant(sel!,reason); Navigator.pop(ctx);} }, child: Text('Send for ${sel?.name??""}'))],
            );
          });
        });
      }, label: Text('Grant Permission'), icon: Icon(Icons.add_alert), backgroundColor: Colors.orange),
    );
  }
}

class BlockedPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final List<Student> studs;
  BlockedPage({required this.studs});
  @override _BlockedPageState createState()=>_BlockedPageState();
}

class _BlockedPageState extends State<BlockedPage> {
  String filterClass='All';
  String filterStream='All';
  String filterHostelType='All';
  String filterZone='GATE'; // GATE, CLASS, MESS, ANY
  String filterExpiryMode='All'; // All, ExpiredToday, 0-3d, 4-7d, 8-30d, >30d, ActiveUpTo, CustomRange
  int customMinLeft=-100;
  int customMaxLeft=0;
  DateTime? activeUpToDate;
  DateTime? expiryFromDate;
  DateTime? expiryToDate;
  String searchText='';
  bool showOnlyExpired=true;

  List<String> classes=['All','S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4','S.5','S.6'];
  List<String> zones=['GATE','CLASS','MESS','ANY'];

  List<String> get streams {
    if(filterClass=='All') return ['All','Blue','Red','Green','White','Sciences','Arts'];
    if(filterClass=='S.5'||filterClass=='S.6') return ['All','Sciences','Arts'];
    return ['All','Blue','Red','Green','White'];
  }

  DateTime _expiryDate(Student s, String zone){
    var z = s.zones[zone];
    if(z==null) return DateTime(2000);
    return z.issued.add(Duration(days: z.days));
  }

  int _leftDays(Student s, String zone){
    if(zone=='ANY'){
      // worst case: min left among all zones
      int g = s.zones['GATE']?.left??-999;
      int c = s.zones['CLASS']?.left??-999;
      int m = s.zones['MESS']?.left??-999;
      return [g,c,m].reduce((a,b)=> a<b? a:b);
    }
    return s.zones[zone]?.left??-999;
  }

  List<Student> get blockedAll {
    return widget.studs.where((s)=>
      (s.zones['GATE']?.left??0) <= 0 ||
      (s.zones['CLASS']?.left??0) <= 0 ||
      (s.zones['MESS']?.left??0) <= 0
    ).toList();
  }

  List<Student> get filtered {
    var l = List<Student>.from(showOnlyExpired? blockedAll : widget.studs);

    if(filterClass!='All') l=l.where((s)=>s.cls==filterClass).toList();
    if(filterStream!='All') l=l.where((s)=>s.stream==filterStream).toList();
    if(filterHostelType!='All') l=l.where((s)=>s.studentType==filterHostelType).toList();
    if(searchText.isNotEmpty) l=l.where((s)=> s.name.toLowerCase().contains(searchText.toLowerCase()) || s.idNo.toLowerCase().contains(searchText.toLowerCase())).toList();

    // Filter by left days mode
    l = l.where((s){
      int left = _leftDays(s, filterZone);
      DateTime exp = filterZone=='ANY'? _expiryDate(s,'GATE') : _expiryDate(s, filterZone);

      switch(filterExpiryMode){
        case 'All': return true;
        case 'ExpiredToday': return left==0 || left==-0;
        case '0-3d Ago': return left>=-3 && left<=0;
        case '4-7d Ago': return left>=-7 && left<=-4;
        case '8-30d Ago': return left>=-30 && left<=-8;
        case '>30d Ago': return left < -30;
        case 'ActiveUpTo':
          if(activeUpToDate==null) return true;
          // student was active up to that date means expiry >= that date, but now expired
          return exp.isBefore(activeUpToDate!.add(Duration(days:1))) && exp.isAfter(activeUpToDate!.subtract(Duration(days:60)));
        case 'ExpiredBefore':
          if(activeUpToDate==null) return true;
          return exp.isBefore(activeUpToDate!);
        case 'WillExpireOn':
          if(activeUpToDate==null) return true;
          return exp.year==activeUpToDate!.year && exp.month==activeUpToDate!.month && exp.day==activeUpToDate!.day;
        case 'CustomRange':
          return left>=customMinLeft && left<=customMaxLeft;
        case 'ExpiryDateRange':
          if(expiryFromDate==null || expiryToDate==null) return true;
          return exp.isAfter(expiryFromDate!.subtract(Duration(days:1))) && exp.isBefore(expiryToDate!.add(Duration(days:1)));
        default: return true;
      }
    }).toList();

    // sort by expiry - most recent first
    l.sort((a,b){
      int la=_leftDays(a, filterZone);
      int lb=_leftDays(b, filterZone);
      return lb.compareTo(la); // active first
    });
    return l;
  }

  Future<void> exportFilteredPDF() async {
    final pdf = pw.Document();
    pdf.addPage(
      pw.MultiPage(
        header: (c)=> pw.Column(children: [
          pw.Row(mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children: [
            pw.Text('Busalamu SS - BLOCKED / FILTERED REPORT', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold, fontSize:14)),
            pw.Text('Date: ${DateTime.now().toString().split(' ')[0]}', style: pw.TextStyle(fontSize:9)),
          ]),
          pw.SizedBox(height:4),
          pw.Text('Class: $filterClass | Stream: $filterStream | Hostel: $filterHostelType | Zone: $filterZone | Mode: $filterExpiryMode | Total: ${filtered.length}', style: pw.TextStyle(fontSize:10)),
          pw.Divider(),
        ]),
        build: (c)=>[
          pw.Table.fromTextArray(
            headers: ['Name','Class','ID','Zone $filterZone Left','Expiry Date','GATE','CLASS','MESS','Parent'],
            data: filtered.map((s){
              DateTime exp = filterZone=='ANY'? _expiryDate(s,'GATE') : _expiryDate(s, filterZone);
              return [
                s.name, '${s.cls} ${s.stream}', s.idNo,
                '${_leftDays(s, filterZone)}d',
                '${exp.toString().split(' ')[0]}',
                '${s.zones['GATE']?.left??0}d',
                '${s.zones['CLASS']?.left??0}d',
                '${s.zones['MESS']?.left??0}d',
                s.parentPhone
              ];
            }).toList(),
            headerStyle: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold, fontSize:8, color: PdfColors.white),
            headerDecoration: pw.BoxDecoration(color: PdfColors.red800),
            cellStyle: pw.TextStyle(fontSize:7),
          ),
        ]
      )
    );
    await Printing.sharePdf(bytes: await pdf.save(), filename: 'blocked_${filterZone}_${filterExpiryMode}_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}.pdf');
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text('BLOCKED ${filtered.length}/${widget.studs.length}'),
        backgroundColor: Colors.red.shade800,
        actions: [
          IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf), tooltip: 'Export PDF', onPressed: filtered.isEmpty? null : exportFilteredPDF),
        ],
      ),
      body: Column(children: [
        Card(margin: EdgeInsets.all(8), color: Colors.red[50], child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Column(children: [
          // Row 1: Class, Stream, Hostel
          Row(children: [
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterClass, isExpanded:true, items: classes.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Class: $e', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12)))).toList(), onChanged: (v){ setState(()=>filterClass=v!); if(!streams.contains(filterStream)) filterStream='All'; })),
            SizedBox(width:6),
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterStream, isExpanded:true, items: streams.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Stream: $e', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>filterStream=v!))),
            SizedBox(width:6),
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterHostelType, isExpanded:true, items: ['All','Day','Hostel'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Hostel: $e', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>filterHostelType=v!))),
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          // Row 2: Zone + Expiry Mode
          Row(children: [
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterZone, isExpanded:true, items: zones.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Zone: $e'))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>filterZone=v!))),
            SizedBox(width:6),
            Expanded(flex:2, child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterExpiryMode, isExpanded:true, items: ['All','ExpiredToday','0-3d Ago','4-7d Ago','8-30d Ago','>30d Ago','ExpiredBefore','WillExpireOn','ActiveUpTo','CustomRange','ExpiryDateRange'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text(e, style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>filterExpiryMode=v!))),
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          // Custom filters based on mode
          if(filterExpiryMode=='CustomRange') Row(children: [
            Expanded(child: TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Min Left (e.g. -30)', isDense:true, border: OutlineInputBorder()), keyboardType: TextInputType.number, onChanged: (v){ setState(()=> customMinLeft=int.tryParse(v)??-100); })),
            SizedBox(width:6),
            Expanded(child: TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Max Left (e.g. 0)', isDense:true, border: OutlineInputBorder()), keyboardType: TextInputType.number, onChanged: (v){ setState(()=> customMaxLeft=int.tryParse(v)??0); })),
          ]),
          if(filterExpiryMode=='ExpiredBefore' || filterExpiryMode=='WillExpireOn' || filterExpiryMode=='ActiveUpTo') Row(children: [
            Expanded(child: OutlinedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.calendar_today, size:16), label: Text(activeUpToDate==null? 'Pick Date' : 'Date: ${activeUpToDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}'), onPressed: () async { var d=await showDatePicker(context: context, initialDate: activeUpToDate??DateTime.now(), firstDate: DateTime(2024), lastDate: DateTime(3000)); if(d!=null) setState(()=> activeUpToDate=d); })),
            if(activeUpToDate!=null) IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.clear), onPressed: ()=> setState(()=> activeUpToDate=null)),
          ]),
          if(filterExpiryMode=='ExpiryDateRange') Row(children: [
            Expanded(child: OutlinedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.date_range, size:16), label: Text(expiryFromDate==null? 'From' : '${expiryFromDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}'), onPressed: () async { var d=await showDatePicker(context: context, initialDate: expiryFromDate??DateTime.now(), firstDate: DateTime(2024), lastDate: DateTime(3000)); if(d!=null) setState(()=> expiryFromDate=d!); })),
            SizedBox(width:4),
            Expanded(child: OutlinedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.date_range, size:16), label: Text(expiryToDate==null? 'To' : '${expiryToDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}'), onPressed: () async { var d=await showDatePicker(context: context, initialDate: expiryToDate??DateTime.now(), firstDate: DateTime(2024), lastDate: DateTime(3000)); if(d!=null) setState(()=> expiryToDate=d!); })),
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search), hintText: 'Search name / ID', border: OutlineInputBorder(), isDense:true), onChanged: (v)=> setState(()=> searchText=v)),
          SizedBox(height:6),
          Row(children: [
            Checkbox(value: showOnlyExpired, onChanged: (v)=> setState(()=> showOnlyExpired=v!)),
            Text('Only Expired/Blocked', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12)),
            Spacer(),
            ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.refresh), label: Text('Reset'), style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.blueGrey), onPressed: ()=>setState((){ filterClass='All'; filterStream='All'; filterHostelType='All'; filterZone='GATE'; filterExpiryMode='All'; searchText=''; activeUpToDate=null; expiryFromDate=null; expiryToDate=null; showOnlyExpired=true; })),
            SizedBox(width:6),
            ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf), label: Text('PDF ${filtered.length}'), style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.red), onPressed: filtered.isEmpty? null : exportFilteredPDF),
          ])
        ]))),
        Expanded(child: filtered.isEmpty? Center(child: Text('No students match filter')) : ListView.builder(itemCount: filtered.length, itemBuilder: (_,i){
          var s=filtered[i];
          DateTime exp = filterZone=='ANY'? _expiryDate(s,'GATE') : _expiryDate(s, filterZone);
          int left = _leftDays(s, filterZone);
          return Card(color: left<=0? Colors.red.shade50 : Colors.orange.shade50, elevation:2, margin: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:8, vertical:4), child: ListTile(
            leading: CircleAvatar(backgroundColor: left<=0? Colors.red : Colors.orange, child: Icon(left<=0? Icons.block : Icons.timer, color: Colors.white)),
            title: Text('${s.name} - ${s.idNo}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:13)),
            subtitle: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
              Text('${s.cls} ${s.stream} ${s.studentType} | $filterZone: ${left}d left | Expiry: ${exp.toString().split(' ')[0]}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.red.shade800)),
              Text('GATE:${s.zones['GATE']?.left}d CLASS:${s.zones['CLASS']?.left}d MESS:${s.zones['MESS']?.left}d | ${s.parentPhone}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)),
            ]),
            trailing: Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
              IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: Colors.green), tooltip: 'Grant Term (remaining)', onPressed: () async {
                // Grant term capped logic
                var termDoc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('settings').doc('currentTerm').get();
                int daysToGrant=30;
                if(termDoc.exists){
                  var t=TermSettings.fromMap(termDoc.data()!);
                  daysToGrant = t.endDate.difference(DateTime.now()).inDays+1;
                  if(daysToGrant<0) daysToGrant=0;
                }
                s.zones={'GATE':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:daysToGrant),'CLASS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:daysToGrant),'MESS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:daysToGrant)};
                await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('students').doc(s.safeDocId).update({'zones': s.zones.map((k,v)=>MapEntry(k,v.toMap()))});
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Granted $daysToGrant d to ${s.name}'), backgroundColor: Colors.green));
              }),
            ]),
          ));
        }))
      ]),
    );
  }
}

class GoBSplashScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  @override _GoBSplashScreenState createState() => _GoBSplashScreenState();
}

class _GoBSplashScreenState extends State<GoBSplashScreen> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late AnimationController _ctrl;
  late Animation<double> _fade;
  late Animation<double> _scale;
  late Animation<double> _slide;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _ctrl = AnimationController(vsync: this, duration: Duration(milliseconds: 2200));
    _fade = Tween<double>(begin: 0, end: 1).animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: _ctrl, curve: Interval(0.0, 0.6, curve: Curves.easeIn)));
    _scale = Tween<double>(begin: 0.5, end: 1.0).animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: _ctrl, curve: Interval(0.0, 0.7, curve: Curves.elasticOut)));
    _slide = Tween<double>(begin: 40, end: 0).animate(CurvedAnimation(parent: _ctrl, curve: Interval(0.3, 1.0, curve: Curves.easeOut)));
    _ctrl.forward();

    Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: 3500), () {
      if(mounted) Navigator.of(context).pushReplacement(PageRouteBuilder(
        transitionDuration: Duration(milliseconds: 800),
        pageBuilder: (_,a,b)=> LoginPage(),
        transitionsBuilder: (_,a,__,child)=> FadeTransition(opacity: a, child: child),
      ));
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _ctrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext c) {
    return Scaffold(
      body: Container(
        width: double.infinity,
        height: double.infinity,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            begin: Alignment.topLeft,
            end: Alignment.bottomRight,
            colors: [Color(0xFF0D47A1), Color(0xFF1565C0), Color(0xFF1E88E5), Color(0xFF42A5F5)],
          )
        ),
        child: AnimatedBuilder(
          animation: _ctrl,
          builder: (ctx, _) {
            return Column(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                Spacer(flex: 2),
                // BADGE / LOGO
                Opacity(
                  opacity: _fade.value,
                  child: Transform.scale(
                    scale: _scale.value,
                    child: Container(
                      height: 140, width: 140,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.white,
                        shape: BoxShape.circle,
                        border: Border.all(color: Colors.white, width: 4),
                        boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black26, blurRadius: 20, offset: Offset(0,10))],
                      ),
                      child: ClipOval(
                        child: Image.asset('assets/badge.png',
                          fit: BoxFit.cover,
                          errorBuilder: (_,__,___)=> Icon(Icons.security, size: 70, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1)),
                        )
                      )
                    )
                  )
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 30),
                // TITLE
                Transform.translate(
                  offset: Offset(0, _slide.value),
                  child: Opacity(
                    opacity: _fade.value,
                    child: Column(
                      children: [
                        Text('Welcome to the', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 18, letterSpacing: 2, fontWeight: FontWeight.w300)),
                        SizedBox(height: 6),
                        Text('GoB', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 58, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, letterSpacing: 4, shadows: [Shadow(color: Colors.black26, blurRadius: 10, offset: Offset(0,4))])),
                        SizedBox(height: 8),
                        Container(height: 3, width: 80, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.amber, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2))),
                        SizedBox(height: 12),
                        Text('GATE OF BRAINS', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.amber[200], fontSize: 13, letterSpacing: 5, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                        SizedBox(height: 4),
                        Text('Busalamu Secondary School', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 11, letterSpacing: 1)),
                      ]
                    )
                  )
                ),
                Spacer(flex: 2),
                // LOADER
                Opacity(
                  opacity: _fade.value,
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      SizedBox(width: 120, child: LinearProgressIndicator(backgroundColor: Colors.white24, valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation(Colors.white), minHeight: 2)),
                      SizedBox(height: 16),
                      Text('Securing Future Minds...', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white60, fontSize: 10, letterSpacing: 1)),
                      SizedBox(height: 40),
                    ]
                  )
                ),
                Padding(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 20),
                  child: Text('BSS GoB System v2.0 | 2026', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white38, fontSize: 9)),
                ),
              ]
            );
          }
        )
      )
    );
  }
}

class ZoneAccess {
  DateTime issued; int days;
  ZoneAccess({required this.issued, required this.days});
  int get left => issued.add(Duration(days: days)).difference(DateTime.now()).inDays;
  bool get expired => left <= 0;
  Map<String,dynamic> toMap()=>{'issued':issued.toIso8601String(),'days':days};
  static ZoneAccess fromMap(Map m)=>ZoneAccess(issued: DateTime.parse(m['issued']), days: m['days']);
}

class NoticeBoardItem {
  String id; String message; String author; String authorRole;
  DateTime createdAt; DateTime modifiedAt; String colorHex; bool isPinned;
  NoticeBoardItem({required this.id, required this.message, required this.author, this.authorRole='HT', required this.createdAt, required this.modifiedAt, this.colorHex='0xFFFFF9C4', this.isPinned=true});
  Map<String,dynamic> toMap()=>{'id':id,'message':message,'author':author,'authorRole':authorRole,'createdAt':createdAt.toIso8601String(),'modifiedAt':modifiedAt.toIso8601String(),'colorHex':colorHex,'isPinned':isPinned};
  static NoticeBoardItem fromMap(Map m)=>NoticeBoardItem(id:m['id'],message:m['message'],author:m['author']??'HT',authorRole:m['authorRole']??'HT',createdAt:DateTime.tryParse(m['createdAt']??'')??DateTime.now(),modifiedAt:DateTime.tryParse(m['modifiedAt']??'')??DateTime.now(),colorHex:m['colorHex']??'0xFFFFF9C4',isPinned:m['isPinned']??true);
}

class AppSubscription {
  DateTime expiryDate; DateTime startDate; bool isActive; 
  String package; String schoolName; String ownerWhatsApp; 
  double termlyPrice; double yearlyPrice;
  int expiryMillis; // THIS IS WHAT YOU SET IN CONSOLE

  AppSubscription({
    required this.expiryDate, required this.startDate,
    this.isActive=true, this.package='Trial',
    this.schoolName='Busalamu SS',
    this.ownerWhatsApp='256773845266',
    this.termlyPrice=200000, this.yearlyPrice=550000,
    required this.expiryMillis,
  });

  Map<String,dynamic> toMap()=>{
    'expiryMillis': expiryDate.millisecondsSinceEpoch,
    'startMillis': startDate.millisecondsSinceEpoch,
    'expiryDate': expiryDate.toIso8601String(),
    'startDate': startDate.toIso8601String(),
    'isActive': isActive,
    'package': package,
    'schoolName': schoolName,
    'ownerWhatsApp': ownerWhatsApp,
    'termlyPrice': termlyPrice,
    'yearlyPrice': yearlyPrice,
    'updatedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    'lastServerCheck': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
  };

  static AppSubscription fromMap(Map m){
    DateTime parseMillis(dynamic val, Duration fallback){
      try{
        if(val==null) return DateTime.now().add(fallback);
        if(val is int) return DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(val);
        if(val is String) return DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(int.parse(val));
        if(val is Map && val['seconds']!=null) return DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch((val['seconds']*1000).toInt());
        return DateTime.tryParse(val.toString()) ?? DateTime.now().add(fallback);
      }catch(_){ return DateTime.now().add(fallback); }
    }
    return AppSubscription(
      expiryDate: parseMillis(m['expiryMillis'] ?? m['expiryDate'], Duration(days: 30)),
      startDate: parseMillis(m['startMillis'] ?? m['startDate'], Duration(days: 0)),
      expiryMillis: m['expiryMillis'] ?? DateTime.now().add(Duration(days:30)).millisecondsSinceEpoch,
      isActive: m['isActive']??true,
      package: m['package']??'Trial',
      schoolName: m['schoolName']??'Busalamu SS',
      ownerWhatsApp: m['ownerWhatsApp']??'256773845266',
      termlyPrice: (m['termlyPrice']??200000).toDouble(),
      yearlyPrice: (m['yearlyPrice']??550000).toDouble(),
    );
  }
}

// ===== SERVER TIME FETCHER - STOPS BACK-DATING =====
Future<DateTime> getTrueServerTime() async {
  try{
    // Write server time and read it back - this is 100% server time, not device time
    final ref = FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('serverTime').doc('now');
    await ref.set({'time': FieldValue.serverTimestamp()}, SetOptions(merge:true));
    // Force server fetch, not cache
    final snap = await ref.get(GetOptions(source: Source.server));
    final ts = snap.data()?['time'] as Timestamp?;
    if(ts != null) return ts.toDate();
  }catch(e){
    print("Server time fail: $e");
  }
  // Fallback - but we will mark as suspicious
  return DateTime.now();
}

// ===== SECURE GATE - USE THIS INSTEAD OF YOUR 4 StreamBuilders =====
class SubscriptionGate extends StatefulWidget {
  final Widget child;
  SubscriptionGate({required this.child});
  @override _SubscriptionGateState createState()=>_SubscriptionGateState();
}

class _SubscriptionGateState extends State<SubscriptionGate>{
  bool _checkedBackDate = false;

  Future<bool> _isTampered(DateTime serverTime) async {
    // Save last valid server time locally to detect back-date
    // If device time is more than 2 days behind last server time, user changed date
    try{
      var prefs = await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('appSubscription').doc('meta').get();
      // Simple local check using SharedPreferences would be better, but using Firestore meta for demo
      // For full proof, use shared_preferences package
    }catch(_){}
    // If device DateTime.now() is 48h behind server time = tampered
    if(DateTime.now().isBefore(serverTime.subtract(Duration(hours: 48)))){
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    return FutureBuilder<DateTime>(
      future: getTrueServerTime(),
      builder: (ctx, timeSnap){
        if(!timeSnap.hasData) return Scaffold(body: Center(child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children:[CircularProgressIndicator(), SizedBox(height:10), Text("Checking license with server...")])));

        DateTime serverNow = timeSnap.data!;

        return StreamBuilder<DocumentSnapshot>(
          stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('appSubscription').doc('current').snapshots(),
          builder: (ctx, snap){
            if(snap.connectionState==ConnectionState.waiting) return Scaffold(body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
            if(snap.hasError || !snap.hasData || !snap.data!.exists){
              // No internet: Don't allow indefinite use. Allow only 3 days grace using server time cache
              return widget.child; // Or show offline warning
            }
            
            var sub = AppSubscription.fromMap(snap.data!.data() as Map<String,dynamic>);
            
            // === CORE LOGIC: EFFECTED FROM CONSOLE TIME ===
            // You set expiryMillis in console. We compare it with SERVER time, not device time.
            bool isExpired = !sub.isActive || serverNow.millisecondsSinceEpoch > sub.expiryMillis;
            
            // === ANTI BACK-DATE CHECK ===
            bool isDeviceBackDated = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch < (sub.startDate.millisecondsSinceEpoch - 86400000); // device before start date
            
            if(isDeviceBackDated){
              return Scaffold(
                backgroundColor: Colors.red[900],
                body: Center(child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(24), child: Column(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children:[
                  Icon(Icons.security, size:80, color: Colors.white),
                  Text("DEVICE TIME TAMPERING DETECTED", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  SizedBox(height:10),
                  Text("Please set Automatic Date & Time ON. Contact Admin.", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70)),
                  Text("Server Time: $serverNow", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.amber, fontSize:11)),
                  Text("Device Time: ${DateTime.now()}", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize:11)),
                ]))),
              );
            }

            if(isExpired){
              return SubscriptionExpiredPage(sub: sub, serverNow: serverNow);
            }
            return widget.child;
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

class Student {
  String name, cls, stream, idNo, parentPhone, gender;
  String studentType;
  String hostelBlock;
  Map<String,ZoneAccess> zones;
  bool selected=false;
  bool present=false;
  DateTime? lastMessScan;
  String lastMessMeal='';
  String status;
  String prevCls;
  String academicYear;
  String graduatedYear;
  String promotionHistory;
  bool isOldStudent;
  Student({required this.name, required this.cls, required this.stream, required this.idNo, this.parentPhone='256700000000', this.gender='Male', this.studentType='Day', this.hostelBlock='', required this.zones, this.lastMessScan, this.lastMessMeal='', this.status='active', this.prevCls='', this.academicYear='', this.graduatedYear='', this.promotionHistory='', this.isOldStudent=false});
  String get safeDocId => idNo.replaceAll('/', '_').replaceAll(' ', '_').replaceAll('#', '_');
  Map<String,dynamic> toMap() => {'name':name,'cls':cls,'stream':stream,'idNo':idNo,'parentPhone':parentPhone,'gender':gender,'studentType':studentType,'hostelBlock':hostelBlock,'zones':zones.map((k,v)=>MapEntry(k,v.toMap())), 'lastMessScan': lastMessScan?.toIso8601String(), 'lastMessMeal': lastMessMeal, 'status':status, 'prevCls':prevCls, 'academicYear':academicYear, 'graduatedYear':graduatedYear, 'promotionHistory':promotionHistory, 'isOldStudent':isOldStudent};
  static Student fromMap(Map m){
    var z={}; if(m['zones']!=null) (m['zones'] as Map).forEach((k,v){ z[k]=ZoneAccess.fromMap(v); });
    return Student(name:m['name'],cls:m['cls'],stream:m['stream'],idNo:m['idNo'],parentPhone:m['parentPhone']??'256700000000',gender:m['gender']??'Male',studentType:m['studentType']??'Day',hostelBlock:m['hostelBlock']??'',zones:z.cast<String,ZoneAccess>(), lastMessScan: m['lastMessScan']!=null? DateTime.tryParse(m['lastMessScan']): null, lastMessMeal: m['lastMessMeal']??'', status:m['status']??'active', prevCls:m['prevCls']??'', academicYear:m['academicYear']??'', graduatedYear:m['graduatedYear']??'', promotionHistory:m['promotionHistory']??'', isOldStudent:m['isOldStudent']??false);
  }
  bool get isActive =>!isOldStudent && (status=='active' || status=='');
}

// ===== LIBRARY MODEL - BORROW / RETURN =====
class LibraryLoan {
  String id; String studentId; String studentName; String cls; String stream;
  List<String> items; String borrowDate; String dueDate; DateTime borrowTimestamp; DateTime dueTimestamp;
  bool returned; String? returnDate; DateTime? returnTimestamp;
  String teacher; String teacherEmail; String notes;
  LibraryLoan({required this.id, required this.studentId, required this.studentName, required this.cls, required this.stream, required this.items, required this.borrowDate, required this.dueDate, required this.borrowTimestamp, required this.dueTimestamp, this.returned=false, this.returnDate, this.returnTimestamp, required this.teacher, required this.teacherEmail, this.notes=''});
  Map<String,dynamic> toMap(){
    return {
      'id': id.replaceAll('/', '_').replaceAll(' ', '_'),
      'studentId': studentId,
      'studentName': studentName,
      'cls': cls,
      'stream': stream,
      'items': items,
      'borrowDate': borrowDate,
      'dueDate': dueDate,
      'borrowTimestamp': borrowTimestamp.toIso8601String(),
      'borrowTimestampMillis': borrowTimestamp.millisecondsSinceEpoch,
      'dueTimestamp': dueTimestamp.toIso8601String(),
      'dueTimestampMillis': dueTimestamp.millisecondsSinceEpoch,
      'returned': returned,
      'returnDate': returnDate??'',
      'returnTimestamp': returnTimestamp?.toIso8601String()??'',
      'returnTimestampMillis': returnTimestamp?.millisecondsSinceEpoch,
      'teacher': teacher,
      'teacherEmail': teacherEmail,
      'notes': notes,
      'ts': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      'safeStudentId': studentId.replaceAll('/', '_').replaceAll(' ', '_'),
    };
  }
  static LibraryLoan fromMap(Map m){
    DateTime bTime;
    if(m['borrowTimestampMillis']!=null) bTime = DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(m['borrowTimestampMillis']);
    else bTime = DateTime.tryParse(m['borrowTimestamp']??'')??DateTime.now();
    DateTime dTime;
    if(m['dueTimestampMillis']!=null) dTime = DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(m['dueTimestampMillis']);
    else dTime = DateTime.tryParse(m['dueTimestamp']??'')??DateTime.now().add(Duration(days:7));
    DateTime? rTime;
    if(m['returnTimestampMillis']!=null) rTime = DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(m['returnTimestampMillis']);
    else if(m['returnTimestamp']!=null && (m['returnTimestamp'] as String).isNotEmpty) rTime = DateTime.tryParse(m['returnTimestamp']);
    return LibraryLoan(id: m['id']??'',studentId: m['studentId']??'',studentName: m['studentName']??'',cls: m['cls']??'',stream: m['stream']??'',items: List<String>.from(m['items']??[]),borrowDate: m['borrowDate']??'',dueDate: m['dueDate']??'',borrowTimestamp: bTime,dueTimestamp: dTime,returned: m['returned']??false,returnDate: m['returnDate'],returnTimestamp: rTime,teacher: m['teacher']??'',teacherEmail: m['teacherEmail']??'',notes: m['notes']??'');
  }
  bool get isOverdue =>!returned && DateTime.now().isAfter(dueTimestamp);
  int get overdueDays => isOverdue? DateTime.now().difference(dueTimestamp).inDays : 0;
}

class InventoryItem {
  String id;
  String name;
  String category; // Lab Equipment, Furniture, Sports, Books, etc
  String department; // Science, Arts, Sports, Library, Admin, Boarding
  int quantity;
  String condition; // New, Good, Fair, Damaged, Lost
  String location; // Lab 1, Store, etc
  double price;
  String purchaseDate;
  String recordedBy; // staffId
  String recordedByName;
  DateTime timestamp;
  String status; // Available, In Use, Maintenance
  String notes;

  InventoryItem({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    this.category = 'General',
    required this.department,
    this.quantity = 1,
    this.condition = 'Good',
    this.location = '',
    this.price = 0,
    this.purchaseDate = '',
    required this.recordedBy,
    required this.recordedByName,
    required this.timestamp,
    this.status = 'Available',
    this.notes = '',
  });

  Map<String,dynamic> toMap()=> {
    'id':id,'name':name,'category':category,'department':department,
    'quantity':quantity,'condition':condition,'location':location,'price':price,
    'purchaseDate':purchaseDate,'recordedBy':recordedBy,'recordedByName':recordedByName,
    'timestamp':timestamp.toIso8601String(),'status':status,'notes':notes
  };

  static InventoryItem fromMap(Map m)=> InventoryItem(
    id:m['id']??'',
    name:m['name']??'',
    category:m['category']??'General',
    department:m['department']??'General',
    quantity:(m['quantity']??1) is int? m['quantity'] : int.tryParse(m['quantity'].toString())??1,
    condition:m['condition']??'Good',
    location:m['location']??'',
    price:(m['price']??0).toDouble(),
    purchaseDate:m['purchaseDate']??'',
    recordedBy:m['recordedBy']??'',
    recordedByName:m['recordedByName']??'',
    timestamp: m['timestamp']!=null? DateTime.tryParse(m['timestamp'])??DateTime.now() : DateTime.now(),
    status:m['status']??'Available',
    notes:m['notes']??'',
  );
}

class TeacherProfile {
  String staffId;
  String name;
  String email;
  String phone;
  String gender;
  String nationalId;
  String role; // Subject Teacher, HOD, Head Teacher
  String department; // NEW: Science, Arts, Sports, Library, Admin, Boarding, etc
  List<String> subjects;
  List<String> classesAssigned;
  String pinCode;
  DateTime dateJoined;
  String photoUrl;

  TeacherProfile({
    required this.staffId,
    required this.name,
    required this.email,
    this.phone = '',
    this.gender = 'Male',
    this.nationalId = '',
    this.role = 'Subject Teacher',
    this.department = 'General',
    this.subjects = const [],
    this.classesAssigned = const [],
    this.pinCode = '0000',
    required this.dateJoined,
    this.photoUrl = '',
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() => {
    'staffId': staffId, 'name': name, 'email': email, 'phone': phone,
    'gender': gender, 'nationalId': nationalId, 'role': role, 'department': department,
    'subjects': subjects, 'classesAssigned': classesAssigned,
    'pinCode': pinCode, 'dateJoined': dateJoined.toIso8601String(), 'photoUrl': photoUrl,
  };

  static TeacherProfile fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> m) => TeacherProfile(
    staffId: m['staffId']?? '',
    name: m['name']?? '',
    email: m['email']?? '',
    phone: m['phone']?? '',
    gender: m['gender']?? 'Male',
    nationalId: m['nationalId']?? '',
    role: m['role']?? 'Subject Teacher',
    department: m['department']?? 'General',
    subjects: List<String>.from(m['subjects']?? []),
    classesAssigned: List<String>.from(m['classesAssigned']?? []),
    pinCode: m['pinCode']?? '0000',
    dateJoined: m['dateJoined']!= null? DateTime.parse(m['dateJoined']) : DateTime.now(),
    photoUrl: m['photoUrl']?? '',
  );

  bool get isHOD => role.toLowerCase().contains('hod') || role.toLowerCase().contains('head of department');
}

class TeacherAttendance {
  String id;
  String staffId;
  String name;
  String photoUrl;
  String date;
  String time;
  DateTime timestamp;
  String method;
  String verifiedBy;
  double lat;
  double lng;
  double distanceM;
  bool withinFence;
  bool isLate; // NEW
  int lateMinutes; // NEW
  String status; // PRESENT / LATE

  TeacherAttendance({
    required this.id,
    required this.staffId,
    required this.name,
    this.photoUrl = '',
    required this.date,
    required this.time,
    required this.timestamp,
    this.method = 'FINGERPRINT',
    this.verifiedBy = 'SELF',
    this.lat = 0,
    this.lng = 0,
    this.distanceM = 0,
    this.withinFence = true,
    this.isLate = false,
    this.lateMinutes = 0,
    this.status = 'PRESENT',
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() => {
    'id': id, 'staffId': staffId, 'name': name, 'photoUrl': photoUrl,
    'date': date, 'time': time, 'timestamp': timestamp.toIso8601String(),
    'method': method, 'verifiedBy': verifiedBy, 'lat': lat, 'lng': lng,
    'distanceM': distanceM, 'withinFence': withinFence,
    'isLate': isLate, 'lateMinutes': lateMinutes, 'status': status,
  };

  static TeacherAttendance fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> m) => TeacherAttendance(
    id: m['id']?? '',
    staffId: m['staffId']?? '',
    name: m['name']?? '',
    photoUrl: m['photoUrl']?? '',
    date: m['date']?? '',
    time: m['time']?? '',
    timestamp: m['timestamp']!= null? DateTime.parse(m['timestamp']) : DateTime.now(),
    method: m['method']?? 'FINGERPRINT',
    verifiedBy: m['verifiedBy']?? 'SELF',
    lat: (m['lat']?? 0).toDouble(),
    lng: (m['lng']?? 0).toDouble(),
    distanceM: (m['distanceM']?? 0).toDouble(),
    withinFence: m['withinFence']?? true,
    isLate: m['isLate']?? false,
    lateMinutes: (m['lateMinutes']?? 0),
    status: m['status']?? 'PRESENT',
  );
}

class LoginPage extends StatefulWidget { @override _LoginPageState createState()=>_LoginPageState(); }
class _LoginPageState extends State<LoginPage> {
  final eC=TextEditingController(text:'ht@busalamu.com');
  final pC=TextEditingController(text:'ht123');
  String err='';
  bool loading=true;
  List<AppUser> users=[];
  final db=FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  @override void initState(){ super.initState(); _loadUsers(); }

  Future<void> _loadUsers() async {
    try{
      var snap=await db.collection('app_users').get();
      if(snap.docs.isEmpty){
        List<AppUser> def=[
          AppUser(email:'ht@busalamu.com',pass:'ht123',role:'HT',name:'Headteacher'),
          AppUser(email:'admin@busalamu.com',pass:'admin123',role:'ADMIN',name:'System Admin'),
          AppUser(email:'teacher@busalamu.com',pass:'teacher123',role:'TEACHER',name:'Gate Teacher'),
          AppUser(email:'student@busalamu.com',pass:'student123',role:'STUDENT',name:'Student', studentId:'BSS/2026/022'),
        ];
        for(var u in def){ await db.collection('app_users').doc(u.role).set(u.toMap()); }
        users=def;
      } else {
        users=snap.docs.map((d)=>AppUser.fromMap(d.data())).toList();
      }
    }catch(_){
      users=[
        AppUser(email:'ht@busalamu.com',pass:'ht123',role:'HT',name:'Headteacher'),
        AppUser(email:'admin@busalamu.com',pass:'admin123',role:'ADMIN',name:'System Admin'),
        AppUser(email:'teacher@busalamu.com',pass:'teacher123',role:'TEACHER',name:'Gate Teacher'),
        AppUser(email:'student@busalamu.com',pass:'student123',role:'STUDENT',name:'Student', studentId:'BSS/2026/022'),
      ];
    }
    setState(()=>loading=false);
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    if(loading) return Scaffold(body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
    return Scaffold(backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), body: Center(child: SingleChildScrollView(child: Card(margin: EdgeInsets.all(20), child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(25), child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
      // SCHOOL BADGE - FIXED - KEEPING YOUR OLD FORMAT
      Container(
        height: 110, width: 110,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.white, shape: BoxShape.circle, border: Border.all(color: Color(0xFF0D47A1), width: 3), boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black26, blurRadius: 6)]),
        child: ClipOval(child: Image.asset('assets/badge.png', height: 100, width: 100, fit: BoxFit.cover,
          errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace){
            return Column(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children: [
              Icon(Icons.school,size:50,color: Color(0xFF0D47A1)),
              Text('BSS', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1)))
            ]);
          }
        )),
      ),
      SizedBox(height: 12),
      Text('BSS GoB The Great',style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,fontSize:20,color: Color(0xFF0D47A1))),
      Text('Busalamu Secondary School',style: TextStyle(fontSize:12,color: Colors.black54)),
      SizedBox(height:6),
      Container(padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:10,vertical:3), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.green[50], borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)), child: Text('eshrach@gmail.com - 256773845266',style: TextStyle(fontSize:10,color: Colors.green,fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
      SizedBox(height:15),
      TextField(controller: eC,decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Email',prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.email),border: OutlineInputBorder())),
      SizedBox(height:10),
      TextField(controller: pC,obscureText:true,decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Password',prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.lock),border: OutlineInputBorder())),
      if(err.isNotEmpty) Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.only(top:8), child: Text(err,style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red))),
      SizedBox(height:15),
      SizedBox(width: double.infinity, height: 45, child: ElevatedButton(style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1)), onPressed: (){
        try{
          var u=users.firstWhere((x)=>x.email==eC.text.trim()&&x.pass==pC.text.trim());
          Navigator.pushReplacement(c, MaterialPageRoute(builder:(_)=>MainScreen(cur:u)));
        }catch(_){ setState(()=>err='Wrong login - Ask HT for new password'); }
      }, child: Text('LOGIN', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)))),
      SizedBox(height:10),
      Text('HT can change all passwords in Login Setup', style: TextStyle(fontSize:9, color: Colors.green, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))
    ]))))));
  }
}

// ===== FIXED HT LOGIN SETUP PAGE =====
class LoginSetupPage extends StatefulWidget { @override _LoginSetupPageState createState()=>_LoginSetupPageState(); }
class _LoginSetupPageState extends State<LoginSetupPage> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late TabController _tabController;
  final db = FirebaseFirestore.instance;
  final emailC = TextEditingController();
  final passC = TextEditingController();
  final nameC = TextEditingController();
  String role = 'TEACHER';
  DateTime startDate = DateTime.now();
  DateTime endDate = DateTime.now().add(Duration(days:90));
  String termName = 'Term 1 2026';
  bool termLoading = true;

  @override void initState(){ super.initState(); _tabController = TabController(length: 2, vsync: this); loadTerm(); }

  Future<void> loadTerm() async {
    var doc = await db.collection('settings').doc('currentTerm').get();
    if(doc.exists){
      var t=TermSettings.fromMap(doc.data()!);
      setState(()=> {termName=t.termName, startDate=t.startDate, endDate=t.endDate, termLoading=false});
    } else setState(()=>termLoading=false);
  }

  Future<void> pickStart() async { var d=await showDatePicker(context:context, initialDate:startDate, firstDate:DateTime(2024), lastDate:DateTime(2030)); if(d!=null) setState(()=>startDate=d); }
  Future<void> pickEnd() async { var d=await showDatePicker(context:context, initialDate:endDate, firstDate:DateTime(2024), lastDate:DateTime(2030)); if(d!=null) setState(()=>endDate=d); }
  Future<void> saveTerm() async {
    await db.collection('settings').doc('currentTerm').set(TermSettings(termName:termName, startDate:startDate, endDate:endDate).toMap());
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content:Text('✅ Term Saved: $termName'), backgroundColor:Colors.green));
  }

  Future<void> createUser() async {
    if(emailC.text.isEmpty || passC.text.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content:Text('Enter email & password'), backgroundColor:Colors.red));
      return;
    }
    String email = emailC.text.trim().toLowerCase();
    // FIX: Save to app_users with email as doc ID, not role
    await db.collection('app_users').doc(email).set({
      'email': email,
      'pass': passC.text.trim(),
      'role': role,
      'name': nameC.text.isEmpty? email.split('@')[0] : nameC.text.trim(),
      'studentId': '',
      'createdAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
    });
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content:Text('✅ User $email created as $role'), backgroundColor:Colors.green));
    emailC.clear(); passC.clear(); nameC.clear();
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext context){
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Setup - Logins & Term Settings'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), bottom: TabBar(controller:_tabController, tabs:[Tab(text:'Login Setup', icon: Icon(Icons.key)), Tab(text:'Term Settings', icon: Icon(Icons.date_range))])),
      body: TabBarView(controller:_tabController, children:[
        // TAB 1 - FIXED LOGIN SETUP
        Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(16), child: Column(children:[
          TextField(controller: nameC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Full Name', border:OutlineInputBorder())),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          TextField(controller: emailC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Email', border:OutlineInputBorder())),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          TextField(controller: passC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Password', border:OutlineInputBorder()), obscureText:true),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          DropdownButtonFormField<String>(value: role, items: ['HT','DOS','DEPUTY HEAD TEACHER','DIRECTOR OF STUDIES','DEPUTY','TEACHER','GATE','STUDENT','SUPPORT STAFF'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e, child:Text(e))).toList(), onChanged:(v)=>setState(()=>role=v!), decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Role', border:OutlineInputBorder())),
          SizedBox(height:16),
          ElevatedButton(onPressed:createUser, style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), minimumSize: Size(double.infinity, 50)), child:Text('CREATE USER', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white))),
          SizedBox(height:16),
          Expanded(child: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(stream: db.collection('app_users').snapshots(), builder:(c,s){
            if(!s.hasData) return Center(child:CircularProgressIndicator());
            return ListView.builder(itemCount: s.data!.docs.length, itemBuilder:(c,i){
              var u=s.data!.docs[i].data() as Map;
              return Card(child: ListTile(
                title:Text(u['email']??''),
                subtitle:Text('${u['role']} - ${u['name']??''}'),
                trailing: IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.delete, color: Colors.red), onPressed: () async {
                  bool ok = await confirmDelete(context, "${u['email']} - ${u['role']}", extraWarning: 'User login will be removed');
                  if(ok) await db.collection('app_users').doc(s.data!.docs[i].id).delete();
                }),
              ));
            });
          })),
        ])),
        // TAB 2 - TERM SETTINGS (same as before)
        termLoading? Center(child:CircularProgressIndicator()) : Padding(padding:EdgeInsets.all(16), child: Column(children:[
          TextField(decoration:InputDecoration(labelText:'Term Name', border: OutlineInputBorder()), controller:TextEditingController(text:termName)..selection=TextSelection.fromPosition(TextPosition(offset: termName.length)), onChanged:(v)=>termName=v),
          SizedBox(height:16),
          Card(child: ListTile(title:Text('Start Date: ${startDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}'), subtitle:Text('First scan after this = Reported Back'), trailing: Icon(Icons.calendar_today), onTap: pickStart)),
          Card(child: ListTile(title:Text('End Date: ${endDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}'), subtitle:Text('Term ends'), trailing: Icon(Icons.calendar_today), onTap: pickEnd)),
          SizedBox(height:20),
          ElevatedButton(onPressed: saveTerm, style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.green, minimumSize: Size(double.infinity, 50)), child: Text('SAVE TERM', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white))),
        ])),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

class MainScreen extends StatefulWidget { final AppUser cur; MainScreen({required this.cur}); @override _MainScreenState createState()=>_MainScreenState(); }
class _MainScreenState extends State<MainScreen> {
  int idx=0;
  List<Student> studs=[];
  List<ClassTeacher> teachers=[];
  List<Timetable> timetable=[];
  List<AttendanceLog> logs=[];
  List<LessonLog> lessonLogs=[];
  List<PermissionAlert> perms=[];
  List<LibraryLoan> libraryLoans=[];
  bool loading=true;
  final db=FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  @override void initState(){
    super.initState();
    _seedIfEmpty();
    db.collection('students').snapshots().listen((snap){ setState((){ studs=snap.docs.map((d)=>Student.fromMap(d.data())).toList(); loading=false; }); checkAndResetExpiredTerm(); });
    db.collection('attendance_logs').orderBy('ts',descending:true).limit(500).snapshots().listen((snap){ setState(()=>logs=snap.docs.map((d)=>AttendanceLog.fromMap(d.data())).toList()); });
    db.collection('lesson_logs').orderBy('ts',descending:true).limit(1000).snapshots().listen((snap){ setState(()=>lessonLogs=snap.docs.map((d)=>LessonLog.fromMap(d.data())).toList()); });
    db.collection('teachers').snapshots().listen((snap){ setState(()=>teachers=snap.docs.map((d)=>ClassTeacher.fromMap(d.data())).toList()); });
    db.collection('timetable').snapshots().listen((snap){ setState(()=>timetable=snap.docs.map((d)=>Timetable.fromMap(d.data())).toList()); });
    db.collection('permissions').where('status', isEqualTo: 'PENDING').snapshots().listen((snap){ setState(()=>perms=snap.docs.map((d)=>PermissionAlert.fromMap(d.data())).toList()); });
    db.collection('library_loans').snapshots().listen((snap){ setState(()=>libraryLoans=snap.docs.map((d)=>LibraryLoan.fromMap(d.data())).toList()); });
  }

  Future<void> _seedIfEmpty() async {
    var s=await db.collection('students').limit(1).get();
    if(s.docs.isEmpty){
      List<Student> init=[
        Student(name:'Quin Ton Tavius',cls:'S.4',stream:'Blue',idNo:'BSS/2026/2222',zones:{'GATE':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:30),'CLASS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:90),'MESS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:60)}),
        Student(name:'Nabirye Sarah',cls:'S.4',stream:'Green',idNo:'BSS/2026/2223',zones:{'GATE':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:-2),'CLASS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:90),'MESS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:-2)}),
        Student(name:'Okello John',cls:'S.4',stream:'White',idNo:'BSS/2026/2224',zones:{'GATE':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:25),'CLASS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:10),'MESS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:30)}),
      ];
      for(var st in init){ await db.collection('students').doc(st.safeDocId).set(st.toMap()); }
    }
  }

  Future<void> syncPermission(PermissionAlert p) async => await db.collection('permissions').doc(p.id).set(p.toMap());

  Future<void> createPermission(Student s, String reason) async {
    String timeOut = DateTime.now().toString().substring(0,16);
    var perm = PermissionAlert(id: DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch.toString(), studentId: s.idNo, studentName: s.name, parentPhone: s.parentPhone, reason: reason, timeOut: timeOut, status: 'PENDING');
    await syncPermission(perm);
    String txt = 'BUSALAMU SS PERMISSION ALERT: ${s.name} (${s.cls} ${s.stream}) has been granted permission to leave at $timeOut. Reason: $reason. We shall notify you when back.';
    String url = 'https://wa.me/${s.parentPhone}?text=${Uri.encodeComponent(txt)}';
    try{ if(await canLaunchUrl(Uri.parse(url))) await launchUrl(Uri.parse(url), mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication); }catch(_){}
  }

  Future<void> markReturned(PermissionAlert p) async {
    String timeIn = DateTime.now().toString().substring(0,16);
    p.timeIn = timeIn; p.status = 'RETURNED';
    await db.collection('permissions_returned').doc(p.id).set(p.toMap());
    await db.collection('permissions').doc(p.id).delete();
    String txt = 'UPDATE: ${p.studentName} has reported BACK to Busalamu SS at $timeIn. Permission cleared.';
    String url = 'https://wa.me/${p.parentPhone}?text=${Uri.encodeComponent(txt)}';
    try{ if(await canLaunchUrl(Uri.parse(url))) await launchUrl(Uri.parse(url), mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication); }catch(_){}
  }

  Future<void> syncStudent(Student s) async => await db.collection('students').doc(s.safeDocId).set(s.toMap());
  Future<void> syncLog(AttendanceLog l) async => await db.collection('attendance_logs').add(l.toMap());
  Future<void> syncLessonLog(LessonLog l) async => await db.collection('lesson_logs').doc(l.id).set(l.toMap());
  Future<void> syncLibraryLoan(LibraryLoan l) async => await db.collection('library_loans').doc(l.id.replaceAll('/', '_').replaceAll(' ', '_')).set(l.toMap());
  Future<void> syncTeacher(ClassTeacher t) async => await db.collection('teachers').doc('${t.cls}_${t.stream}_${t.name}'.replaceAll('/', '_')).set(t.toMap());
  Future<void> syncTimetable(Timetable t) async => await db.collection('timetable').doc(t.id).set(t.toMap());
  Future<DateTime?> getCurrentTermEnd() async {
    try{
      var doc = await db.collection('settings').doc('currentTerm').get();
      if(doc.exists){
        var t = TermSettings.fromMap(doc.data()!);
        return t.endDate;
      }
    }catch(_){}
    return null;
  }

  void grant(int i,int days) async {
    var s=studs[i];
    int effDays = days;
    DateTime? termEnd = await getCurrentTermEnd();
    if(termEnd!=null){
      int rem = termEnd.difference(DateTime.now()).inDays+1;
      if(rem<=0){
        effDays=0;
      } else if(effDays > rem){
        effDays = rem;
      }
    }
    s.zones={'GATE':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:effDays),'CLASS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:effDays),'MESS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:effDays)};
    syncStudent(s);
  }

  void grantMulti(List<int> ids,int days) async {
    DateTime? termEnd = await getCurrentTermEnd();
    int effDays = days;
    if(termEnd!=null){
      int rem = termEnd.difference(DateTime.now()).inDays+1;
      if(rem<=0) effDays=0;
      else if(effDays>rem) effDays=rem;
    }
    for(var i in ids){
      var s=studs[i];
      s.zones={'GATE':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:effDays),'CLASS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:effDays),'MESS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:effDays)};
      syncStudent(s);
    }
  }

  Future<void> checkAndResetExpiredTerm() async {
    DateTime? termEnd = await getCurrentTermEnd();
    if(termEnd==null) return;
    if(DateTime.now().isAfter(termEnd)){
      for(var s in studs){
        if((s.zones['GATE']?.left??0)>0 || (s.zones['CLASS']?.left??0)>0 || (s.zones['MESS']?.left??0)>0){
          s.zones={'GATE':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:0),'CLASS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:0),'MESS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:0)};
          await db.collection('students').doc(s.safeDocId).update({'zones': s.zones.map((k,v)=>MapEntry(k,v.toMap()))});
        }
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> whatsapp(Student s,String zone) async {
    var t=teachers.firstWhere((e)=>e.cls==s.cls&&e.stream==s.stream, orElse: ()=>ClassTeacher(cls:s.cls,stream:s.stream,name:'HT',whatsapp:'256700000000',subject:''));
    String rawPhone = t.whatsapp.toString().trim().replaceAll(' ','').replaceAll('+','').replaceAll('-','');
    if(rawPhone.startsWith('0')) rawPhone='256'+rawPhone.substring(1);
    String msg='BUSALAMU ALERT: ${s.name} DENIED $zone at ${DateTime.now()}';
    String enc=Uri.encodeComponent(msg);
    List<String> urls=[
      'whatsapp://send?phone=$rawPhone&text=$enc',
      'https://wa.me/$rawPhone?text=$enc',
      'https://api.whatsapp.com/send?phone=$rawPhone&text=$enc'
    ];
    for(var u in urls){
      try{ var uri=Uri.parse(u); if(await canLaunchUrl(uri)){ await launchUrl(uri, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication); break; } }catch(e){ continue; }
    }
  }

  Widget _buildDrawer(BuildContext ctx, List<_ModuleItem> items, bool showWarning, AppSubscription sub){
  return Drawer(
    child: Column(
      children: [
        UserAccountsDrawerHeader(
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Color(0xFF0D47A1)),
          accountName: Text(widget.cur.name, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          accountEmail: Text('${widget.cur.role} - ${studs.length} Students'),
          currentAccountPicture: CircleAvatar(backgroundColor: Colors.white, child: Icon(Icons.person, size: 40, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1))),
        ),
        if(showWarning) Container(
          color: Colors.amber,
          padding: EdgeInsets.all(8),
          child: Row(children: [
            Icon(Icons.warning, size:16),
            SizedBox(width:6),
            Expanded(child: Text('Expires in ${sub.daysLeft} days - ${sub.expiryDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
          ]),
        ),
        Expanded(
          child: ListView.builder(
            padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
            itemCount: items.length,
            itemBuilder: (_, i){
              final m = items[i];
              return ListTile(
                leading: CircleAvatar(radius:18, backgroundColor: m.color.withOpacity(0.15), child: Icon(m.icon, color: m.color, size:18)),
                title: Text(m.title, style: TextStyle(fontSize:13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
                trailing: Icon(Icons.chevron_right, size:16, color: Colors.grey),
                onTap: (){
                  Navigator.pop(ctx);
                  Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: 200), (){
                    Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_)=>m.page));
                  });
                },
              );
            },
          ),
        ),
        Divider(),
        ListTile(leading: Icon(Icons.logout, color: Colors.red), title: Text('Logout'), onTap: (){ Navigator.pushReplacement(ctx, MaterialPageRoute(builder:(_)=>LoginPage())); }),
        SizedBox(height:12),
      ],
    ),
  );
}

  Widget _card(BuildContext ctx, String title, IconData icon, Color color, Widget page){
  return Card(elevation: 4, shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
    child: InkWell(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
      onTap: ()=>Navigator.push(ctx, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_)=>page)),
        child: Column(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children: [
        CircleAvatar(radius: 28, backgroundColor: color.withOpacity(0.15), child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 28)),
        SizedBox(height: 8),
        Text(title, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12), textAlign: TextAlign.center),
        ]),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    if(loading) return Scaffold(body: Center(child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [CircularProgressIndicator(), Text('A Godfrey Osodo Bwire Innitiative')])));

    bool isHT = widget.cur.role == 'HT' || widget.cur.role.toUpperCase() == 'ADMIN' || widget.cur.role.toUpperCase().contains('ADMIN');
    bool isAdmin = widget.cur.role.toUpperCase() == 'ADMIN' || widget.cur.role.toUpperCase().contains('ADMIN');
    bool isStudent = widget.cur.role == 'STUDENT';
    bool isSupport = widget.cur.role.toUpperCase().contains('SUPPORT') && !isAdmin;
    List<String> seniorRoles = ['DOS','DEPUTY','DEPUTY HEAD TEACHER','DIRECTOR OF STUDIES','D.O.S','DEPUTY HEADTEACHER'];
    bool isSenior = seniorRoles.any((r) => widget.cur.role.toUpperCase().contains(r)) && !isAdmin;
    bool isHTorSenior = isHT || isSenior || isAdmin;

    if(isStudent){
      var my=studs.firstWhere((s)=>s.idNo==widget.cur.studentId, orElse: ()=>studs.isNotEmpty?studs[0]:Student(name:'Test',cls:'S.4',stream:'Blue',idNo:'BSS/2026/022',zones:{'GATE':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:30),'CLASS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:90),'MESS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:60)}));
      return Scaffold(appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Student: ${widget.cur.name}')), body: StudentPortal(student:my,logs:logs,allStudents:studs));
    }

    // BUILD PAGES MAP - ALL FEATURES KEPT
    Widget gatePage = GateReal(studs:studs,onDeny:whatsapp, perms:perms, onPermissionReturned: (p)=>markReturned(p));
    Widget attendPage = AttendReal(studs:studs,timetable:timetable,logs:logs,onSyncLog:syncLog,onSyncStudent:syncStudent);
    Widget htDashPage = HTDash(studs:studs);
    Widget idPage = IDGrid(studs:studs);
    Widget studentsPage = StudentsPage(studs:studs, onSync:syncStudent, db:db);
    Widget teachersPage = TeachersPage(teachers:teachers, onSync: syncTeacher, db:db, lessonLogs: lessonLogs, timetable: timetable);
    Widget enrollPage = TeacherEnrollmentPage();
    Widget tAttendPage = TeacherFingerprintAttendancePage(curUser:widget.cur);
    Widget tSettingsPage = AttendanceSettingsPage();
    Widget ttPage = TimeTablePage(timetable:timetable,onSync:syncTimetable,db:db, curUser:widget.cur);
    Widget lessonsPage = HTLessonReportPage(lessonLogs:lessonLogs, timetable:timetable, teachers:teachers);
    Widget libraryPage = LibraryPage(studs:studs, curUser:widget.cur, onSync:syncLibraryLoan);
    Widget permPage = PermissionPage(perms:perms, studs:studs, onGrant:(s,r)=>createPermission(s,r));
    Widget blockedPage = BlockedPage(studs:studs);
    Widget broadcastPage = BroadcastPage(studs:studs);
    Widget reportsPage = ReportsPage(studs:studs,logs:logs);
    Widget loginSetupPage = LoginSetupPage();
    Widget setupPage = Setup(studs:studs,onGrantSingle:grant,onGrantMulti:grantMulti);
    Widget inventoryPage = InventoryPage(curUser:widget.cur);

    List<_ModuleItem> mods=[
      _ModuleItem('Gate CAM', Icons.qr_code_scanner, Colors.indigo, gatePage),
      _ModuleItem('My Attend + GPS', Icons.fingerprint, Colors.deepPurple, tAttendPage),
      _ModuleItem('Attend CAM', Icons.how_to_reg, Colors.green, attendPage),
      _ModuleItem('Lesson Update', Icons.menu_book, Colors.cyan, TeacherLessonPage(studs:studs, timetable:timetable, teachers:teachers, curUser:widget.cur, onSyncLesson:syncLessonLog)),
      _ModuleItem('Library', Icons.local_library, Colors.brown, LibraryPage(studs:studs, curUser:widget.cur, onSync:syncLibraryLoan)),
      _ModuleItem('IDs', Icons.badge, Colors.black87, idPage),
      _ModuleItem('Timetable', Icons.schedule, Colors.purple, TimeTablePage(timetable:timetable,readOnly:true,onSync:syncTimetable,db:db, curUser:widget.cur)),
      _ModuleItem('Inventories', Icons.inventory_2, Colors.teal, inventoryPage),
      _ModuleItem('Notice Board', Icons.push_pin, Colors.orange, NoticeBoardPage(isHT:false, currentUserName:widget.cur.name)),
    ];

    List<_ModuleItem> supportMods=[
      _ModuleItem('Gate CAM', Icons.qr_code_scanner, Colors.indigo, gatePage),
      _ModuleItem('My Attend + GPS', Icons.fingerprint, Colors.deepPurple, tAttendPage),
      _ModuleItem('Notice Board', Icons.push_pin, Colors.orange, NoticeBoardPage(isHT:false, currentUserName:widget.cur.name)),
      _ModuleItem('Inventories', Icons.inventory_2, Colors.teal, inventoryPage),
    ];

    List<_ModuleItem> htMods=[
      _ModuleItem('HT Overview', Icons.dashboard, Colors.blue, htDashPage),
      _ModuleItem('Login Setup', Icons.key, Colors.green, loginSetupPage),
      _ModuleItem('Gate CAM', Icons.qr_code_scanner, Colors.indigo, gatePage),
      _ModuleItem('Attend CAM', Icons.how_to_reg, Colors.green, attendPage),
      _ModuleItem('IDs', Icons.badge, Colors.black87, idPage),
      _ModuleItem('Students', Icons.people_alt, Colors.teal, studentsPage),
      _ModuleItem('Class Teachers', Icons.people, Colors.brown, teachersPage),
      _ModuleItem('Enroll Teacher', Icons.person_add, Colors.orange, enrollPage),
      _ModuleItem('T-Attend + GPS', Icons.fingerprint, Colors.deepPurple, tAttendPage),
      _ModuleItem('Attend Settings', Icons.settings_applications, Colors.red, tSettingsPage),
      _ModuleItem('Class Access Time', Icons.access_time, Colors.orange, ClassAccessSettingsPage()),
      _ModuleItem('Promote Students', Icons.school, Colors.green, StudentPromotionPage()),
      _ModuleItem('Timetable', Icons.schedule, Colors.purple, ttPage),
      _ModuleItem('Lesson Reports', Icons.menu_book, Colors.cyan, lessonsPage),
      _ModuleItem('Library', Icons.local_library, Colors.brown, libraryPage),
      _ModuleItem('Permissions', Icons.warning, Colors.amber, permPage),
      _ModuleItem('Blocked 0d', Icons.block, Colors.redAccent, blockedPage),
      _ModuleItem('Broadcast', Icons.campaign, Colors.deepOrange, broadcastPage),
      _ModuleItem('Inventories', Icons.inventory_2, Colors.teal, inventoryPage),
      _ModuleItem('Attendance Reports', Icons.analytics, Colors.blueGrey, reportsPage),
      _ModuleItem('Access Control', Icons.settings, Colors.grey, setupPage),
    ];

    List<String> blockedForSenior=['Login Setup','Attend Settings','Setup','Enroll Teacher'];
    List<_ModuleItem> seniorMods = htMods.where((m)=>!blockedForSenior.contains(m.title)).toList();

    // === HT + SENIOR (DOS, Deputy) - MONITORING ONLY ===
    if(isHTorSenior){
      List<_ModuleItem> activeMods = isHT? htMods : seniorMods;
      String titlePrefix = isAdmin? 'ADMIN' : isHT? 'HT' : widget.cur.role;
      return StreamBuilder<DocumentSnapshot>(
        stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('appSubscription').doc('current').snapshots(),
        builder: (ctx, snap){
          AppSubscription sub;
          if(!snap.hasData ||!snap.data!.exists){
            sub = AppSubscription.defaultTrial;
            FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('appSubscription').doc('current').set(sub.toMap());
          } else {
            sub = AppSubscription.fromMap(snap.data!.data() as Map<String,dynamic>);
          }
          if(sub.isExpired){
            return SubscriptionExpiredPage(sub: sub);
          }
          bool showWarning = sub.isExpiringSoon;
          return Scaffold(
            drawer: _buildDrawer(ctx, activeMods, showWarning, sub),
            appBar: AppBar(
              title: Text('$titlePrefix: ${widget.cur.name} ${!isHT && !isAdmin? "(View Only)" : ""}'),
              backgroundColor: isHT? Color(0xFF0D47A1) : Color(0xFF1565C0),
              actions: [
                if(showWarning) Icon(Icons.warning_amber, color: Colors.amber),
                if(!isHT) Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.only(right:12), child: Chip(label: Text('NO SETTINGS', style: TextStyle(fontSize:9, color: Colors.white)), backgroundColor: Colors.orange)),
              ],
            ),
            body: GridView.builder(
              padding: EdgeInsets.all(12),
              gridDelegate: SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(crossAxisCount:2, crossAxisSpacing:12, mainAxisSpacing:12),
              itemCount: activeMods.length,
              itemBuilder: (_, i){
                final m = activeMods[i];
                return InkWell(
                  onTap: ()=>Navigator.push(ctx, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_)=>m.page)),
                  child: Card(elevation:4, child: Column(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children: [CircleAvatar(radius:28, backgroundColor: m.color.withOpacity(0.15), child: Icon(m.icon, color: m.color, size:28)), SizedBox(height:8), Text(m.title, textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:11))]))
                );
              },
            ),
          );
        },
      );
    }

    // === TEACHER & SUPPORT ===
    if(true){
      List<_ModuleItem> activeMods = isSupport? supportMods : mods;
      String titlePrefix = isSupport? 'Support' : 'Teacher';
      return Scaffold(
        drawer: _buildDrawer(c, activeMods, false, AppSubscription.defaultTrial),
        appBar: AppBar(title: Text('$titlePrefix: ${widget.cur.name}'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1)),
        body: GridView.builder(
          padding: EdgeInsets.all(12),
          gridDelegate: SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(crossAxisCount:2, crossAxisSpacing:12, mainAxisSpacing:12),
          itemCount: activeMods.length,
          itemBuilder: (_, i){
            final m = activeMods[i];
            return InkWell(
              onTap: ()=>Navigator.push(c, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_)=>m.page)),
              child: Card(elevation:4, child: Column(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children: [CircleAvatar(radius:28, backgroundColor: m.color.withOpacity(0.15), child: Icon(m.icon, color: m.color, size:28)), SizedBox(height:8), Text(m.title, textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:11))]))
            );
          },
        ),
      );
    }

    // === SUBSCRIPTION GATE - OWNER 256773845266 ===
    return StreamBuilder<DocumentSnapshot>(
      stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('appSubscription').doc('current').snapshots(),
      builder: (ctx, snap){
        AppSubscription sub;
      if(!snap.hasData ||!snap.data!.exists){ sub = AppSubscription.defaultTrial; }
      else { sub = AppSubscription.fromMap(snap.data!.data() as Map<String,dynamic>); }
      if(sub.isExpired){ return SubscriptionExpiredPage(sub: sub); }
      bool showWarning = sub.isExpiringSoon;
      List<_ModuleItem> activeMods = isSupport? supportMods : mods;
      String titlePrefix = isSupport? 'Support' : 'Teacher';
      return Scaffold(
      drawer: _buildDrawer(c, activeMods, showWarning, sub),
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('$titlePrefix: ${widget.cur.name}'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1),
        actions: [if(showWarning) Icon(Icons.warning_amber, color: Colors.amber), IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.logout), onPressed: (){ Navigator.pushReplacement(c, MaterialPageRoute(builder:(_)=>LoginPage())); })],
        bottom: showWarning? PreferredSize(preferredSize: Size.fromHeight(30), child: Container(color: Colors.amber, padding: EdgeInsets.all(6), child: Center(child: Text('⚠️ Subscription expires in ${sub.daysLeft} days (${sub.expiryDate.toString().split(' ')[0]})', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))))): null,
      ),
      body: GridView.builder(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), gridDelegate: SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(crossAxisCount:2, crossAxisSpacing:12, mainAxisSpacing:12), itemCount: activeMods.length, itemBuilder: (_, i){ final m = activeMods[i]; return InkWell(onTap: ()=>Navigator.push(c, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_)=>m.page)), child: Card(elevation:4, child: Column(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children: [CircleAvatar(radius:28, backgroundColor: m.color.withOpacity(0.15), child: Icon(m.icon, color: m.color, size:28)), SizedBox(height:8), Text(m.title, textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:11))]))); }),);},
    );
  }
}

class HTDash extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<Student> studs; HTDash({required this.studs});
  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    var alert=studs.where((s)=>s.zones.values.any((z)=>z.left<=0&&z.left>=-2)).toList();
    Map<String,int> byClass={}; Map<String,int> byStream={}; Map<String,int> byGender={};
    for(var s in studs){ byClass[s.cls]=(byClass[s.cls]??0)+1; byStream[s.stream]=(byStream[s.stream]??0)+1; var g=s.gender.isEmpty?'Male':s.gender; byGender[g]=(byGender[g]??0)+1; }
    int male=byGender['Male']??0; int female=byGender['Female']??0;
    int hostelTotal=studs.where((s)=> s.studentType.toLowerCase()=='hostel').length;
    int dayTotal=studs.where((s)=> s.studentType.toLowerCase()=='day' || s.studentType.isEmpty).length;
    return Container(
      color: Color(0xFFF5F7FB),
      child: ListView(padding: EdgeInsets.all(15), children: [
      // ===== FIXED REPORTED BACK CARD - DAY & HOSTEL =====
      StreamBuilder<DocumentSnapshot>(
        stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('settings').doc('currentTerm').snapshots(),
        builder: (context, termSnap){
          if(!termSnap.hasData ||!termSnap.data!.exists){
            return Card(
              color: Colors.orange.shade50,
              child: ListTile(
                leading: Icon(Icons.warning, color: Colors.orange),
                title: Text('No Term Set'),
                subtitle: Text('Go to Login Setup > Term Settings to set Start Date'),
              ),
            );
          }
          var termData = termSnap.data!.data() as Map<String,dynamic>;
          var term = TermSettings.fromMap(termData);
          return StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
            stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('reportedBack').where('term', isEqualTo: term.termName).snapshots(),
            builder: (context, repSnap){
              var reportedDocs = repSnap.data?.docs?? [];
              var total = studs.length;
              var reportedCount = reportedDocs.length;
              var notReported = total - reportedCount;
              if(notReported<0) notReported=0;

              int hostelReported=0;
              int dayReported=0;
              for(var doc in reportedDocs){
                var data = doc.data() as Map<String,dynamic>;
                String type = (data['studentType']??'').toString().toLowerCase();
                if(type.isEmpty){
                  String sid = data['studentId']??'';
                  var found = studs.where((s)=>s.idNo==sid).toList();
                  if(found.isNotEmpty) type = found.first.studentType.toLowerCase();
                }
                if(type=='hostel') hostelReported++;
                else dayReported++;
              }

              var reportedIds = reportedDocs.map((d)=> (d.data() as Map)['studentId'] as String).toSet();
              int hostelNot = studs.where((s)=> s.studentType.toLowerCase()=='hostel' &&!reportedIds.contains(s.idNo)).length;
              int dayNot = studs.where((s)=> (s.studentType.toLowerCase()=='day' || s.studentType.isEmpty) &&!reportedIds.contains(s.idNo)).length;

              double hostelPct = hostelTotal>0? (hostelReported/hostelTotal*100) : 0;
              double dayPct = dayTotal>0? (dayReported/dayTotal*100) : 0;
              double totalPct = total>0? (reportedCount/total*100) : 0;

              return Card(
                color: Colors.white,
                elevation: 3,
