                child: Padding(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.all(12),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children:[
                        Text('📅 ${term.termName}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:16, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1))),
                        Container(padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:8,vertical:4), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.blue[50], borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)), child: Text('${totalPct.toStringAsFixed(0)}% Reported', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1)))),
                      ]),
                      Text('${term.startDate.toString().split(' ')[0]} to ${term.endDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey[700], fontSize:11)),
                      SizedBox(height:12),
                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
                        children: [
                          Column(children:[Text('$total', style:TextStyle(fontSize:20,fontWeight:FontWeight.bold)), Text('Total', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11))]),
                          Column(children:[Text('$reportedCount', style:TextStyle(fontSize:20,fontWeight:FontWeight.bold,color:Colors.green)), Text('Reported', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11))]),
                          Column(children:[Text('$notReported', style:TextStyle(fontSize:20,fontWeight:FontWeight.bold,color:Colors.red)), Text('Not Yet', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11))]),
                        ],
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height:12),
                      Container(padding: EdgeInsets.all(10), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.orange[50], borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10), border: Border.all(color: Colors.orange.shade200)), child: Column(children:[
                        Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children:[
                          Row(children:[Icon(Icons.hotel, size:16, color: Colors.orange.shade800), SizedBox(width:6), Text('Hostel Scholars', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:12))]),
                          Text('$hostelReported/$hostelTotal (${hostelPct.toStringAsFixed(0)}%)', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:12, color: Colors.orange.shade800)),
                        ]),
                        SizedBox(height:6),
                        LinearProgressIndicator(value: hostelTotal>0? hostelReported/hostelTotal : 0, backgroundColor: Colors.orange.shade100, valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation(Colors.orange)),
                        SizedBox(height:4),
                        Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children:[
                          Text('Reported: $hostelReported', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10, color: Colors.green.shade700)),
                          Text('Pending: $hostelNot', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10, color: Colors.red.shade700)),
                        ]),
                      ])),
                      SizedBox(height:8),
                      Container(padding: EdgeInsets.all(10), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.blue[50], borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10), border: Border.all(color: Colors.blue.shade200)), child: Column(children:[
                        Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children:[
                          Row(children:[Icon(Icons.directions_walk, size:16, color: Colors.blue.shade800), SizedBox(width:6), Text('Day Scholars', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:12))]),
                          Text('$dayReported/$dayTotal (${dayPct.toStringAsFixed(0)}%)', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:12, color: Colors.blue.shade800)),
                        ]),
                        SizedBox(height:6),
                        LinearProgressIndicator(value: dayTotal>0? dayReported/dayTotal : 0, backgroundColor: Colors.blue.shade100, valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation(Colors.blue)),
                        SizedBox(height:4),
                        Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children:[
                          Text('Reported: $dayReported', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10, color: Colors.green.shade700)),
                          Text('Pending: $dayNot', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10, color: Colors.red.shade700)),
                        ]),
                      ])),
                      SizedBox(height:10),
                      if(notReported==0 && total>0)
                        Container(width: double.infinity, padding: EdgeInsets.all(10), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.green.shade100, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), border: Border.all(color: Colors.green)), child: Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children:[Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: Colors.green.shade800, size:18), SizedBox(width:6), Text('✅ All $total Students Reported!', style: TextStyle(fontWeight:FontWeight.bold, color:Colors.green.shade800))])),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              );
            },
          );
        },
      ),
      SizedBox(height:8),
      Text('HT DASHBOARD', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:18, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1))),
      Card(elevation:3, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1), child: ListTile(title: Text('Total ${studs.length} Students', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:18)), subtitle: Text('Male: $male | Female: $female', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70)), leading: Icon(Icons.people, color: Colors.white, size:36))),

      if(alert.isNotEmpty) Card(color: Colors.red[50], child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [Text('ALERT: Special Access Ended!',style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red,fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, decoration: TextDecoration.none)),...alert.map((s)=>Text('${s.name} ${s.cls} ${s.stream} ${s.gender} GATE ${s.zones['GATE']!.left}d ENDED', style: TextStyle(decoration: TextDecoration.none, color: Colors.black87))).toList()]))),

      SizedBox(height:8),
      Text('By Class', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1), fontSize:22, decoration: TextDecoration.none)),
      Card(color: Colors.white, child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Wrap(spacing:8, runSpacing:8, children: byClass.entries.map((e)=> Chip(label: Text('${e.key}: ${e.value}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, decoration: TextDecoration.none, color: Colors.black87)), backgroundColor: Colors.blue[50])).toList()))),

      SizedBox(height:6),
      Text('By Stream', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1), fontSize:22, decoration: TextDecoration.none)),
      Card(color: Colors.white, child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Wrap(spacing:8, runSpacing:8, children: byStream.entries.map((e)=> Chip(label: Text('${e.key}: ${e.value}', style: TextStyle(decoration: TextDecoration.none, color: Colors.black87)), backgroundColor: Colors.grey[200])).toList()))),

      SizedBox(height:6),
      Text('By Gender', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1), fontSize:22, decoration: TextDecoration.none)),
      Row(children: [
        Expanded(child: Card(color: Colors.blue[50], child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: Column(children: [Icon(Icons.male, color: Colors.blue, size:32), Text('Male', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, decoration: TextDecoration.none, color: Colors.black)), Text('$male', style: TextStyle(fontSize:22, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.blue, decoration: TextDecoration.none))])))),
        Expanded(child: Card(color: Colors.pink[50], child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: Column(children: [Icon(Icons.female, color: Colors.pink, size:32), Text('Female', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, decoration: TextDecoration.none, color: Colors.black)), Text('$female', style: TextStyle(fontSize:22, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.pink, decoration: TextDecoration.none))])))),
      ]),
    ...byClass.entries.map((e)=> Card(color: Colors.white, child: ListTile(leading: Icon(Icons.class_, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1)), title: Text(e.key, style: TextStyle(decoration: TextDecoration.none, color: Colors.black87)), trailing: Text('${e.value} Students', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, decoration: TextDecoration.none, color: Colors.black87)), subtitle: Text('M:${studs.where((s)=>s.cls==e.key&&s.gender=='Male').length} F:${studs.where((s)=>s.cls==e.key&&s.gender=='Female').length} | Streams: ${studs.where((s)=>s.cls==e.key).map((s)=>s.stream).toSet().join(', ')}', style: TextStyle(decoration: TextDecoration.none, color: Colors.black54))))).toList(),
    ]),
    );
  }
}

class StudentPortal extends StatefulWidget {
  final Student student; final List<AttendanceLog> logs; final List<Student> allStudents;
  StudentPortal({required this.student, required this.logs, required this.allStudents});
  @override _StudentPortalState createState()=>_StudentPortalState();
}
class _StudentPortalState extends State<StudentPortal> {
  Student? verifiedStudent;
  MobileScannerController ctrl = MobileScannerController(detectionSpeed: DetectionSpeed.noDuplicates);
  bool isProcessing=false;
  @override void initState(){ super.initState(); verifiedStudent = null; }
  void onScan(String code){
    if(isProcessing) return;
    setState(()=>isProcessing=true);
    String id = code.split('|').length>1? code.split('|')[1].trim().toUpperCase() : code.trim().toUpperCase();
    try{
      var found = widget.allStudents.firstWhere((s)=> s.idNo.toUpperCase().trim() == id);
      setState(()=>verifiedStudent = found);
      ctrl.stop();
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Welcome ${found.name}! Fetched LIVE'), backgroundColor: Colors.green));
    }catch(_){
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('QR not found: $id'), backgroundColor: Colors.red));
    }
    Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: 800), ()=>setState(()=>isProcessing=false));
  }
  @override void dispose(){ ctrl.dispose(); super.dispose(); }
  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    if(verifiedStudent==null){
      return Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(title: Text('My Days - SCAN YOUR QR'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1)),
        body: Column(children: [
          Card(color: Colors.orange[50], margin: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: Row(children: [Icon(Icons.qr_code_scanner,color: Colors.orange), SizedBox(width:8), Expanded(child: Text('Scan your ID QR to fetch info from system', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)))]))),
          Expanded(child: Stack(children: [
            MobileScanner(controller: ctrl, onDetect: (cap){ if(isProcessing) return; for(var b in cap.barcodes){ if(b.rawValue!=null){ onScan(b.rawValue!); break; } } }),
            Center(child: Container(width: 240, height: 240, decoration: BoxDecoration(border: Border.all(color: Colors.green, width:4), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)))),
            if(isProcessing) Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.green)),
            Positioned(bottom:20, left:0, right:0, child: Center(child: Container(color: Colors.black87, padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Text('Point to your ID QR', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white))))),
          ])),
        ])
      );
    }
    var my=widget.logs.where((l)=>l.idNo==verifiedStudent!.idNo).toList();
    int p=my.where((l)=>l.status=='PRESENT').length;
    int a=my.where((l)=>l.status!='PRESENT').length;
    return ListView(padding: EdgeInsets.all(15), children: [
      Card(color: Colors.green[50], child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(10), child: Row(children: [Icon(Icons.verified,color: Colors.green), SizedBox(width:8), Expanded(child: Text('Verified: ${verifiedStudent!.name} ${verifiedStudent!.idNo} ${verifiedStudent!.cls} ${verifiedStudent!.stream}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)))]))),
      Card(color: Colors.blue[50], child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(15), child: Column(children: [
        CircleAvatar(radius: 30, backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), child: Text(verifiedStudent!.name[0], style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize:24))),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        Text(verifiedStudent!.name, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:18)),
        Text('${verifiedStudent!.cls} ${verifiedStudent!.stream} | ${verifiedStudent!.idNo}'),
        SizedBox(height:10),
        Container(padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:12,vertical:4), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: verifiedStudent!.zones['GATE']!.expired? Colors.red[100]:Colors.green[100], borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)), child: Text('GATE: ${verifiedStudent!.zones['GATE']!.left}d left', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
        SizedBox(height:10),
        Row(children: [
          Expanded(child: Card(child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: Column(children: [Text('$p',style: TextStyle(fontSize:28,color: Colors.green,fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), Text('Present')])))),
          Expanded(child: Card(child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: Column(children: [Text('$a',style: TextStyle(fontSize:28,color: Colors.red,fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), Text('Absent')])))),
        ]),
        ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.qr_code), label: Text('Re-Scan My QR'), style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.orange), onPressed: (){ setState((){ verifiedStudent=null; ctrl.start(); }); })
      ]))),
 ...my.map((l)=>Card(child: ListTile(leading: Icon(l.status=='PRESENT'?Icons.check_circle:Icons.cancel,color: l.status=='PRESENT'?Colors.green:Colors.red), title: Text('${l.date} ${l.period}'), trailing: Text(l.status, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: l.status=='PRESENT'?Colors.green:Colors.red))))).toList()
    ]);
  }
}

class StudentQR extends StatelessWidget {
  final Student student; final List<AttendanceLog> logs;
  StudentQR({required this.student, required this.logs});
  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    var my=logs.where((l)=>l.idNo==student.idNo).toList();
    int p=my.where((l)=>l.status=='PRESENT').length;
    int a=my.where((l)=>l.status!='PRESENT').length;
    String qrData='BUSALAMU|${student.idNo}|${student.name}|${student.cls}-${student.stream}|GATE:${student.zones['GATE']!.left}d|CLASS:${student.zones['CLASS']!.left}d';
    return ListView(padding: EdgeInsets.all(15), children: [
      Card(color: Colors.blue[50], child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: Column(children: [
        Text('My QR Code - Generated From System (4 Streams)', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1))),
        Text('Show this at Gate & Class', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)),
      ]))),
      Card(elevation:4, child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(20), child: Column(children: [
        QrImageView(data: qrData, size: 220, version: QrVersions.auto, backgroundColor: Colors.white),
        SizedBox(height:12),
        Text(student.name, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:18)),
        Text('${student.cls} ${student.stream}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:14)),
        Text(student.idNo, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1))),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children: [
          Container(padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:8,vertical:4), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: student.zones['GATE']!.expired? Colors.red[100]:Colors.green[100], borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)), child: Text('GATE ${student.zones['GATE']!.left}d', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
          SizedBox(width:6),
          Container(padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:8,vertical:4), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: student.zones['CLASS']!.expired? Colors.red[100]:Colors.green[100], borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)), child: Text('CLASS ${student.zones['CLASS']!.left}d', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
        ]),
        SizedBox(height:12),
        Text('Present: $p | Absent: $a - LIVE', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12)),
      ]))),
    ]);
  }
}

class GateReal extends StatefulWidget {
  final List<Student> studs;
  final Function(Student,String) onDeny;
  final List<PermissionAlert> perms;
  final Function(PermissionAlert) onPermissionReturned;
  GateReal({required this.studs, required this.onDeny, required this.perms, required this.onPermissionReturned});
  @override _GateRealState createState()=>_GateRealState();
}

class _GateRealState extends State<GateReal> {
  String zone='GATE';
  ClassAccessConfig classAccessConfig=ClassAccessConfig();
  bool configLoaded=false;
  String selectedMeal='Auto'; // <-- for manual meal choice
  bool? ok;
  String msg='Ready - Continuous Scan';
  final ctrl=TextEditingController();

  // MESS anti-double helpers
  String getCurrentMeal(){
    if(selectedMeal!= 'Auto') return selectedMeal;
    int h=DateTime.now().hour;
    if(h>=5 && h<11) return 'Breakfast';
    if(h>=11 && h<16) return 'Lunch';
    if(h>=17 && h<22) return 'Supper';
    return 'Snack';
  }
  bool isSameDay(DateTime a, DateTime b)=> a.year==b.year && a.month==b.month && a.day==b.day;

  MobileScannerController scannerController = MobileScannerController(detectionSpeed: DetectionSpeed.noDuplicates);
  bool isScanning=true; bool isProcessing=false; String lastCode='';

  @override void initState(){ super.initState(); scannerController.start(); loadClassConfig(); }
  Future<void> loadClassConfig() async {
    try{
      var doc=await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('app_settings').doc('class_access_config').get();
      if(doc.exists){ if(mounted) setState(()=>classAccessConfig=ClassAccessConfig.fromMap(doc.data()!)); configLoaded=true; }
      FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('app_settings').doc('class_access_config').snapshots().listen((d){
        if(d.exists && mounted){ setState(()=>classAccessConfig=ClassAccessConfig.fromMap(d.data()!)); }
      });
    }catch(_){}
  }

    void verify(String code) async {
    if(isProcessing) return;
    if(code.isEmpty) return;
    if(code==lastCode) return;

    // PERMISSION AUTO-BACK - FIXED: use widget.perms directly, not findAncestor
    try{
      var pending = widget.perms.where((p)=> code.contains(p.studentId) || p.studentId.contains(code.split('|').length>1?code.split('|')[1]:code) ).toList();
      if(pending.isNotEmpty){
        await widget.onPermissionReturned(pending.first);
        if(mounted){
          setState((){ ok=true; msg='${pending.first.studentName} IS BACK - Permission Cleared ✅'; isProcessing=false; lastCode=''; });
          Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: 1500), (){ if(mounted){ setState((){ lastCode=''; }); } });
        }
        return;
      }
    }catch(_){}

    setState((){ isProcessing=true; lastCode=code; });
    String id=code.split('|').length>1?code.split('|')[1].trim().toUpperCase():code.trim().toUpperCase();
    var s=widget.studs.firstWhere((e)=>e.idNo.toUpperCase().trim()==id, orElse: ()=>Student(name:'Unknown',cls:'?',stream:'?',idNo:id,zones:{'GATE':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:0),'CLASS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:0),'MESS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:0)}));

    bool valid=s.zones[zone]!=null&&!s.zones[zone]!.expired;
    // === CLASS ACCESS TIME CHECK - NEW ===
    if(valid && zone=='CLASS' && classAccessConfig.enforceTime && s.name!='Unknown'){
      if(!classAccessConfig.isTimeAllowedForClass(s.cls, s.stream, DateTime.now())){
        valid=false;
        setState((){
          ok=false;
          msg='${s.name} ${s.cls} ${s.stream} DENIED - CLASS CLOSED ⏰ ${classAccessConfig.timeReason(s.cls, s.stream)}';
        });
        Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: 2500), (){ if(mounted){ setState((){ isProcessing=false; lastCode=''; }); } });
        return;
      }
    }
    String currentMeal = getCurrentMeal();
    if(valid && zone=='MESS' && s.name!='Unknown'){
      // === NEW RULE: Day = Lunch only Mon-Fri, Hostel = all meals everyday ===
      bool isDay = s.studentType.toLowerCase().trim()=='day' || s.studentType.trim().isEmpty;
      int wd = DateTime.now().weekday; // 1=Mon ... 7=Sun
      bool isWeekend = wd==DateTime.saturday || wd==DateTime.sunday;

      if(isDay){
        if(isWeekend){
          valid=false;
          setState((){ ok=false; msg='${s.name} DENIED - DAY SCHOLARS NO WEEKEND MEALS (${currentMeal})'; });
          try{
            await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('meal_logs').add({
              'studentId': s.idNo, 'name': s.name, 'cls': s.cls, 'stream': s.stream,
              'meal': currentMeal, 'status': 'DENIED_DAY_WEEKEND', 'time': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
            });
          }catch(_){}
          Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: 2500), (){ if(mounted){ setState((){ isProcessing=false; lastCode=''; }); } });
          return;
        }
        if(currentMeal!='Lunch'){
          valid=false;
          setState((){ ok=false; msg='${s.name} DENIED - DAY SCHOLARS LUNCH ONLY (tried $currentMeal)'; });
          try{
            await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('meal_logs').add({
              'studentId': s.idNo, 'name': s.name, 'cls': s.cls, 'stream': s.stream,
              'meal': currentMeal, 'status': 'DENIED_DAY_ONLY_LUNCH', 'time': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
            });
          }catch(_){}
          Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: 2500), (){ if(mounted){ setState((){ isProcessing=false; lastCode=''; }); } });
          return;
        }
      }
      // Hostel: no restriction, all meals everyday
      // --- Anti-double check (applies to both Day and Hostel) ---
      if(s.lastMessScan!=null && isSameDay(s.lastMessScan!, DateTime.now()) && s.lastMessMeal==currentMeal){
        valid=false;
        setState((){ ok=false; msg='${s.name} ALREADY SERVED $currentMeal today at ${s.lastMessScan!.hour}:${s.lastMessScan!.minute.toString().padLeft(2,'0')} - DENIED DOUBLE'; });
        try{
          await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('meal_logs').add({
            'studentId': s.idNo, 'name': s.name, 'cls': s.cls, 'stream': s.stream,
            'meal': currentMeal, 'status': 'DENIED_DOUBLE', 'time': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
          });
        }catch(_){}
        Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: 2000), (){ if(mounted){ setState((){ isProcessing=false; lastCode=''; }); } });
        return;
      }
    }
    
    if(valid && zone=='MESS' && s.name!='Unknown'){
      s.lastMessScan = DateTime.now();
      s.lastMessMeal = currentMeal;
      try{
        await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('students').doc(s.safeDocId).update({
          'lastMessScan': s.lastMessScan!.toIso8601String(), 'lastMessMeal': currentMeal,
        });
        await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('meal_logs').add({
          'studentId': s.idNo, 'name': s.name, 'cls': s.cls, 'stream': s.stream,
          'meal': currentMeal, 'status': 'SERVED', 'time': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
        });
      }catch(_){}
    }
    if(valid && s.name!='Unknown'){
  try{
    var termDoc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('settings').doc('currentTerm').get();
    if(termDoc.exists){
      var term = TermSettings.fromMap(termDoc.data()!);
      String docId = '${s.idNo}_${term.termName}'.replaceAll('/', '_').replaceAll(' ', '_');
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('reportedBack').doc(docId).set({
        'studentId': s.idNo,
        'studentName': s.name,
        'cls': s.cls,
        'stream': s.stream,
        'studentType': s.studentType,
        'term': term.termName,
        'reportedDate': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
        'reportedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
        'reportedBy': 'Gate $zone',
        'zone': zone,
      }, SetOptions(merge: true));
    }
  }catch(_){}
}
    setState((){ ok=valid; msg='${s.name} ${s.cls} ${s.stream} ${zone}:${s.zones[zone]?.left}d ${zone=='MESS' && valid? ' $currentMeal SERVED':''}'; });
    if(!valid&&s.name!='Unknown') widget.onDeny(s,zone);
    Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: 1500), (){ if(mounted){ setState((){ isProcessing=false; lastCode=''; }); } });
  }

  @override void dispose(){ scannerController.dispose(); super.dispose(); }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c)=>Scaffold(
    appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Gate $zone - CONTINUOUS'),backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1)),
    body: Column(children: [
      Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Row(children:[
        Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: zone,isExpanded:true,items: ['GATE','CLASS','MESS'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text(e))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>zone=v!))),
        if(zone=='MESS') SizedBox(width:8),
        if(zone=='MESS') Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: selectedMeal,isExpanded:true,items: ['Auto','Breakfast','Lunch','Supper','Snack'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text(e))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>selectedMeal=v!))),
      ])),
      Expanded(flex:3, child: Stack(children: [
        MobileScanner(controller: scannerController, onDetect: (capture){ if(isProcessing) return; for(final barcode in capture.barcodes){ if(barcode.rawValue!=null){ verify(barcode.rawValue!); break; } } }),
        Center(child: Container(width: 220, height: 220, decoration: BoxDecoration(border: Border.all(color: ok==null? Colors.green : ok!? Colors.green : Colors.red,width:4), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)))),
        if(isProcessing) Center(child: Container(padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:16,vertical:8), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black54, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)), child: Text('SCANNED! NEXT...',style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white,fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)))),
        Positioned(bottom:10, left:0, right:0, child: Center(child: Container(color: Colors.black87, padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Text(msg,style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white,fontSize:12))))),
      ])),
      Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: Column(children: [
        Row(children: [Expanded(child: TextField(controller: ctrl,decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Manual QR',border: OutlineInputBorder()), onSubmitted: verify)), SizedBox(width:8), ElevatedButton(onPressed: ()=>verify(ctrl.text), child: Text('CHECK'))]),
        if(ok!=null) Container(margin: EdgeInsets.only(top:10),padding: EdgeInsets.all(14), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: ok!?Colors.green[50]:Colors.red[50], borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), border: Border.all(color: ok!?Colors.green:Colors.red)), child: Row(children: [Icon(ok!?Icons.verified:Icons.block,size:40,color: ok!?Colors.green:Colors.red), SizedBox(width:10), Expanded(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [Text(ok!?'ALLOWED':'DENIED',style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,fontSize:16)), Text(msg)]))]))
      ]))
    ]));
}

class AttendReal extends StatefulWidget { final List<Student> studs; final List<Timetable> timetable; final List<AttendanceLog> logs; final Function(AttendanceLog) onSyncLog; final Function(Student) onSyncStudent; AttendReal({required this.studs, required this.timetable, required this.logs, required this.onSyncLog, required this.onSyncStudent}); @override _AttendRealState createState()=>_AttendRealState(); }
class _AttendRealState extends State<AttendReal> {
  String selClass='All'; String selStream='All'; String selHostelType='All'; String selHostelStatus='All';
  DateTime fromDate=DateTime.now().subtract(Duration(days:30));
  DateTime toDate=DateTime.now();
  TimeOfDay fromTime=TimeOfDay(hour: 0, minute: 0);
  TimeOfDay toTime=TimeOfDay(hour: 23, minute: 59);
  List<String> classes=['All','S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4','S.5','S.6'];
  List<String> get streams {
    if(selClass=='All') return ['All','Blue','Red','Green','White','Sciences','Arts'];
    if(selClass=='S.5'||selClass=='S.6') return ['All','Sciences','Arts'];
    return ['All','Blue','Red','Green','White'];
  }
  MobileScannerController scannerController = MobileScannerController(detectionSpeed: DetectionSpeed.noDuplicates);
  bool isProcessing=false; String lastCode=''; String lastScannedName=''; bool showFloat=false;
  ClassAccessConfig classAccessConfig=ClassAccessConfig(); bool configLoaded=false;
  @override void initState(){ super.initState(); scannerController.start(); loadClassConfigAttend(); }
  Future<void> loadClassConfigAttend() async { try{ var doc=await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('app_settings').doc('class_access_config').get(); if(doc.exists && mounted) setState(()=>classAccessConfig=ClassAccessConfig.fromMap(doc.data()!)); FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('app_settings').doc('class_access_config').snapshots().listen((d){ if(d.exists && mounted) setState(()=>classAccessConfig=ClassAccessConfig.fromMap(d.data()!)); }); configLoaded=true; }catch(_){} }
  @override void dispose(){ scannerController.dispose(); super.dispose(); }
  DateTime get fromDateTime => DateTime(fromDate.year, fromDate.month, fromDate.day, fromTime.hour, fromTime.minute);
  DateTime get toDateTime => DateTime(toDate.year, toDate.month, toDate.day, toTime.hour, toTime.minute);
  List<AttendanceLog> get filteredLogs => widget.logs.where((l){
    if(selClass!='All' && l.cls!=selClass) return false;
    if(selStream!='All' && l.stream!=selStream) return false;
    if(selHostelType!='All'){ var st = widget.studs.firstWhere((e)=>e.idNo==l.idNo, orElse: ()=>Student(name:'',cls:'',stream:'',idNo:'',zones:{},studentType:'Day')); if(st.studentType!=selHostelType) return false; }
    if(selHostelStatus=='PRESENT' && l.status!='PRESENT') return false;
    if(selHostelStatus=='ABSENT' && l.status=='PRESENT') return false;
    try{ DateTime d=DateTime.parse(l.date); return!d.isBefore(fromDateTime) &&!d.isAfter(toDateTime); }catch(_){ return true; }
  }).toList();

  Future<void> mark(String code) async {
    if(isProcessing) return; if(code==lastCode) return;
    setState(()=>isProcessing=true); lastCode=code;
    String id=code.split('|').length>1?code.split('|')[1].trim().toUpperCase():code.trim().toUpperCase();
    int idx=widget.studs.indexWhere((s)=>s.idNo.toUpperCase().trim()==id);
    if(idx!=-1){
      var s=widget.studs[idx];
      // === CLASS ACCESS TIME CHECK FOR ATTEND CAM - NEW ===
      if(classAccessConfig.enforceTime){
        if(!classAccessConfig.isTimeAllowedForClass(s.cls, s.stream, DateTime.now())){
          setState((){
            lastScannedName='${s.name} - ${s.cls} ${s.stream} CLOSED ⏰ ${classAccessConfig.timeReason(s.cls, s.stream)}';
            showFloat=true;
          });
          Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds:2000),(){ if(mounted) setState(()=>isProcessing=false); });
          Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds:3500),(){ if(mounted) setState((){lastScannedName=''; showFloat=false;}); });
          return;
        }
      }
      // ENFORCE FILTER
      if(selClass!='All' && s.cls!=selClass){
        setState((){
          lastScannedName='${s.name} - ${s.cls} ${s.stream} ❌ WRONG CLASS! Selected $selClass';
          showFloat=true;
        });
        Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds:2000),(){ if(mounted) setState(()=>isProcessing=false); });
        Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds:3500),(){ if(mounted) setState((){lastScannedName=''; showFloat=false;}); });
        return;
      }
      if(selStream!='All' && s.stream!=selStream){
        setState((){
          lastScannedName='${s.name} - ${s.cls} ${s.stream} ❌ WRONG STREAM! Selected $selStream';
          showFloat=true;
        });
        Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds:2000),(){ if(mounted) setState(()=>isProcessing=false); });
        Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds:3500),(){ if(mounted) setState((){lastScannedName=''; showFloat=false;}); });
        return;
      }
      var log=AttendanceLog(idNo:s.idNo,cls:s.cls,stream:s.stream,date:DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),period:'P1',status:'PRESENT');
      widget.onSyncLog(log);
      try{
  var termDoc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('settings').doc('currentTerm').get();
  if(termDoc.exists){
    var term = TermSettings.fromMap(termDoc.data()!);
    String docId = '${s.idNo}_${term.termName}'.replaceAll('/', '_').replaceAll(' ', '_');
    await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('reportedBack').doc(docId).set({
      'studentId': s.idNo,
      'studentName': s.name,
      'cls': s.cls,
      'stream': s.stream,
      'studentType': s.studentType,
      'term': term.termName,
      'reportedDate': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      'reportedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      'reportedBy': 'Attend CAM',
    }, SetOptions(merge: true));
  }
}catch(_){}
      setState((){
        lastScannedName='${s.name} - ${s.cls} ${s.stream} - PRESENT ✅';
        showFloat=true;
      });
    }
    else {
      setState((){
        lastScannedName='Unknown ID: $id ❌';
        showFloat=true;
      });
    }
    Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: 2000), (){ if(mounted) setState(()=>isProcessing=false); });
    Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: 3500), (){ if(mounted) setState((){ lastScannedName=''; showFloat=false; }); });
  }

  Future<void> exportPdf(bool present) async {
    var logsToExport=filteredLogs.where((l)=> present? l.status=='PRESENT' : l.status!='PRESENT').toList();
    if(logsToExport.isEmpty){ ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('No data'), backgroundColor: Colors.orange)); return; }
    final pdf=pw.Document();
    pdf.addPage(pw.MultiPage(header: (c)=>pw.Column(children:[pw.Text('BSS ATTENDANCE - ${present?'PRESENT':'ABSENT'} $selClass $selStream $selHostelType', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold, fontSize:12)), pw.Text('From: $fromDateTime To: $toDateTime', style: pw.TextStyle(fontSize:10))]), build: (c)=>[ pw.Table.fromTextArray(headers: ['Date','Class','Stream','Hostel','ID','Name','Status'], data: logsToExport.map((l){ var s=widget.studs.firstWhere((e)=>e.idNo==l.idNo, orElse: ()=>Student(name:'?',cls:l.cls,stream:l.stream,idNo:l.idNo,zones:{})); return [l.date.split('T')[0], l.cls, l.stream, s.studentType, l.idNo, s.name, l.status]; }).toList())]));
    await Printing.layoutPdf(onLayout: (f) async => pdf.save());
  }
  Future<void> exportExcel(bool all) async {
    var excel=Excel.createExcel(); var sheet=excel['Sheet1'];
    sheet.appendRow([TextCellValue('Date'),TextCellValue('Class'),TextCellValue('Stream'),TextCellValue('Hostel'),TextCellValue('ID'),TextCellValue('Name'),TextCellValue('Status')]);
    for(var l in filteredLogs){ var s=widget.studs.firstWhere((e)=>e.idNo==l.idNo, orElse: ()=>Student(name:'?',cls:l.cls,stream:l.stream,idNo:l.idNo,zones:{})); sheet.appendRow([TextCellValue(l.date),TextCellValue(l.cls),TextCellValue(l.stream),TextCellValue(s.studentType),TextCellValue(l.idNo),TextCellValue(s.name),TextCellValue(l.status)]); }
    var bytes=excel.save(); if(bytes!=null){ await Printing.sharePdf(bytes: Uint8List.fromList(bytes), filename: 'attend_${selClass}_${selStream}_${selHostelType}.xlsx'); }
  }
  Future<void> pickFromDate() async { var d=await showDatePicker(context: context, initialDate: fromDate, firstDate: DateTime(2025), lastDate: DateTime(3000)); if(d!=null) setState(()=>fromDate=d); }
  Future<void> pickToDate() async { var d=await showDatePicker(context: context, initialDate: toDate, firstDate: DateTime(2025), lastDate: DateTime(3000)); if(d!=null) setState(()=>toDate=d); }
  Future<void> pickFromTime() async { var t=await showTimePicker(context: context, initialTime: fromTime); if(t!=null) setState(()=>fromTime=t); }
  Future<void> pickToTime() async { var t=await showTimePicker(context: context, initialTime: toTime); if(t!=null) setState(()=>toTime=t); }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    var presentLogs=filteredLogs.where((l)=>l.status=='PRESENT').length;
    var absentLogs=filteredLogs.where((l)=>l.status!='PRESENT').length;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Attend CAM $selClass $selStream $selHostelType'),backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1)),
      floatingActionButtonLocation: FloatingActionButtonLocation.endFloat,
      floatingActionButton: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
        SizedBox(height: 28, child: FloatingActionButton.extended(heroTag:'p', backgroundColor: Colors.green, icon: Icon(Icons.check, size:16), label: Text('PRESENT ($presentLogs)', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)), onPressed: ()=>exportPdf(true))),
        SizedBox(height:4),
        SizedBox(height: 28, child: FloatingActionButton.extended(heroTag:'a', backgroundColor: Colors.red, icon: Icon(Icons.close, size:16), label: Text('ABSENT ($absentLogs)', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)), onPressed: ()=>exportPdf(false))),
        SizedBox(height:4),
        SizedBox(height: 28, child: FloatingActionButton.extended(heroTag:'e', backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), icon: Icon(Icons.table_chart, size:16), label: Text('EXCEL', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)), onPressed: ()=>exportExcel(true))),
      ]),
      body: Column(children: [
        // REDUCED CAMERA GRID - 160 height so all features seen
        Container(height: 160, margin: EdgeInsets.all(8), decoration: BoxDecoration(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12), border: Border.all(color: Colors.green, width:2)), child: ClipRRect(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10), child: Stack(children:[
          MobileScanner(controller: scannerController, fit: BoxFit.cover, onDetect: (cap){ for(final b in cap.barcodes){ if(b.rawValue!=null){ mark(b.rawValue!); break; } } }),
          Center(child: Container(width: 160, height: 160, decoration: BoxDecoration(border: Border.all(color: Colors.green, width:3), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)))),
          if(isProcessing) Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.greenAccent)),
          if(showFloat) Positioned(bottom:8, left:8, right:8, child: Container(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black87, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)), child: Text(lastScannedName, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize:11), textAlign: TextAlign.center))),
        ]))),
        // FILTERS - NOW WITH HOSTEL
        Card(color: Colors.blue[50], margin: EdgeInsets.all(6), child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Column(children:[
          Text('Filters: Class / Stream / Hostel / Date & Time', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:11, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1))),
          SizedBox(height:6),
          Row(children:[
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: selClass, isExpanded:true, items: classes.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Class: $e', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)))).toList(), onChanged: (v){ setState((){ selClass=v!; if(!streams.contains(selStream)) selStream='All'; }); })),
            SizedBox(width:6),
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: selStream, isExpanded:true, items: streams.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Stream: $e', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>selStream=v!))),
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:6),
          Row(children:[
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: selHostelType, isExpanded:true, items: ['All','Day','Hostel'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Hostel: $e', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>selHostelType=v!))),
            SizedBox(width:6),
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: selHostelStatus, isExpanded:true, items: ['All','PRESENT','ABSENT'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text(e=='All'? 'All Status' : e, style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>selHostelStatus=v!))),
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:6),
          Row(children:[
            Expanded(child: ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.calendar_today, size:12), onPressed: pickFromDate, label: Text('From: ${fromDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:9)))),
            SizedBox(width:4),
            Expanded(child: ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.access_time, size:12), onPressed: pickFromTime, label: Text('${fromTime.format(context)}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:9)))),
          ]),
          Row(children:[
            Expanded(child: ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.calendar_today, size:12), onPressed: pickToDate, label: Text('To: ${toDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:9)))),
            SizedBox(width:4),
            Expanded(child: ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.access_time, size:12), onPressed: pickToTime, label: Text('${toTime.format(context)}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:9)))),
          ]),
        ]))),
        Expanded(child: ListView.builder(itemCount: filteredLogs.length, itemBuilder: (ctx,i){
          var l=filteredLogs[i]; var s=widget.studs.firstWhere((e)=>e.idNo==l.idNo, orElse: ()=>Student(name:'?',cls:l.cls,stream:l.stream,idNo:l.idNo,zones:{}));
          return ListTile(dense:true, title: Text('${s.name} - ${s.cls} ${s.stream} - ${s.studentType}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12)), subtitle: Text('${l.date} | ${l.status}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)), leading: Icon(l.status=='PRESENT'? Icons.check_circle : Icons.cancel, color: l.status=='PRESENT'? Colors.green : Colors.red));
        })),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

class ReportsPage extends StatefulWidget { final List<Student> studs; final List<AttendanceLog> logs; ReportsPage({required this.studs, required this.logs}); @override _ReportsPageState createState()=>_ReportsPageState(); }
class _ReportsPageState extends State<ReportsPage> {
  String selClass='All'; String selStream='All'; String selHostelType='All';
  DateTime fromDate=DateTime.now().subtract(Duration(days:30));
  DateTime toDate=DateTime.now();
  TimeOfDay fromTime=TimeOfDay(hour: 0, minute: 0);
  TimeOfDay toTime=TimeOfDay(hour: 23, minute: 59);
  List<String> classes=['All','S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4','S.5','S.6'];
  List<String> get streams {
    if(selClass=='All') return ['All','Blue','Red','Green','White','Sciences','Arts'];
    if(selClass=='S.5'||selClass=='S.6') return ['All','Sciences','Arts'];
    return ['All','Blue','Red','Green','White'];
  }
  DateTime get fromDateTime => DateTime(fromDate.year, fromDate.month, fromDate.day, fromTime.hour, fromTime.minute);
  DateTime get toDateTime => DateTime(toDate.year, toDate.month, toDate.day, toTime.hour, toTime.minute);
  List<Map<String,dynamic>> get mostAbsent {
    var filtered=widget.studs.where((s){ if(selClass!='All' && s.cls!=selClass) return false; if(selStream!='All'&&s.stream!=selStream) return false; if(selHostelType!='All' && s.studentType!=selHostelType) return false; return true; }).toList();
    var logsInRange = widget.logs.where((l){
      if(selClass!='All' && l.cls!=selClass) return false;
      if(selStream!='All' && l.stream!=selStream) return false;
      try{ DateTime d=DateTime.parse(l.date); return!d.isBefore(fromDateTime) &&!d.isAfter(toDateTime); }catch(_){ return true; }
    }).toList();
    List<Map<String,dynamic>> data=filtered.map((s){ int absent=logsInRange.where((l)=>l.idNo==s.idNo&&l.status!='PRESENT').length; int present=logsInRange.where((l)=>l.idNo==s.idNo&&l.status=='PRESENT').length; return {'student':s,'absent':absent,'present':present,'total':logsInRange.where((l)=>l.idNo==s.idNo).length}; }).toList();
    data.sort((a,b)=>(b['absent'] as int).compareTo(a['absent'] as int));
    return data;
  }
  Future<void> exportPdf() async {
    final pdf=pw.Document();
    pdf.addPage(pw.MultiPage(header: (c)=>pw.Column(children:[
      pw.Text('BSS GoB REPORTS - $selClass $selStream', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold, fontSize:14)),
      pw.Text('From: ${fromDateTime.toString().split('.')[0]} To: ${toDateTime.toString().split('.')[0]}', style: pw.TextStyle(fontSize:10)),
      pw.Text('Total Students: ${mostAbsent.length} | Generated: ${DateTime.now()}', style: pw.TextStyle(fontSize:8)),
    ]), build: (c)=>[pw.Table.fromTextArray(headers: ['Name','ID','Class','Stream','Present','Absent','Total'], data: mostAbsent.map((m){ var s=m['student'] as Student; return [s.name,s.idNo,s.cls,s.stream,m['present'].toString(),m['absent'].toString(),m['total'].toString()]; }).toList())]));
    await Printing.layoutPdf(onLayout: (f) async => pdf.save());
  }
  Future<void> exportExcel() async {
    var excel=Excel.createExcel(); var sheet=excel['Reports'];
    sheet.appendRow([TextCellValue('Name'),TextCellValue('ID'),TextCellValue('Class'),TextCellValue('Stream'),TextCellValue('Present'),TextCellValue('Absent'),TextCellValue('Total'),TextCellValue('From'),TextCellValue('To')]);
    for(var m in mostAbsent){
      var s=m['student'] as Student;
      sheet.appendRow([TextCellValue(s.name),TextCellValue(s.idNo),TextCellValue(s.cls),TextCellValue(s.stream),IntCellValue(m['present']),IntCellValue(m['absent']),IntCellValue(m['total']),TextCellValue(fromDateTime.toString()),TextCellValue(toDateTime.toString())]);
    }
    var bytes=excel.save(); if(bytes!=null){ await Printing.sharePdf(bytes: Uint8List.fromList(bytes), filename: 'reports_${selClass}_${selStream}_${fromDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}_to_${toDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}.xlsx'); }
  }
  Future<void> pickFromDate() async { var d=await showDatePicker(context: context, initialDate: fromDate, firstDate: DateTime(2025), lastDate: DateTime(3000)); if(d!=null) setState(()=>fromDate=d); }
  Future<void> pickToDate() async { var d=await showDatePicker(context: context, initialDate: toDate, firstDate: DateTime(2025), lastDate: DateTime(3000)); if(d!=null) setState(()=>toDate=d); }
  Future<void> pickFromTime() async { var t=await showTimePicker(context: context, initialTime: fromTime); if(t!=null) setState(()=>fromTime=t); }
  Future<void> pickToTime() async { var t=await showTimePicker(context: context, initialTime: toTime); if(t!=null) setState(()=>toTime=t); }
  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    var data=mostAbsent;
    var totalPresent=data.fold(0, (sum,m)=>sum+(m['present'] as int));
    var totalAbsent=data.fold(0, (sum,m)=>sum+(m['absent'] as int));
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Attendance Reports $selClass $selStream'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1)),
      floatingActionButtonLocation: FloatingActionButtonLocation.endFloat,
      floatingActionButton: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end, children:[
        SizedBox(height: 28, child: FloatingActionButton.extended(heroTag:'pdf', backgroundColor: Colors.red, icon: Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf, size:16), label: Text('PDF ${data.length}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), extendedPadding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:10), onPressed: exportPdf)),
        SizedBox(height:4),
        SizedBox(height: 28, child: FloatingActionButton.extended(heroTag:'excel', backgroundColor: Colors.green, icon: Icon(Icons.table_chart, size:16), label: Text('EXCEL ${data.length}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), extendedPadding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:10), onPressed: exportExcel)),
        SizedBox(height:8),
      ]),
      body: Column(children: [
        Card(color: Colors.blue[50], margin: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Column(children:[
          Text('Filters: Class / Stream / Date & Time', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1))),
          SizedBox(height:6),
          Row(children:[
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: selClass, isExpanded:true, items: classes.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Class: $e', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12)))).toList(), onChanged: (v){ setState((){ selClass=v!; if(!streams.contains(selStream)) selStream='All'; }); })),
            SizedBox(width:6),
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: selStream, isExpanded:true, items: streams.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Stream: $e', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>selStream=v!))),
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:6),
          Row(children: [
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: selHostelType,isExpanded:true,items: ['All','Day','Hostel'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Hostel: $e'))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>selHostelType=v!))),
            SizedBox(width:6),
            Expanded(child: OutlinedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.refresh), label: Text('Reset'), onPressed: ()=>setState((){selClass='All'; selStream='All'; selHostelType='All';}))),
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:6),
          Row(children:[ Expanded(child: ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.calendar_today, size:14), onPressed: pickFromDate, label: Text('From: ${fromDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)))), SizedBox(width:4), Expanded(child: ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.access_time, size:14), onPressed: pickFromTime, label: Text('${fromTime.format(context)}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)))), ]),
          Row(children:[ Expanded(child: ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.calendar_today, size:14), onPressed: pickToDate, label: Text('To: ${toDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)))), SizedBox(width:4), Expanded(child: ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.access_time, size:14), onPressed: pickToTime, label: Text('${toTime.format(context)}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)))), ]),
          Container(padding: EdgeInsets.all(6), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.white, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6)), child: Text('Class: $selClass | Stream: $selStream | Students: ${data.length} | P:$totalPresent A:$totalAbsent | ${fromDate.toString().split(' ')[0]} ${fromTime.format(context)} -> ${toDate.toString().split(' ')[0]} ${toTime.format(context)}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold), textAlign: TextAlign.center)),
        ]))),
        Expanded(child: data.isEmpty? Center(child: Text('No students for $selClass $selStream', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey))) : ListView.builder(itemCount: data.length, itemBuilder: (c,i){ var m=data[i]; var s=m['student'] as Student; return Card(child: ListTile(leading: CircleAvatar(child: Text('${i+1}')), title: Text('${s.name} - ${s.cls} ${s.stream}'), subtitle: Text('P:${m['present']} A:${m['absent']} Total:${m['total']} | ${s.idNo}'), trailing: m['absent']>2? Icon(Icons.warning, color: Colors.red): Icon(Icons.check, color: Colors.green))); }))
      ]));
  }
}

class IDGrid extends StatefulWidget { final List<Student> studs; IDGrid({required this.studs}); @override _IDGridState createState()=>_IDGridState(); }
class _IDGridState extends State<IDGrid> {
  String filterClass='All'; String filterStream='All'; String filterHostelType='All'; bool selectMode=false;
  String searchId=''; String searchName='';
  List<String> classes=['All','S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4','S.5','S.6'];
  List<String> get streams {
    if(filterClass=='All') return ['All','Blue','Red','Green','White','Sciences','Arts'];
    if(filterClass=='S.5'||filterClass=='S.6') return ['All','Sciences','Arts'];
    return ['All','Blue','Red','Green','White'];
  }
  List<Student> get list { var l=widget.studs; if(filterClass!='All') l=l.where((s)=>s.cls==filterClass).toList(); if(filterStream!='All') l=l.where((s)=>s.stream==filterStream).toList(); if(filterHostelType!='All') l=l.where((s)=>s.studentType==filterHostelType).toList(); if(searchId.isNotEmpty) l=l.where((s)=>s.idNo.toLowerCase().contains(searchId.toLowerCase())).toList(); if(searchName.isNotEmpty) l=l.where((s)=>s.name.toLowerCase().contains(searchName.toLowerCase())).toList(); return l; }
  Future<void> exportPdf(bool all) async {
    List<Student> toExport=all? list : list.where((s)=>s.selected).toList();
    if(toExport.isEmpty){ ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Select IDs first'))); return; }
    final pdf=pw.Document(); var format = PdfPageFormat(144, 144, marginAll: 5);
    for(var s in toExport){
      String qr='BUSALAMU|${s.idNo}|GATE:${s.zones['GATE']!.left}d';
      pdf.addPage(pw.Page(pageFormat: format, build: (ctx)=>pw.Container(decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(border: pw.Border.all(width:1)), padding: pw.EdgeInsets.all(4), child: pw.Column(crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.center, children: [
        pw.Text('BSS GoB The Great', style: pw.TextStyle(fontSize:6, fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold)),
        pw.Text(s.name, style: pw.TextStyle(fontSize:7, fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold)),
        pw.Text('${s.cls} ${s.stream}', style: pw.TextStyle(fontSize:5)),
        pw.Text(s.idNo, style: pw.TextStyle(fontSize:5)),
        pw.SizedBox(height:3),
        pw.BarcodeWidget(data: qr, barcode: pw.Barcode.qrCode(), width: 60, height: 60),
        pw.SizedBox(height:2),
        pw.Text('GATE ${s.zones['GATE']!.left}d', style: pw.TextStyle(fontSize:5, fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold)),
      ]))));
    }
    await Printing.layoutPdf(onLayout: (f) async => pdf.save());
  }
  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('IDs ${list.length} 2x2 inch'),backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1)),
      floatingActionButton: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
        FloatingActionButton.extended(heroTag:'all', backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), icon: Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf), label: Text('PDF ALL (${list.length})'), onPressed: ()=>exportPdf(true)),
        SizedBox(height:6),
        FloatingActionButton.extended(heroTag:'sel', backgroundColor: Colors.green, icon: Icon(Icons.checklist), label: Text('PDF Sel (${list.where((s)=>s.selected).length})'), onPressed: ()=>exportPdf(false)),
      ]),
      body: Column(children: [
        Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Column(children: [
          Row(children: [
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterClass,isExpanded:true,items: classes.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Class: $e'))).toList(), onChanged: (v){ setState(()=>filterClass=v!); if(!streams.contains(filterStream)) filterStream='All'; })),
            SizedBox(width:6),
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterStream,isExpanded:true,items: streams.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Stream: $e'))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>filterStream=v!))),
            IconButton(icon: Icon(selectMode?Icons.close:Icons.checklist), onPressed: ()=>setState(()=>selectMode=!selectMode))
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:6),
          TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Search by Student ID', prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.badge, size:18), border: OutlineInputBorder(), isDense:true), style: TextStyle(fontSize:12), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>searchId=v)),
          SizedBox(height:6),
          TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Search by Student Name', prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.person_search, size:18), border: OutlineInputBorder(), isDense:true), style: TextStyle(fontSize:12), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>searchName=v)),
        ])),
        Expanded(child: GridView.builder(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), gridDelegate: SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(crossAxisCount:2, childAspectRatio:0.78, crossAxisSpacing:8, mainAxisSpacing:8), itemCount: list.length, itemBuilder: (c,i){
          var s=list[i];
          return Card(elevation:3, child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Column(children: [
            if(selectMode) Checkbox(value: s.selected, onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>s.selected=v!)),
            Text(s.name,style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,fontSize:11),maxLines:1),
            Text('${s.cls} ${s.stream} ${s.idNo}',style: TextStyle(fontSize:9)),
            QrImageView(data: 'BUSALAMU|${s.idNo}|GATE:${s.zones['GATE']!.left}d',size:85),
            Container(padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:6,vertical:2), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: s.zones['GATE']!.expired?Colors.red[100]:Colors.green[100], borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4)), child: Text('GATE ${s.zones['GATE']!.left}d',style: TextStyle(fontSize:9,fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)))
          ])));
        })),
        SizedBox(height:130)
      ])
    );
  }
}

class StudentsPage extends StatefulWidget { final List<Student> studs; final Function(Student) onSync; final FirebaseFirestore db; StudentsPage({required this.studs, required this.onSync, required this.db}); @override _StudentsPageState createState()=>_StudentsPageState(); }
class _StudentsPageState extends State<StudentsPage> {
  final db = FirebaseFirestore.instance;
  String filterClass='All'; String filterStream='All'; 
  String filterHostelType='All'; String searchName='';
  List<String> classes=['All','S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4','S.5','S.6'];
  List<String> get streams {
    if(filterClass=='All') return ['All','Blue','Red','Green','White','Sciences','Arts'];
    if(filterClass=='S.5'||filterClass=='S.6') return ['All','Sciences','Arts'];
    return ['All','Blue','Red','Green','White'];
  }
  List<Student> get filtered { var l=widget.studs; 
    if(filterClass!='All') l=l.where((s)=>s.cls==filterClass).toList(); 
    if(filterStream!='All') l=l.where((s)=>s.stream==filterStream).toList(); 
    if(filterHostelType!='All') l=l.where((s)=>s.studentType==filterHostelType).toList(); 
    if(searchName.isNotEmpty) l=l.where((s)=>s.name.toLowerCase().contains(searchName.toLowerCase())||s.idNo.toLowerCase().contains(searchName.toLowerCase())||s.parentPhone.contains(searchName)).toList(); return l; }
  void addManual(){
  var name=TextEditingController();
  var cls=TextEditingController(text:'S.4');
  var stream=TextEditingController(text:'Blue');
  var id=TextEditingController();
  var parentPhoneC=TextEditingController(text:'256');
  String gender='Male';
  String studentType='Day';
  var hostelBlockC=TextEditingController();
  var gateDaysC=TextEditingController(text:'30'); // NEW
  var classDaysC=TextEditingController(text:'90');
  var messDaysC=TextEditingController(text:'60');
  List<String> s14=['Blue','Red','Green','White'];
  List<String> s56=['Sciences','Arts'];
  showDialog(context: context, builder: (x)=>StatefulBuilder(builder: (c2,setS){
    List<String> curStreams = (cls.text=='S.5'||cls.text=='S.6')? s56 : s14;
    return AlertDialog(title: Text('Add Student - With Gate Access'), content: SingleChildScrollView(child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
      TextField(controller: name,decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Name')),
      TextField(controller: cls,decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Class e.g S.4'), onChanged: (v)=>setS((){})),
      DropdownButton<String>(value: curStreams.contains(stream.text)? stream.text : curStreams.first, isExpanded:true, items: curStreams.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Stream: $e'))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setS(()=>stream.text=v!)),
      SizedBox(height:6),
      Row(children:[Icon(Icons.person, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1)), SizedBox(width:6), Text('Gender:', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), SizedBox(width:12), DropdownButton<String>(value: gender, items: ['Male','Female'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text(e))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setS(()=>gender=v!)),]),
      Row(children:[Text('Type:'), SizedBox(width:12), DropdownButton<String>(value: studentType, items: ['Day','Hostel'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text(e))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setS(()=>studentType=v!)),]),
      if(studentType=='Hostel') TextField(controller: hostelBlockC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Hostel Block e.g Block A')),
      TextField(controller: id,decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'ID No e.g BSS/2026/022')),
      SizedBox(height:8),
      TextField(controller: parentPhoneC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Parent WhatsApp e.g 0772123456', prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.chat, color: Colors.green)), keyboardType: TextInputType.phone),
      Divider(),
      Text('Allocate Access Days', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:12, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1))),
      Row(children: [
        Expanded(child: TextField(controller: gateDaysC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'GATE Days', prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.door_front_door)), keyboardType: TextInputType.number)),
        SizedBox(width:6),
        Expanded(child: TextField(controller: classDaysC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'CLASS Days'), keyboardType: TextInputType.number)),
        SizedBox(width:6),
        Expanded(child: TextField(controller: messDaysC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'MESS Days'), keyboardType: TextInputType.number)),
      ]),
    ])), actions: [ElevatedButton(onPressed: (){
      if(name.text.isNotEmpty&&id.text.isNotEmpty){
        String pPhone = parentPhoneC.text.trim().replaceAll(' ', '').replaceAll('-', '').replaceAll('+', '');
        if(pPhone.startsWith('0')) pPhone = '256' + pPhone.substring(1);
        if(pPhone.isEmpty) pPhone='256700000000';
        int gDays = int.tryParse(gateDaysC.text)?? 30;
        int cDays = int.tryParse(classDaysC.text)?? 90;
        int mDays = int.tryParse(messDaysC.text)?? 60;
                var s=Student(name:name.text,cls:cls.text,stream:stream.text,idNo:id.text,parentPhone:pPhone,gender:gender,studentType:studentType,hostelBlock:hostelBlockC.text,zones:{
          'GATE':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:gDays),
          'CLASS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:cDays),
          'MESS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:mDays)
        });
        widget.onSync(s); Navigator.pop(x);
      }
    }, child: Text('Save'))]);
  }));
  }
  void editParentPhone(Student s){
  var phoneC = TextEditingController(text: s.parentPhone);
  var gateC = TextEditingController(text: s.zones['GATE']?.days.toString()?? '30');
  var classC = TextEditingController(text: s.zones['CLASS']?.days.toString()?? '90');
  var messC = TextEditingController(text: s.zones['MESS']?.days.toString()?? '60');
  String gender=s.gender;
  String studentType=s.studentType;
  var hostelBlockC=TextEditingController(text: s.hostelBlock);
  showDialog(context: context, builder: (x)=>AlertDialog(
    title: Text('Edit ${s.name} - Access & Phone'),
    content: SingleChildScrollView(child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
      Text('${s.cls} ${s.stream} ${s.idNo}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:11)),
      SizedBox(height:10),
      TextField(controller: phoneC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Parent WhatsApp', prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.chat, color: Colors.green), border: OutlineInputBorder()), keyboardType: TextInputType.phone),
      SizedBox(height:12),
      Row(children:[Text('Gender:'), SizedBox(width:12), DropdownButton<String>(value: gender, items: ['Male','Female'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text(e))).toList(), onChanged: (v){ gender=v!; (context as Element).markNeedsBuild(); }),]),
      SizedBox(height:6),
      Row(children:[Text('Hostel Type:'), SizedBox(width:12), DropdownButton<String>(value: studentType, items: ['Day','Hostel'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text(e))).toList(), onChanged: (v){ studentType=v!; (context as Element).markNeedsBuild(); }),]),
      if(studentType=='Hostel') TextField(controller: hostelBlockC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Hostel Block e.g Block A', border: OutlineInputBorder())),
      SizedBox(height:8),
      Text('Gate Access Days', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1), fontSize:12)),
      SizedBox(height:6),
      Row(children: [
        Expanded(child: TextField(controller: gateC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'GATE', border: OutlineInputBorder()), keyboardType: TextInputType.number)),
        SizedBox(width:6),
        Expanded(child: TextField(controller: classC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'CLASS', border: OutlineInputBorder()), keyboardType: TextInputType.number)),
        SizedBox(width:6),
        Expanded(child: TextField(controller: messC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'MESS', border: OutlineInputBorder()), keyboardType: TextInputType.number)),
      ]),
      SizedBox(height:8),
      Container(padding: EdgeInsets.all(6), color: Colors.blue[50], child: Text('GATE Days = How long student can enter school gate. 0 = BLOCKED', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10))),
    ])),
    actions: [
      TextButton(onPressed: ()=>Navigator.pop(x), child: Text('Cancel')),
      ElevatedButton(style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.green), onPressed: () async {
        String pPhone = phoneC.text.trim().replaceAll(' ', '').replaceAll('-', '').replaceAll('+', '');
        if(pPhone.startsWith('0')) pPhone = '256' + pPhone.substring(1);
        int gDays = int.tryParse(gateC.text)?? s.zones['GATE']!.days;
        int cDays = int.tryParse(classC.text)?? s.zones['CLASS']!.days;
        int mDays = int.tryParse(messC.text)?? s.zones['MESS']!.days;
        var updated = Student(
          name: s.name, cls: s.cls, stream: s.stream, idNo: s.idNo, parentPhone: pPhone, gender: gender, studentType: studentType, hostelBlock: hostelBlockC.text,
          zones: {
            'GATE': ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:gDays),
            'CLASS': ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:cDays),
            'MESS': ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:mDays),
          }
        );
        await db.collection('students').doc(s.safeDocId).set(updated.toMap());
        Navigator.pop(x);
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('✅ ${s.name} updated - GATE $gDays days'), backgroundColor: Colors.green));
      }, child: Text('SAVE ALL')),
    ],
  ));
  }
  Future<void> exportExcel({bool empty=false}) async {
    var excel=Excel.createExcel(); Sheet sheet=excel['Students'];
    // FIXED: Added CLASS Days, MESS Days
    sheet.appendRow([
      TextCellValue('Name'),
      TextCellValue('Class'),
      TextCellValue('Stream'),
      TextCellValue('ID No'),
      TextCellValue('GATE Days'),
      TextCellValue('CLASS Days'),
      TextCellValue('MESS Days'),
      TextCellValue('Parent WhatsApp'),
      TextCellValue('Gender'),
      TextCellValue('Hostel Type'),
      TextCellValue('Hostel Block')
    ]);
    if(!empty){
      for(var s in filtered){
        sheet.appendRow([
          TextCellValue(s.name),
          TextCellValue(s.cls),
          TextCellValue(s.stream),
          TextCellValue(s.idNo),
          IntCellValue(s.zones['GATE']?.left ?? 0),
          IntCellValue(s.zones['CLASS']?.left ?? 0),
          IntCellValue(s.zones['MESS']?.left ?? 0),
          TextCellValue(s.parentPhone),
          TextCellValue(s.gender),
          TextCellValue(s.studentType),
          TextCellValue(s.hostelBlock)
        ]);
      }
    } else {
      // Template with 4 streams example - now with MESS/CLASS
      sheet.appendRow([
        TextCellValue('Quin Ton Tavius'),
        TextCellValue('S.4'),
        TextCellValue('Blue'),
        TextCellValue('BSS/2026/2222'),
        IntCellValue(30), // GATE
        IntCellValue(90), // CLASS
        IntCellValue(60), // MESS
        TextCellValue('256772123456'),
        TextCellValue('Male'),
        TextCellValue('Day'),
        TextCellValue('')
      ]);
      sheet.appendRow([
        TextCellValue('Nabirye Sarah'),
        TextCellValue('S.4'),
        TextCellValue('Green'),
        TextCellValue('BSS/2026/2223'),
        IntCellValue(30),
        IntCellValue(90),
        IntCellValue(60),
        TextCellValue('256700000001'),
        TextCellValue('Female'),
        TextCellValue('Hostel'),
        TextCellValue('Block A')
      ]);
      sheet.appendRow([
        TextCellValue('Okello John'),
        TextCellValue('S.5'),
        TextCellValue('Sciences'),
        TextCellValue('BSS/2026/2224'),
        IntCellValue(30),
        IntCellValue(90),
        IntCellValue(60),
        TextCellValue('256700000002'),
        TextCellValue('Male'),
        TextCellValue('Hostel'),
        TextCellValue('Block B')
      ]);
    }
    var bytes=excel.save(); if(bytes!=null){ await Printing.sharePdf(bytes: Uint8List.fromList(bytes), filename: empty? 'template_4_streams.xlsx' : 'students_export.xlsx'); }
  }
  Future<void> importExcel() async {
    try{
      FilePickerResult? result=await FilePicker.platform.pickFiles(type: FileType.custom, allowedExtensions: ['xlsx'], withData: true);
      if(result==null || result.files.first.bytes==null) return;

      var excel=Excel.decodeBytes(result.files.first.bytes!);
      // Pick sheet with most rows
      Sheet? sheet;
      int maxR=0;
      for(var k in excel.tables.keys){
        var s=excel.tables[k];
        if(s!=null && s.maxRows>maxR){ maxR=s.maxRows; sheet=s; }
      }
      if(sheet==null){
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('❌ No data sheet'), backgroundColor: Colors.red));
        return;
      }

      String getVal(List<Data?> row, int idx){
        if(idx>=row.length) return '';
        var cell=row[idx];
        if(cell==null || cell.value==null) return '';
        try{
          var v=cell.value;
          if(v is TextCellValue) return v.value.toString().trim();
          if(v is IntCellValue) return v.value.toString().trim();
          if(v is DoubleCellValue){
            double d=v.value;
            return (d==d.truncate()? d.toInt().toString() : d.toString()).trim();
          }
          return v.toString().trim();
        }catch(_){
          return cell.value.toString().trim();
        }
      }

      int added=0, updated=0, skipped=0;
      WriteBatch batch = db.batch();
      int batchCount=0;
      List<String> seenInFile=[]; // to detect duplicates inside Excel

      showDialog(context: context, barrierDismissible: false, builder: (ctx)=> AlertDialog(title: Text('Importing ${sheet!.maxRows-1} rows...'), content: Row(children:[CircularProgressIndicator(), SizedBox(width:10), Text('Please wait...')])));

      for(var i=1;i<sheet.maxRows;i++){
        var row=sheet.row(i);
        if(row.isEmpty) continue;
        String name=getVal(row,0);
        if(name.isEmpty) continue;
        String low=name.toLowerCase();
        if(low=='name' || low.contains('student name') || low.contains('full name')) continue;

        String cls=getVal(row,1); if(cls.isEmpty) cls='S.1';
        String stream=getVal(row,2); if(stream.isEmpty) stream='Blue';
        String idNo=getVal(row,3);
        if(idNo.isEmpty) idNo='BSS/${DateTime.now().year}/${(i+1).toString().padLeft(4,'0')}';
        idNo=idNo.replaceAll(' ', '').toUpperCase();
        if(seenInFile.contains(idNo)){ skipped++; continue; } // duplicate in Excel
        seenInFile.add(idNo);

        // NEW: GATE, CLASS, MESS days support - backward compatible with old template
        int gateDays = 30, classDays = 90, messDays = 60;
        String parentPhone, genderImp, hostelTypeImp, hostelBlockImp;

        // Detect old format (9 cols) vs new format (11 cols)
        // Old: 0 Name,1 Class,2 Stream,3 ID,4 GATE,5 Phone,6 Gender,7 HostelType,8 Block
        // New: 0 Name,1 Class,2 Stream,3 ID,4 GATE,5 CLASS,6 MESS,7 Phone,8 Gender,9 HostelType,10 Block
        
        // Try to parse as new format first
        if (row.length >= 10 && getVal(row,6).isNotEmpty && int.tryParse(getVal(row,6)) != null) {
          // New format detected
          gateDays = int.tryParse(getVal(row,4)) ?? 30;
          classDays = int.tryParse(getVal(row,5)) ?? 90;
          messDays = int.tryParse(getVal(row,6)) ?? 60;
          parentPhone = getVal(row,7);
          genderImp = getVal(row,8);
          hostelTypeImp = getVal(row,9);
          hostelBlockImp = getVal(row,10);
        } else {
          // Old format fallback
          gateDays = int.tryParse(getVal(row,4)) ?? 30;
          classDays = gateDays; // old template used same days for all
          messDays = gateDays;
          parentPhone = getVal(row,5);
          genderImp = getVal(row,6);
          hostelTypeImp = getVal(row,7);
          hostelBlockImp = getVal(row,8);
        }
        if(genderImp.isEmpty) genderImp='Male';
        if(genderImp.toLowerCase().startsWith('f')) genderImp='Female'; else genderImp='Male';
        if(hostelTypeImp.isEmpty) hostelTypeImp='Day';
        if(hostelTypeImp.toLowerCase().startsWith('h') || hostelTypeImp.toLowerCase().startsWith('b')) hostelTypeImp='Hostel'; else hostelTypeImp='Day';

        parentPhone=parentPhone.replaceAll(' ', '').replaceAll('-', '').replaceAll('+', '').replaceAll('(', '').replaceAll(')', '');
        if(parentPhone.startsWith('0')) parentPhone='256'+parentPhone.substring(1);
        if(parentPhone.length==9) parentPhone='256'+parentPhone;
        if(parentPhone.isEmpty || parentPhone.length<9) parentPhone='256700000000';

        var safeId = idNo.replaceAll('/', '_').replaceAll(' ', '_').replaceAll('#', '_');
        var existing = widget.studs.where((s)=>s.idNo.toUpperCase()==idNo.toUpperCase()).toList();

        Map<String,dynamic> data;
        if(existing.isNotEmpty){
          var s=existing.first;
          data = Student(name:s.name, cls:s.cls, stream:s.stream, idNo:s.idNo, parentPhone:parentPhone, gender:genderImp, studentType:hostelTypeImp, hostelBlock:hostelBlockImp, zones:s.zones, status:s.status, prevCls:s.prevCls, academicYear:s.academicYear, graduatedYear:s.graduatedYear, promotionHistory:s.promotionHistory, isOldStudent:s.isOldStudent).toMap();
          updated++;
        } else {
          data = Student(name:name, cls:cls, stream:stream, idNo:idNo, parentPhone:parentPhone, gender:genderImp, studentType:hostelTypeImp, hostelBlock:hostelBlockImp, zones:{'GATE':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:gateDays),'CLASS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:classDays),'MESS':ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(),days:messDays)}, status:'active', academicYear:DateTime.now().year.toString(), isOldStudent:false).toMap();
          added++;
        }

        batch.set(db.collection('students').doc(safeId), data);
        batchCount++;

        if(batchCount>=400){ // Firestore batch limit 500
          await batch.commit();
          batch = db.batch();
          batchCount=0;
        }
      }

      if(batchCount>0) await batch.commit();

      if(mounted){
        Navigator.pop(context); // close loading
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('✅ Done! Imported $added new, Updated $updated, Skipped duplicates $skipped / Total ${sheet.maxRows-1} rows'), backgroundColor: Colors.green, duration: Duration(seconds: 6)));
      }
    }catch(e, st){
      print('Import failed $e $st');
      if(Navigator.canPop(context)) Navigator.pop(context);
      if(mounted) ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Import failed: $e'), backgroundColor: Colors.red));
    }
  }
  
  Future<void> deleteAllStudents() async {
    bool confirm = await showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (c) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text("DELETE ALL STUDENTS?", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        content: Text("This will DELETE ${widget.studs.length} students from Busalamu Firestore PERMANENTLY. Cannot undo! Are you sure?"),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: ()=> Navigator.pop(c,false), child: Text("Cancel")),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.red),
            onPressed: ()=> Navigator.pop(c,true),
            child: Text("DELETE ALL", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white))
          ),
        ],
      ),
    )??false;

    if(!confirm) return;

    showDialog(context: context, barrierDismissible: false, builder: (ctx)=> AlertDialog(title: Text('Deleting...'), content: Row(children:[CircularProgressIndicator(), SizedBox(width:10), Text('Deleting ${widget.studs.length} students')])));

    var firestore = FirebaseFirestore.instance;
    var snap = await firestore.collection('students').get();

    for (var i = 0; i < snap.docs.length; i += 500) {
      var batch = firestore.batch();
      var end = (i+500 > snap.docs.length)? snap.docs.length : i+500;
      for (var j = i; j < end; j++) {
        batch.delete(snap.docs[j].reference);
      }
      await batch.commit();
    }

    if(mounted){
      Navigator.pop(context);
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text("✅ Deleted ${snap.docs.length} students"), backgroundColor: Colors.green));
    }
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c)=>Scaffold(
    appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Students ${filtered.length}/${widget.studs.length}'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1)),
    floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton(child: Icon(Icons.person_add), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), onPressed: addManual),
    body: Column(children: [
      Card(margin: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Column(children: [
        TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(hintText:'Search by Name / ID / Phone - Select from list', prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1)), suffixIcon: searchName.isNotEmpty? IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.clear, size:16), onPressed: ()=>setState(()=>searchName='')) : null, border: OutlineInputBorder(), isDense:true, contentPadding: EdgeInsets.all(10)), style: TextStyle(fontSize:12), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>searchName=v)),
        SizedBox(height:6),
        Row(children: [
          Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterClass,isExpanded:true,items: classes.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Class: $e', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11)))).toList(), onChanged: (v){ setState(()=>filterClass=v!); if(!streams.contains(filterStream)) filterStream='All'; })),
          SizedBox(width:6),
          Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterStream,isExpanded:true,items: streams.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Stream: $e', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>filterStream=v!))),
        ]),
        SizedBox(height:6),
        Row(children: [
          Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterHostelType,isExpanded:true,items: ['All','Day','Hostel'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Hostel: $e', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>filterHostelType=v!))),
          SizedBox(width:6),
          Expanded(child: OutlinedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.refresh, size:16), label: Text('Reset', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11)), onPressed: ()=>setState((){filterClass='All'; filterStream='All'; filterHostelType='All';}))),
        ]),
        SizedBox(height:4),
        Container(padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:8, vertical:4), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.blue[50], borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6)), child: Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children:[Text('Found: ${filtered.length}/${widget.studs.length}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), if(searchName.isNotEmpty) Text('Search: "$searchName"', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1)))])),
        SizedBox(height:4),
        Row(children: [
