          Expanded(child: ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.upload_file, size:16), label: Text('Import', style:TextStyle(fontSize:10)), onPressed: importExcel)),
          SizedBox(width:4),
          Expanded(child: ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.download, size:16), label: Text('Export', style:TextStyle(fontSize:10)), onPressed: ()=>exportExcel())),
          SizedBox(width:4),
          Expanded(child: ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.note_add, size:16), label: Text('Template', style:TextStyle(fontSize:10)), style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.orange), onPressed: ()=>exportExcel(empty:true))),
        ]),
        SizedBox(height:4),
        SizedBox(
          width: double.infinity,
          child: ElevatedButton.icon(
            icon: Icon(Icons.delete_forever, size:16, color: Colors.white),
            label: Text('DELETE ALL ${filtered.length} STUDENTS', style:TextStyle(fontSize:10, color: Colors.white)),
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.red),
            onPressed: deleteAllStudents
          ),
        )
      ]))),
      Expanded(child: ListView.builder(itemCount: filtered.length, itemBuilder: (c,i){
        var s=filtered[i];
        bool isFake = s.parentPhone=='256700000000' || s.parentPhone.isEmpty;
        return Card(color: isFake? Colors.red.shade50 : Colors.white, child: ListTile(
          leading: CircleAvatar(backgroundColor: isFake? Colors.red : Colors.green, child: Icon(isFake? Icons.warning : Icons.person, color: Colors.white)),
          title: Text(s.name, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:13)),
          subtitle: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            Text('${s.cls} ${s.stream} ${s.idNo} - ${s.studentType} ${s.hostelBlock}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)),
            Row(children: [
              Icon(Icons.chat, size:12, color: isFake? Colors.red : Colors.green),
              SizedBox(width:4),
              Text(s.parentPhone, style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, color: isFake? Colors.red : Colors.green, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              if(isFake) Text(' - NO PARENT!', style: TextStyle(fontSize:9, color: Colors.red)),
            ]),
          ]),
          trailing: Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
            IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.edit, color: Colors.blue), onPressed: ()=>editParentPhone(s)),
            IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.delete,color: Colors.red), onPressed: () async {
              bool ok = await confirmDelete(context, '${s.name} - ${s.cls} ${s.stream} ${s.idNo}');
              if(ok) db.collection('students').doc(s.safeDocId).delete();}),
          ]),
          onTap: ()=>editParentPhone(s),
        ));
      }))
    ])
  );
}

class BroadcastPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final List<Student> studs;
  final String currentUserName;
  BroadcastPage({required this.studs, this.currentUserName='HT'});
  @override _BroadcastPageState createState()=>_BroadcastPageState();
}

class _BroadcastPageState extends State<BroadcastPage> {
  String filterClass='S.4'; String filterStream='Blue'; String filterHostelType='All';
  List<String> classes=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4','S.5','S.6'];
  List<String> get streams => (filterClass=='S.5'||filterClass=='S.6')? ['All','Sciences','Arts'] : ['All','Blue','Red','Green','White'];
  TextEditingController msgCtrl=TextEditingController(text:'Hello {name} from Busalamu SS - {class} - {id}');
  TextEditingController searchCtrl=TextEditingController();
  bool sending=false; int sentCount=0; int currentIndex=0;
  String selectedStudentId='';
  List<Student> get allFilteredRaw => widget.studs.where((s){
    if(s.cls!=filterClass) return false;
    if(filterStream!='All'&&s.stream!=filterStream) return false;
    if(filterHostelType!='All' && s.studentType!=filterHostelType) return false;
    return true;
  }).toList();
  List<Student> get filtered => allFilteredRaw.where((s){ var p=s.parentPhone.trim(); return p.isNotEmpty && p.length>=9; }).toList();
  List<Student> get searchFiltered {
    var list = filtered;
    if(searchCtrl.text.trim().isNotEmpty){
      var q=searchCtrl.text.toLowerCase();
      list = list.where((s)=> s.name.toLowerCase().contains(q) || s.idNo.toLowerCase().contains(q) || s.parentPhone.contains(q)).toList();
    }
    if(selectedStudentId.isNotEmpty){
      list = list.where((s)=> s.idNo==selectedStudentId).toList();
    }
    return list;
  }

  Future<void> sendSingle(Student s) async {
    String personalized = msgCtrl.text.replaceAll('{name}', s.name).replaceAll('{class}', '${s.cls} ${s.stream}').replaceAll('{id}', s.idNo);
    String phone = s.parentPhone.toString().trim();
    if(phone.startsWith('0')) phone='256${phone.substring(1)}';
    phone=phone.replaceAll('+','').replaceAll(' ','').replaceAll('-','');
    String enc=Uri.encodeComponent(personalized);
    String url='https://wa.me/$phone?text=$enc';
    try{
      await launchUrl(Uri.parse(url), mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
    }catch(e){
      if(mounted) ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Failed: $e'), backgroundColor: Colors.red));
    }
  }

  Future<void> broadcastWhatsApp() async {
  if(msgCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty){
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Enter message'), backgroundColor: Colors.red));
    return;
  }
  var listToSend = searchFiltered.isNotEmpty? searchFiltered : filtered;
  if(listToSend.isEmpty){
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('No parents'), backgroundColor: Colors.orange));
    return;
  }

  setState((){
    sending=true;
    sentCount=0;
    currentIndex=0;
  });

  for(int i=0;i<listToSend.length;i++){
    var s=listToSend[i];

    String personalized = msgCtrl.text.replaceAll('{name}', s.name).replaceAll('{class}', '${s.cls} ${s.stream}').replaceAll('{id}', s.idNo);
    String phone = s.parentPhone.toString().trim();
    if(phone.startsWith('0')) phone='256${phone.substring(1)}';
    phone=phone.replaceAll('+','').replaceAll(' ','').replaceAll('-','');
    String enc=Uri.encodeComponent(personalized);
    String url='https://wa.me/$phone?text=$enc';

    try{
      await launchUrl(Uri.parse(url), mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
      // DO NOT break if!mounted - keep loop alive
    }catch(e){
      debugPrint('Broadcast error for ${s.name}: $e');
    }

    if(mounted){
      setState((){
        currentIndex=i;
        sentCount++;
      });
    }

    // If not last, wait for user to return from WhatsApp and confirm NEXT
    if(i < listToSend.length - 1){
      if(mounted){
        await showDialog(
          context: context,
          barrierDismissible: false,
          builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
            title: Text('Sent ${i+1}/${listToSend.length}'),
            content: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
              Text('Just sent to: ${s.name}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              SizedBox(height:8),
              Text('Next: ${listToSend[i+1].name} - ${listToSend[i+1].parentPhone}'),
              SizedBox(height:8),
              Text('1. Send message in WhatsApp\n2. Come back here\n3. Tap SEND NEXT', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12, color: Colors.grey[700])),
            ]),
            actions: [
              TextButton(onPressed: (){
                Navigator.pop(ctx);
                // skip remaining? no, just continue
              }, child: Text('CANCEL BROADCAST')),
              ElevatedButton(
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.green),
                onPressed: ()=> Navigator.pop(ctx),
                child: Text('SEND NEXT →')
              ),
            ],
          ),
        );
        // Small gap so WhatsApp can fully close
        await Future.delayed(Duration(milliseconds: 500));
      } else {
        // If app in background, wait 4 sec for user to return
        await Future.delayed(Duration(seconds: 4));
      }
    }
  }

  if(mounted){
    setState(()=>sending=false);
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Broadcast done: $sentCount/${listToSend.length} opened in WhatsApp'), backgroundColor: Colors.green));
  }
}

  Future<void> copyNumbers() async {
    var nums = searchFiltered.map((s)=>s.parentPhone).join(', ');
    await Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: nums));
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Copied ${searchFiltered.length} numbers')));
  }

  Future<void> exportExcel() async {
    var excel=Excel.createExcel(); Sheet sheet=excel['Broadcast'];
    sheet.appendRow([TextCellValue('Name'),TextCellValue('Class'),TextCellValue('Stream'),TextCellValue('ID'),TextCellValue('Phone'),TextCellValue('Hostel')]);
    for(var s in searchFiltered){ sheet.appendRow([TextCellValue(s.name),TextCellValue(s.cls),TextCellValue(s.stream),TextCellValue(s.idNo),TextCellValue(s.parentPhone),TextCellValue(s.studentType)]); }
    var bytes=excel.save();
    if(bytes!=null){ await Printing.sharePdf(bytes: Uint8List.fromList(bytes), filename: 'broadcast_${filterClass}_${filterStream}.xlsx'); }
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    return DefaultTabController(length: 2, child: Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Broadcast & Notice Board'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), bottom: TabBar(tabs: [Tab(icon: Icon(Icons.campaign), text: 'Broadcast'), Tab(icon: Icon(Icons.push_pin), text: 'Notice Board')])),
      body: TabBarView(children: [
        _buildBroadcastTab(c),
        NoticeBoardPage(isHT: true, currentUserName: widget.currentUserName),
      ]),
    ));
  }

  Widget _buildBroadcastTab(BuildContext c){
    int totalRaw=allFilteredRaw.length; int valid=filtered.length; int invalid=totalRaw-valid;
    return Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: Column(children:[
        Row(children:[
          Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterClass,isExpanded:true,items: classes.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Class: $e'))).toList(), onChanged: (v){ setState(()=>filterClass=v!); if(!streams.contains(filterStream)) filterStream='All'; selectedStudentId=''; })),
          SizedBox(width:8),
          Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterStream,isExpanded:true,items: streams.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Stream: $e'))).toList(), onChanged: (v){ setState((){filterStream=v!; selectedStudentId='';}); })),
        ]),
        SizedBox(height:6),
        Row(children:[
          Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterHostelType,isExpanded:true,items: ['All','Day','Hostel'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Hostel: $e'))).toList(), onChanged: (v){ setState(()=>filterHostelType=v!); selectedStudentId=''; })),
          SizedBox(width:8),
          Expanded(child: OutlinedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.refresh), label: Text('Reset Filters'), onPressed: ()=>setState((){filterClass='S.4'; filterStream='Blue'; filterHostelType='All'; selectedStudentId=''; searchCtrl.clear();}))),
        ]),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        Card(child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround, children:[Text('Total: $totalRaw'), Text('Valid: $valid', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.green, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), if(invalid>0) Text('Invalid: $invalid', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red)), Text('Hostel: $filterHostelType', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1)))]))),
        TextField(controller: searchCtrl, decoration: InputDecoration(prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search), hintText: 'Search student name / ID', border: OutlineInputBorder(), suffixIcon: searchCtrl.text.isNotEmpty? IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.clear), onPressed: ()=>setState(()=>searchCtrl.clear())) : null), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>{})),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextField(controller: msgCtrl, maxLines:3, decoration: InputDecoration(border: OutlineInputBorder(), helperText:'Use {name} {class} {id}')),
        Wrap(spacing:6, children:[
          ActionChip(label: Text('{name}'), onPressed: ()=>setState(()=>msgCtrl.text+=' {name}')),
          ActionChip(label: Text('{class}'), onPressed: ()=>setState(()=>msgCtrl.text+=' {class}')),
          ActionChip(label: Text('{id}'), onPressed: ()=>setState(()=>msgCtrl.text+=' {id}')),
        ]),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        if(sending) Column(children:[LinearProgressIndicator(value: searchFiltered.isEmpty? 0 : (currentIndex+1)/searchFiltered.length), SizedBox(height:4), Text('Sending ${currentIndex+1}/${searchFiltered.length} - Sent: $sentCount', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:12))]),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        Row(children:[
          Expanded(child: ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(sending? Icons.hourglass_top : Icons.send), label: Text(sending? 'Sending $sentCount/${searchFiltered.length}' : 'BROADCAST ALL (${searchFiltered.length})'), style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.green), onPressed: sending? null : broadcastWhatsApp)),
          SizedBox(width:8),
          ElevatedButton(onPressed: copyNumbers, child: Text('Copy')),
          SizedBox(width:4),
          ElevatedButton(onPressed: exportExcel, child: Text('Excel')),
        ]),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        if(selectedStudentId.isNotEmpty) Card(color: Colors.blue[50], child: ListTile(leading: Icon(Icons.person, color: Colors.blue), title: Text('Selected: ${widget.studs.firstWhere((e)=>e.idNo==selectedStudentId, orElse: ()=>Student(name:selectedStudentId, cls:'', stream:'', idNo:selectedStudentId, zones:{})).name}'), trailing: IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.close), onPressed: ()=>setState(()=>selectedStudentId='')))),
        Expanded(child: ListView.builder(itemCount: searchFiltered.length, itemBuilder: (ctx,i){
          var s=searchFiltered[i];
          bool isSelected = s.idNo==selectedStudentId;
          return Card(color: isSelected? Colors.green[50] : null, child: ListTile(
            dense:true,
            selected: isSelected,
            leading: CircleAvatar(child: Text(s.name.isNotEmpty? s.name[0] : '?')),
            title: Text('${s.name} - ${s.cls} ${s.stream} ${s.studentType}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12, fontWeight: isSelected? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal)),
            subtitle: Text('${s.idNo} | ${s.parentPhone}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)),
            trailing: Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children:[
              IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.person_pin, color: Colors.blue), onPressed: ()=>setState(()=>selectedStudentId=s.idNo)),
              IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.send, color: Colors.green), onPressed: ()=>sendSingle(s)),
            ]),
            onTap: ()=>setState(()=>selectedStudentId= isSelected? '' : s.idNo),
          ));
        })),
      ]));
  }
}

class NoticeBoardPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final bool isHT;
  final String currentUserName;
  NoticeBoardPage({this.isHT=true, this.currentUserName='HT'});
  @override _NoticeBoardPageState createState()=>_NoticeBoardPageState();
}
class _NoticeBoardPageState extends State<NoticeBoardPage> {
  final db = FirebaseFirestore.instance;
  final List<String> colors = ['0xFFFFF9C4','0xFFFFCCBC','0xFFC8E6C9','0xFFBBDEFB','0xFFD1C4E9','0xFFFFE0B2','0xFFB2DFDB'];

  void addEditNotice({NoticeBoardItem? edit}){
    var msgCtrl = TextEditingController(text: edit?.message??'');
    String selectedColor = edit?.colorHex?? colors[0];
    showDialog(context: context, builder: (x)=>StatefulBuilder(builder: (cx,setS){
      return AlertDialog(
        title: Text(edit==null? '📌 Pin New Notice' : '✏️ Edit Notice'),
        content: SingleChildScrollView(child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children:[
          TextField(controller: msgCtrl, maxLines:5, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Message for Teachers', border: OutlineInputBorder(), hintText:'e.g Staff meeting tomorrow 8am...'), autofocus: true),
          SizedBox(height:12),
          Text('Pick Sticky Color:', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:12)),
          SizedBox(height:6),
          Wrap(spacing:8, children: colors.map((c){
            bool sel = c==selectedColor;
            return GestureDetector(onTap: ()=>setS(()=>selectedColor=c), child: Container(width:36,height:36,decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Color(int.parse(c)), border: sel? Border.all(color: Colors.black, width:3) : Border.all(color: Colors.grey.shade300), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)), child: sel? Icon(Icons.check, size:20) : null));
          }).toList()),
        ])),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: ()=>Navigator.pop(x), child: Text('Cancel')),
          ElevatedButton(style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1)), onPressed: () async {
            if(msgCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty) return;
            var now = DateTime.now();
            if(edit==null){
              var notice = NoticeBoardItem(id: now.millisecondsSinceEpoch.toString(), message: msgCtrl.text.trim(), author: widget.currentUserName, authorRole: widget.isHT? 'HT' : 'Teacher', createdAt: now, modifiedAt: now, colorHex: selectedColor, isPinned: true);
              await db.collection('noticeBoard').doc(notice.id).set(notice.toMap());
            } else {
              var updated = NoticeBoardItem(id: edit.id, message: msgCtrl.text.trim(), author: edit.author, authorRole: edit.authorRole, createdAt: edit.createdAt, modifiedAt: now, colorHex: selectedColor, isPinned: edit.isPinned);
              await db.collection('noticeBoard').doc(updated.id).set(updated.toMap());
            }
            Navigator.pop(x);
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(edit==null? '✅ Notice Pinned!' : '✅ Notice Updated!'), backgroundColor: Colors.green));
          }, child: Text(edit==null? 'PIN IT' : 'UPDATE')),
        ],
      );
    }));
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Colors.grey[100],
      floatingActionButton: widget.isHT? FloatingActionButton.extended(icon: Icon(Icons.push_pin), label: Text('New Notice'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), onPressed: ()=>addEditNotice()) : null,
      body: Column(children:[
        if(widget.isHT) Container(width: double.infinity, padding: EdgeInsets.all(10), color: Colors.amber[100], child: Row(children:[Icon(Icons.info, size:18, color: Colors.orange[800]), SizedBox(width:8), Expanded(child: Text('HT Notice Board: Pin sticky notes for Teachers. Teachers see them live. Includes message, date/time, author.', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, color: Colors.orange[900])))])),
        Expanded(child: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(stream: db.collection('noticeBoard').orderBy('modifiedAt', descending: true).snapshots(), builder: (ctx,snap){
          if(!snap.hasData) return Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
          var docs = snap.data!.docs;
          if(docs.isEmpty) return Center(child: Column(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children:[Icon(Icons.push_pin, size:60, color: Colors.grey[400]), SizedBox(height:12), Text('No Notices Pinned Yet', style: TextStyle(fontSize:16, color: Colors.grey[600])), SizedBox(height:6), if(widget.isHT) Text('Tap + New Notice to pin for Teachers', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12, color: Colors.grey[500]))]));
          return GridView.builder(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), gridDelegate: SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(crossAxisCount: 2, childAspectRatio: 0.85, crossAxisSpacing:10, mainAxisSpacing:10), itemCount: docs.length, itemBuilder: (ctx,i){
            var m = docs[i].data() as Map<String,dynamic>;
            var notice = NoticeBoardItem.fromMap(m);
            var col = Color(int.parse(notice.colorHex));
            return Container(decoration: BoxDecoration(color: col, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12), boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black26, blurRadius:4, offset: Offset(2,2))], border: Border.all(color: Colors.black12)), child: Stack(children:[
              Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children:[
                Row(children:[Icon(Icons.push_pin, size:14, color: Colors.red[700]), SizedBox(width:4), Expanded(child: Text(notice.isPinned? 'PINNED' : '', style: TextStyle(fontSize:9, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.red[700]))), if(widget.isHT) PopupMenuButton<String>(onSelected: (v) async { if(v=='edit') addEditNotice(edit:notice); if(v=='delete'){ bool ok = await confirmDelete(context, 'Notice: ${notice.message.substring(0, notice.message.length>40?40:notice.message.length)}...'); if(ok) db.collection('noticeBoard').doc(notice.id).delete(); } }, itemBuilder: (c)=>[PopupMenuItem(value:'edit', child: Row(children:[Icon(Icons.edit, size:16), SizedBox(width:6), Text('Edit')])), PopupMenuItem(value:'delete', child: Row(children:[Icon(Icons.delete, size:16, color: Colors.red), SizedBox(width:6), Text('Delete', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red))]))])]),
                SizedBox(height:6),
                Expanded(child: SingleChildScrollView(child: Text(notice.message, style: TextStyle(fontSize:13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500, height:1.3)))),
                Divider(height:12),
                Text('By: ${notice.author} (${notice.authorRole})', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.black87)),
                SizedBox(height:2),
                Text('Created: ${notice.createdAt.toString().split('.')[0]}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:8, color: Colors.black54)),
                Text('Modified: ${notice.modifiedAt.toString().split('.')[0]}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:8, color: Colors.black87, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              ])),
              Positioned(top:0, right:12, child: Container(width:16, height:20, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.red[700], borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(bottomLeft: Radius.circular(4), bottomRight: Radius.circular(4))), child: Icon(Icons.push_pin, size:10, color: Colors.white))),
            ]));
          });
        })),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

class TeachersPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final List<ClassTeacher> teachers;
  final Function(ClassTeacher) onSync;
  final FirebaseFirestore db;
  final List<LessonLog> lessonLogs;
  final List<Timetable> timetable;
  TeachersPage({required this.teachers, required this.onSync, required this.db, this.lessonLogs=const [], this.timetable=const []});
  @override _TeachersPageState createState()=>_TeachersPageState();
}
class _TeachersPageState extends State<TeachersPage> {
  void addEdit({ClassTeacher? edit}){
    var name=TextEditingController(text: edit?.name??'');
    var wa=TextEditingController(text: edit?.whatsapp??'2567');
    var sub=TextEditingController(text: edit?.subject??'');
    var cls=TextEditingController(text: edit?.cls??'S.4');
    var stream=TextEditingController(text: edit?.stream??'Blue');
    List<String> s14=['Blue','Red','Green','White'];
    List<String> s56=['Sciences','Arts'];
    String oldKey = edit!=null? '${edit.cls}_${edit.stream}_${edit.name}'.replaceAll('/', '_') : '';
    showDialog(context: context, builder: (x)=>StatefulBuilder(builder: (c2,setS){
      List<String> curStreams = (cls.text=='S.5'||cls.text=='S.6')? s56 : s14;
      return AlertDialog(
        title: Text(edit==null?'Add Teacher Profile':'Edit Teacher Profile', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        content: SingleChildScrollView(child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
          TextField(controller: cls,decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Class (S.1-S.6)', border: OutlineInputBorder()), onChanged: (v)=>setS(()=>{})),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
            value: curStreams.contains(stream.text)? stream.text : curStreams.first,
            isExpanded:true,
            decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Stream', border: OutlineInputBorder()),
            items: curStreams.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Stream: $e'))).toList(),
            onChanged: (v)=>setS(()=>stream.text=v!)),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          TextField(controller: name,decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Full Name', border: OutlineInputBorder(), prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.person))),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          TextField(controller: wa,decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'WhatsApp (256...)', border: OutlineInputBorder(), prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.phone)), keyboardType: TextInputType.phone),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          TextField(controller: sub,decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Subject', border: OutlineInputBorder(), prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.book))),
        ])),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: ()=>Navigator.pop(x), child: Text('Cancel')),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1)),
            onPressed: (){
              if(name.text.trim().isEmpty){ ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Name required'))); return; }
              var t=ClassTeacher(cls:cls.text.trim(),stream:stream.text.trim(),name:name.text.trim(),whatsapp:wa.text.trim(),subject:sub.text.trim());
              String newKey = '${t.cls}_${t.stream}_${t.name}'.replaceAll('/', '_');
              if(oldKey.isNotEmpty && oldKey!=newKey){
                widget.db.collection('teachers').doc(oldKey).delete();
              }
              widget.onSync(t);
              Navigator.pop(x);
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(edit==null?'✅ Teacher Added':'✅ Teacher Updated'), backgroundColor: Colors.green));
            },
            child: Text(edit==null?'Add':'Update', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white))
          )
        ]);
    }));
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    int todayTaught = widget.lessonLogs.where((l)=> l.date==DateTime.now().toString().split(' ')[0]).length;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Teachers (${widget.teachers.length}) - Today: $todayTaught'),backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1)),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(icon: Icon(Icons.person_add), label: Text('Add Teacher'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), onPressed: ()=>addEdit()),
      body: Column(children: [
        if(widget.lessonLogs.isNotEmpty)
          Container(width: double.infinity, color: Colors.green[50], padding: EdgeInsets.all(8),
            child: Row(children:[Icon(Icons.check_circle, size:16, color: Colors.green[800]), SizedBox(width:6), Expanded(child: Text('Today Lessons: $todayTaught | Total Logs: ${widget.lessonLogs.length} | Tap ⋮ for Edit/Delete', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.green[800])))])),
        Expanded(child: widget.teachers.isEmpty? Center(child: Column(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children:[Icon(Icons.people_outline, size:64, color: Colors.grey[400]), SizedBox(height:12), Text('No Teachers Added Yet'), Text('Tap + Add Teacher', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, color: Colors.grey))])) : ListView.builder(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), itemCount: widget.teachers.length, itemBuilder: (c,i){
          var t=widget.teachers[i];
          return Card(elevation:2, margin: EdgeInsets.only(bottom:8), child: ListTile(
            leading: CircleAvatar(backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), child: Text(t.name.isNotEmpty? t.name[0].toUpperCase() : 'T', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
            title: Text('${t.cls} ${t.stream} - ${t.name}', style:TextStyle(fontSize:13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            subtitle: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children:[
              SizedBox(height:2),
              Row(children:[Icon(Icons.book, size:12, color: Colors.blue[700]), SizedBox(width:4), Text('${t.subject}', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500))]),
              SizedBox(height:2),
              Row(children:[Icon(Icons.phone, size:12, color: Colors.green[700]), SizedBox(width:4), Text('${t.whatsapp}', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11))]),
            ]),
            trailing: PopupMenuButton<String>(
              onSelected: (v) async {
                if(v=='edit'){
                  addEdit(edit:t);
                } else if(v=='delete'){
                  bool ok = await confirmDelete(context, 'Teacher Profile: ${t.name} (${t.cls} ${t.stream} - ${t.subject})', extraWarning: 'Are you sure you want to delete this teacher profile? This cannot be undone. Timetable entries will remain.');
                  if(ok){
                    await widget.db.collection('teachers').doc('${t.cls}_${t.stream}_${t.name}'.replaceAll('/', '_')).delete();
                    if(mounted) ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('🗑️ Deleted ${t.name}'), backgroundColor: Colors.red));
                  }
                }
              },
              itemBuilder: (ctx)=>[
                PopupMenuItem(value:'edit', child: Row(children:[Icon(Icons.edit, size:18, color: Colors.blue), SizedBox(width:8), Text('Edit Profile')])),
                PopupMenuItem(value:'delete', child: Row(children:[Icon(Icons.delete, size:18, color: Colors.red), SizedBox(width:8), Text('Delete Profile', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red))])),
              ],
            ),
          ));
        })),
      ])
    );
  }
}

class TimeTablePage extends StatefulWidget {
  final List<Timetable> timetable;
  final Future<void> Function(Timetable) onSync;
  final FirebaseFirestore db;
  final AppUser curUser;
  final bool readOnly;
  const TimeTablePage({super.key, required this.timetable, required this.onSync, required this.db, required this.curUser, this.readOnly=false});
  @override _TimeTablePageState createState()=>_TimeTablePageState();
}
class _TimeTablePageState extends State<TimeTablePage> {
  String filterDay='All'; String filterCls='All'; String filterStream='All'; String filterTime='All'; String searchQuery='';
  List<String> days=['All','Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday'];
  List<String> classes=['All','S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4','S.5','S.6'];
  List<String> get streams { if(filterCls=='All') return ['All','Blue','Red','Green','White','Sciences','Arts']; if(filterCls=='S.5'||filterCls=='S.6') return ['All','Sciences','Arts']; return ['All','Blue','Red','Green','White']; }
  List<String> times=['All','08:00','09:20','10:00','14:00','P1','P2','P3','Break'];
  List<Timetable> get filtered { var l=widget.timetable; if(filterDay!='All') l=l.where((t)=>t.day==filterDay).toList(); if(filterCls!='All') l=l.where((t)=>t.cls==filterCls).toList(); if(filterStream!='All') l=l.where((t)=>t.stream==filterStream).toList(); if(filterTime!='All') l=l.where((t)=>t.time.contains(filterTime)||t.period==filterTime).toList(); if(searchQuery.isNotEmpty) l=l.where((t)=>t.subject.toLowerCase().contains(searchQuery.toLowerCase())||t.teacher.toLowerCase().contains(searchQuery.toLowerCase())).toList(); return l; }
  String _todayStr()=> DateTime.now().toString().split(' ')[0];
  List<String> get csvHeaders => ['id','day','period','time','cls','stream','subject','teacher','isBreak'];

    
  Future<void> exportPDF() async {
  try {
    var rows = filtered;
    if(rows.isEmpty) rows = widget.timetable;
    if(rows.isEmpty){
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('No timetable data to export'), backgroundColor: Colors.orange));
      return;
    }
    List<String> daysOrder = ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday'];
    List<String> allPeriods = rows.map((e)=> e.period.trim()).where((p)=> p.isNotEmpty).toSet().toList();
    allPeriods.sort((a,b){
      bool aBreak = a.toLowerCase().contains('break');
      bool bBreak = b.toLowerCase().contains('break');
      if(aBreak &&!bBreak) return 1;
      if(!aBreak && bBreak) return -1;
      int na = int.tryParse(a.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^0-9]'), ''))?? 999;
      int nb = int.tryParse(b.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^0-9]'), ''))?? 999;
      if(na!=nb) return na.compareTo(nb);
      return a.compareTo(b);
    });

    Map<String, List<Timetable>> byClass = {};
    for(var t in rows){
      var key = '${t.cls.trim()}|${t.stream.trim()}';
      if(key=='|' ) key = 'General|';
      byClass.putIfAbsent(key, ()=> []).add(t);
    }
    var sortedClassKeys = byClass.keys.toList()..sort((a,b){
      int na = int.tryParse(a.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^0-9]'), ''))?? 999;
      int nb = int.tryParse(b.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^0-9]'), ''))?? 999;
      if(na!=nb) return na.compareTo(nb);
      return a.compareTo(b);
    });
    if(filterCls!='All'){
      sortedClassKeys = sortedClassKeys.where((k)=> k.startsWith('${filterCls}|') || k.split('|')[0]==filterCls).toList();
      if(filterStream!='All'){
        sortedClassKeys = sortedClassKeys.where((k)=> k=='${filterCls}|${filterStream}').toList();
      }
    }

    final pdf = pw.Document();
    for(var classKey in sortedClassKeys){
      var classRows = byClass[classKey]!;
      var cls = classKey.split('|')[0];
      var stream = classKey.split('|').length>1? classKey.split('|')[1] : '';
      Map<String, Timetable> lookup = {};
      for(var t in classRows){
        var d = t.day.trim();
        if(d.isNotEmpty) d = d[0].toUpperCase() + d.substring(1).toLowerCase();
        lookup['$d|${t.period.trim()}'] = t;
      }
      var daysToShow = daysOrder;
      if(filterDay!='All') daysToShow = [filterDay.trim()];

      pdf.addPage(
        pw.Page(
          pageFormat: PdfPageFormat.a4.landscape,
          margin: pw.EdgeInsets.all(10),
          build: (c){
            return pw.Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                pw.Text('BUSALAMU SECONDARY SCHOOL - $cls $stream TIMETABLE', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14, color: PdfColors.blue900)),
                pw.Text('Day: $filterDay | Total: ${classRows.length} | Generated: ${_todayStr()}', style: pw.TextStyle(fontSize: 7, color: PdfColors.grey600)),
                pw.SizedBox(height: 8),
                pw.Table(
                  border: pw.TableBorder.all(width: 0.6),
                  columnWidths: {0: pw.FixedColumnWidth(60)},
                  children: [
                    pw.TableRow(decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(color: PdfColors.blue900), children: [
                      pw.Container(padding: pw.EdgeInsets.all(5), alignment: pw.Alignment.center, child: pw.Text('DAY', style: pw.TextStyle(color: PdfColors.white, fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 8))),
                     ...allPeriods.map((p)=> pw.Container(color: p.toLowerCase().contains('break')? PdfColors.orange900 : PdfColors.blue900, padding: pw.EdgeInsets.all(4), alignment: pw.Alignment.center, child: pw.Text(p.toUpperCase(), style: pw.TextStyle(color: PdfColors.white, fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 8)))).toList(),
                    ]),
                   ...daysToShow.map((day){
                      return pw.TableRow(children: [
                        pw.Container(color: PdfColors.grey200, padding: pw.EdgeInsets.all(8), alignment: pw.Alignment.center, child: pw.Text(day.substring(0,3).toUpperCase(), style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 9))),
                       ...allPeriods.map((per){
                          var key = '${day.trim()}|${per.trim()}';
                          var tt = lookup[key];
                          if(tt==null){
                            var _f = lookup.entries.where((e)=> e.key.toLowerCase()==key.toLowerCase()).toList();
                            if(_f.isNotEmpty) tt=_f.first.value;
                          }
                          if(tt==null) return pw.Container(height: 45);
                          if(tt.isBreak || tt.subject.toLowerCase()=='break' || per.toLowerCase().contains('break')){
                            return pw.Container(height: 45, color: PdfColors.amber100, alignment: pw.Alignment.center, child: pw.Text('BREAK', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 8, color: PdfColors.orange900)));
                          }
                          return pw.Container(height: 45, padding: pw.EdgeInsets.all(3), child: pw.Column(mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.center, children:[
                            pw.Text(tt.subject, textAlign: pw.TextAlign.center, style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 8), maxLines: 2),
                            pw.Text(tt.teacher, textAlign: pw.TextAlign.center, style: pw.TextStyle(fontSize: 6, color: PdfColors.grey600), maxLines: 1),
                          ]));
                        }).toList(),
                      ]);
                    }).toList(),
                  ],
                ),
                pw.Spacer(),
                pw.Text('Page ${sortedClassKeys.indexOf(classKey)+1} of ${sortedClassKeys.length} - $cls $stream', style: pw.TextStyle(fontSize: 6)),
              ],
            );
          }
        )
      );
    }
    await Printing.layoutPdf(onLayout: (f)=> pdf.save(), name: 'timetable_by_class_${_todayStr()}.pdf');
    await Printing.sharePdf(bytes: await pdf.save(), filename: 'timetable_by_class_${_todayStr()}.pdf');
  } catch(e, st){
    print('PDF ERR $e $st');
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('PDF Error: $e'), backgroundColor: Colors.red));
  }
}

  Future<void> downloadTemplate() async {
    StringBuffer sb = StringBuffer();
    sb.writeln(csvHeaders.join(','));
    sb.writeln('"T1","Monday","P1","08:00-08:40","S.4","Blue","Mathematics","Mr. John","FALSE"');
    var bytes = Uint8List.fromList(sb.toString().codeUnits);
    await Printing.sharePdf(bytes: bytes, filename: 'timetable_template.csv');
  }

  Future<void> importFile() async {
  try {
    var result = await FilePicker.platform.pickFiles(type: FileType.custom, allowedExtensions: ['csv','xlsx','xls'], withData: true);
    if(result==null || result.files.isEmpty) return;
    var file = result.files.first;
    if(file.bytes==null){ throw 'File bytes empty - pick again'; }

    List<Timetable> toImport = [];
    List<String> errors = [];
    int skipped=0;

    List<String> validDays = ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday'];

    String clean(String v)=> v.trim().replaceAll('"','').replaceAll("'",'').trim();

    if(file.extension=='csv'){
      String txt = String.fromCharCodes(file.bytes!);
      txt = txt.replaceAll('\r',''); // fix Windows \r\n
      if(txt.startsWith('ï»¿')) txt = txt.substring(3); // remove BOM
      var lines = txt.split('\n').where((l)=>l.trim().isNotEmpty).toList();
      if(lines.isEmpty) throw 'Empty file';
      // detect header: if first line contains 'day' skip it
      int startIdx = 0;
      if(lines.first.toLowerCase().contains('day') && lines.first.toLowerCase().contains('period')) startIdx=1;

      for(int i=startIdx; i<lines.length; i++){
        var line = lines[i];
        if(line.trim().isEmpty) { skipped++; continue; }
        List<String> parts = [];
        bool inQuote=false; StringBuffer cur=StringBuffer();
        for(int ci=0; ci<line.length; ci++){
          var ch=line[ci];
          if(ch=='"'){ inQuote=!inQuote; continue; }
          if(ch==',' &&!inQuote){ parts.add(cur.toString()); cur.clear(); } else { cur.write(ch); }
        }
        parts.add(cur.toString());
        if(parts.length < 6){ errors.add('Row ${i+1}: too few columns'); skipped++; continue; }

        String id = clean(parts.length>0? parts[0]: '');
        String day = clean(parts.length>1? parts[1]: '');
        String period = clean(parts.length>2? parts[2]: '');
        String time = clean(parts.length>3? parts[3]: '');
        String cls = clean(parts.length>4? parts[4]: '');
        String stream = clean(parts.length>5? parts[5]: '');
        String subject = clean(parts.length>6? parts[6]: '');
        String teacher = parts.length>7? clean(parts[7]): '';
        String isB = parts.length>8? clean(parts[8]): 'FALSE';

        if(day.isEmpty ||!validDays.map((e)=>e.toLowerCase()).contains(day.toLowerCase())){
          errors.add('Row ${i+1}: invalid day "$day"'); skipped++; continue;
        }
        // Normalize day capitalization
        day = validDays.firstWhere((d)=>d.toLowerCase()==day.toLowerCase(), orElse: ()=>day);

        if(id.isEmpty) id = 'TT_${day}_${cls}_${stream}_${period}_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch+i}'.replaceAll(' ', '_');
        if(period.isEmpty) period = 'P${i}';
        if(time.isEmpty) time = '08:00-08:40';
        if(cls.isEmpty) cls = 'S.4';
        if(stream.isEmpty) stream = 'Blue';

        bool isBreak = isB.toLowerCase()=='true' || isB=='1' || isB.toLowerCase()=='yes' || subject.toLowerCase()=='break';

        toImport.add(Timetable(id: id.replaceAll('/', '_'), day: day, period: period, cls: cls, stream: stream, subject: subject, teacher: teacher, time: time, isBreak: isBreak));
      }
    } else {
      // Excel
      var excel = Excel.decodeBytes(file.bytes!);
      if(excel.tables.isEmpty) throw 'No sheets found';
      var sheet = excel.tables[excel.tables.keys.first]!;
      if(sheet.maxRows<2) throw 'Sheet empty';
      for(int r=1; r<sheet.maxRows; r++){
        var row = sheet.rows[r];
        if(row.every((c)=> c==null || c.value==null || c.value.toString().trim().isEmpty)){ skipped++; continue; }
        String getVal(int idx)=> idx < row.length? (row[idx]?.value?.toString()??'').trim() : '';
        String id = clean(getVal(0));
        String day = clean(getVal(1));
        if(day.isEmpty || day.toLowerCase().contains('day')){ skipped++; continue; } // skip header repeat
        if(!validDays.map((e)=>e.toLowerCase()).contains(day.toLowerCase())){ errors.add('Excel Row ${r+1}: invalid day $day'); skipped++; continue; }
        day = validDays.firstWhere((d)=>d.toLowerCase()==day.toLowerCase(), orElse: ()=>day);
        if(id.isEmpty) id = 'TT_${day}_${getVal(4)}_${getVal(5)}_${getVal(2)}_${r}'.replaceAll(' ', '_');
        toImport.add(Timetable(
          id: id.replaceAll('/', '_'),
          day: day,
          period: clean(getVal(2)).isEmpty? 'P$r': clean(getVal(2)),
          time: clean(getVal(3)).isEmpty? '08:00-08:40': clean(getVal(3)),
          cls: clean(getVal(4)).isEmpty? 'S.4': clean(getVal(4)),
          stream: clean(getVal(5)).isEmpty? 'Blue': clean(getVal(5)),
          subject: clean(getVal(6)),
          teacher: clean(getVal(7)),
          isBreak: clean(getVal(8)).toLowerCase()=='true' || clean(getVal(8))=='1',
        ));
      }
    }

    if(toImport.isEmpty) throw 'No valid rows. Errors: ${errors.take(5).join(', ')} | Skipped $skipped';

    // Confirm with summary
    bool? ok = await showDialog<bool>(context: context, builder: (c)=>AlertDialog(
      title: Text('Import ${toImport.length} periods?'),
      content: SingleChildScrollView(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children:[
        Text('File: ${file.name}'),
        Text('Valid: ${toImport.length}'),
        Text('Skipped: $skipped'),
        if(errors.isNotEmpty)...[SizedBox(height:8), Text('First errors:', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:11)),...errors.take(5).map((e)=>Text('- $e', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10, color: Colors.red)))],
        SizedBox(height:8),
        Text('This will ADD/UPDATE in Firebase. Continue?', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
      ])),
      actions: [TextButton(onPressed: ()=>Navigator.pop(c,false), child: Text('Cancel')), ElevatedButton(onPressed: ()=>Navigator.pop(c,true), child: Text('Import ${toImport.length}'))],
    ));
    if(ok!=true) return;

    // Use batch for speed (like your student import)
    showDialog(context: context, barrierDismissible: false, builder: (c)=>AlertDialog(title: Text('Importing ${toImport.length}...'), content: Row(children:[CircularProgressIndicator(), SizedBox(width:12), Text('Please wait...')])));

    int batchCount=0;
    WriteBatch batch = FirebaseFirestore.instance.batch();
    for(var t in toImport){
      batch.set(FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('timetable').doc(t.id), t.toMap());
      batchCount++;
      if(batchCount>=400){ await batch.commit(); batch = FirebaseFirestore.instance.batch(); batchCount=0; }
    }
    if(batchCount>0) await batch.commit();

    if(mounted) Navigator.pop(context);
    if(mounted) ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('✅ Imported ${toImport.length} | Skipped $skipped'), backgroundColor: Colors.green, duration: Duration(seconds:4)));

  } catch(e){
    try{ if(mounted) Navigator.pop(context); } catch(_){}
    if(mounted) ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('❌ Import failed: $e'), backgroundColor: Colors.red, duration: Duration(seconds:6)));
  }
}

  void addEdit({Timetable? edit}){
    var day=TextEditingController(text: edit?.day??'Monday'); var period=TextEditingController(text: edit?.period??'P1'); var time=TextEditingController(text: edit?.time??'08:00-08:40'); var sub=TextEditingController(text: edit?.subject??''); var teacher=TextEditingController(text: edit?.teacher??''); var cls=TextEditingController(text: edit?.cls??'S.4'); var stream=TextEditingController(text: edit?.stream??'Blue'); bool isBreak=edit?.isBreak??false;
    List<String> s14=['Blue','Red','Green','White']; List<String> s56=['Sciences','Arts'];
    showDialog(context: context, builder: (x)=>StatefulBuilder(builder: (c2,setS){
      List<String> curStreams = (cls.text=='S.5'||cls.text=='S.6')? s56 : s14;
      return AlertDialog(title: Text(edit==null?'Add Period':'Edit'), content: SingleChildScrollView(child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
        DropdownButtonFormField<String>(value: ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday'].contains(day.text)? day.text : 'Monday', decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Day', border: OutlineInputBorder()), items: ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text(e))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>day.text=v!),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextField(controller: period,decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Period', border: OutlineInputBorder())),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextField(controller: time,decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Time', border: OutlineInputBorder())),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextField(controller: sub,decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Subject', border: OutlineInputBorder())),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextField(controller: teacher,decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Teacher', border: OutlineInputBorder())),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextField(controller: cls,decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Class', border: OutlineInputBorder()), onChanged: (v)=>setS(()=>{})),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        DropdownButton<String>(value: curStreams.contains(stream.text)? stream.text : curStreams.first, isExpanded:true, items: curStreams.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Stream: $e'))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setS(()=>stream.text=v!)),
        CheckboxListTile(value: isBreak, onChanged: (v)=>setS(()=>isBreak=v!), title: Text('Is Break?'))
      ])), actions: [TextButton(onPressed: ()=>Navigator.pop(x), child: Text('Cancel')), ElevatedButton(onPressed: (){ var t=Timetable(id: edit?.id??DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch.toString(), day:day.text, period:period.text, cls:cls.text, stream:stream.text, subject:sub.text, teacher:teacher.text, time:time.text, isBreak:isBreak); widget.onSync(t); Navigator.pop(x); }, child: Text('Save'))]);
    }));
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c)=>Scaffold(
    appBar: AppBar(
      title: Text('Timetable - $filterCls $filterStream', style: TextStyle(fontSize:14)),
      backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1),
      actions: [
        IconButton(
          icon: Icon(Icons.grid_on, color: Colors.white),
          tooltip: 'Grid View',
          onPressed: ()=> Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_)=> TimetableGridPage(db: widget.db, curUser: widget.curUser))).then((_)=> setState((){})),
        ),
        PopupMenuButton<String>(
          onSelected: (v){
            if(v=='pdf') exportPDF();
            if(v=='template') downloadTemplate();
            if(v=='import') importFile();
          },
          itemBuilder: (ctx)=>[
            PopupMenuItem(value:'import', child: Row(children: [Icon(Icons.upload_file, size:18), SizedBox(width:8), Text('Import CSV/Excel')])),
            PopupMenuItem(value:'template', child: Row(children: [Icon(Icons.download, size:18), SizedBox(width:8), Text('Download Template')])),
            PopupMenuDivider(),
            PopupMenuItem(value:'pdf', child: Row(children: [Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf, size:18, color: Colors.red), SizedBox(width:8), Text('Export PDF (Per Class)')])),
          ]
        )
      ]
    ),
    floatingActionButton: widget.readOnly?null:FloatingActionButton(child: Icon(Icons.add), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), onPressed: ()=>addEdit()),
    body: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
      stream: widget.db.collection('timetable').snapshots(),
      builder: (ctx, snap){
        // Use LIVE data from Firebase, fallback to widget.timetable while loading
        List<Timetable> liveData = widget.timetable;
        if(snap.hasData){
          liveData = snap.data!.docs.map((d){
            try{
              var m = d.data() as Map;
              // Handle new group field safely
              return Timetable.fromMap(m);
            }catch(e){ return null; }
          }).whereType<Timetable>().toList();
        }

        // Apply your existing filter logic to LIVE data
        List<Timetable> liveFiltered = liveData;
        if(filterDay!='All') liveFiltered=liveFiltered.where((t)=>t.day==filterDay).toList();
        if(filterCls!='All') liveFiltered=liveFiltered.where((t)=>t.cls==filterCls).toList();
        if(filterStream!='All') liveFiltered=liveFiltered.where((t)=>t.stream==filterStream).toList();
        if(filterTime!='All') liveFiltered=liveFiltered.where((t)=>t.time.contains(filterTime)||t.period==filterTime).toList();
        if(searchQuery.isNotEmpty) liveFiltered=liveFiltered.where((t)=>t.subject.toLowerCase().contains(searchQuery.toLowerCase())||t.teacher.toLowerCase().contains(searchQuery.toLowerCase())).toList();

        return Column(children:[
          Card(margin: EdgeInsets.all(8), color: Colors.blue[50], child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Column(children:[
            Row(children:[
              Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterDay, isExpanded:true, items: days.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text(e, style:TextStyle(fontSize:11)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>filterDay=v!))),
              SizedBox(width:4),
              Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterCls, isExpanded:true, items: classes.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Class: $e', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11)))).toList(), onChanged: (v){ setState((){ filterCls=v!; if(!streams.contains(filterStream)) filterStream='All'; }); })),
              SizedBox(width:4),
              Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterStream, isExpanded:true, items: streams.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text('Stream: $e', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>filterStream=v!))),
            ]),
            SizedBox(height:4),
            Row(children:[
              Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterTime, isExpanded:true, items: times.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e,child:Text(e, style:TextStyle(fontSize:11)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>filterTime=v!))),
              SizedBox(width:4),
              Expanded(child: TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(hintText:'Search', prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search, size:16), border: OutlineInputBorder(), isDense:true, contentPadding: EdgeInsets.all(8)), style: TextStyle(fontSize:11), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>searchQuery=v))),
              SizedBox(width:4),
              ElevatedButton(onPressed: ()=>setState((){ filterDay='All'; filterCls='All'; filterStream='All'; filterTime='All'; searchQuery=''; }), child: Text('Reset', style:TextStyle(fontSize:10))),
            ]),
            SizedBox(height:6),
            SingleChildScrollView(scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal, child: Row(children:[
              ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.upload_file, size:16), label: Text('Import', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)), onPressed: importFile, style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.blue)),
              SizedBox(width:4),
              ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf, size:16), label: Text('PDF', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)), onPressed: exportPDF, style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.red)),
              SizedBox(width:4),
              OutlinedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.download, size:16), label: Text('Template', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)), onPressed: downloadTemplate),
            ])),
          ]))),
          Expanded(child: liveFiltered.isEmpty? Center(child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children:[Icon(Icons.search_off, size:40, color: Colors.grey), SizedBox(height:8), Text('No match', style:TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), Text('Live Total: ${liveData.length} | Filters: Day=$filterDay Class=$filterCls Stream=$filterStream', style:TextStyle(fontSize:10)), SizedBox(height:8), ElevatedButton(onPressed: ()=>setState((){ filterDay='All'; filterCls='All'; filterStream='All'; filterTime='All'; searchQuery=''; }), child: Text('Clear Filters'))])) : ListView.builder(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), itemCount: liveFiltered.length, itemBuilder: (c,i){ var t=liveFiltered[i]; return Card(color: t.isBreak?Colors.orange[50]:null, child: ListTile(dense:true, title: Text('${t.day} ${t.period} ${t.time} ${t.subject}', style:TextStyle(fontSize:12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), subtitle: Text('${t.cls} ${t.stream} ${t.teacher} ${t.group??''}', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11)), trailing: widget.readOnly?null:Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.edit,color: Colors.blue, size:18), onPressed: ()=>addEdit(edit:t)), IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.delete,color: Colors.red, size:18), onPressed: () async { bool ok = await confirmDelete(context, 'Timetable: ${t.subject} ${t.cls} ${t.stream} ${t.day} ${t.period}'); if(ok) widget.db.collection('timetable').doc(t.id).delete(); })]))); })),
        ]);
      }
    )
  );
}

// ================= LIBRARY TAB - BORROW / RETURN + OVERDUE REPORT =================
class LibraryPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final List<Student> studs;
  final AppUser curUser;
  final Function(LibraryLoan) onSync;
  LibraryPage({required this.studs, required this.curUser, required this.onSync});
  @override _LibraryPageState createState()=>_LibraryPageState();
}

class _LibraryPageState extends State<LibraryPage> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  MobileScannerController scannerController = MobileScannerController(detectionSpeed: DetectionSpeed.noDuplicates);
  bool isProcessing=false; String lastCode=''; String lastMsg='Ready - Scan student QR for Library Borrow/Return'; bool scanSuccess=false;
  String searchQuery=''; late TabController tabController;
  @override void initState(){ super.initState(); tabController=TabController(length:4, vsync:this); scannerController.start(); }
  @override void dispose(){ scannerController.dispose(); tabController.dispose(); super.dispose(); }
  String getTodayStr()=> DateTime.now().toString().split(' ')[0];

  Future<void> handleScan(String code) async {
    if(isProcessing) return; if(code==lastCode) return;
    setState(()=>isProcessing=true); lastCode=code;
    String id = code.split('|').length>1? code.split('|')[1].trim().toUpperCase() : code.trim().toUpperCase();
    var student = widget.studs.firstWhere((s)=> s.idNo.toUpperCase().trim()==id, orElse: ()=> Student(name:'Unknown',cls:'S.1',stream:'Blue',idNo:id,zones:{}));
    if(student.name=='Unknown'){ setState(()=>{lastMsg='❌ Unknown QR: $id', scanSuccess=false, isProcessing=false}); Future.delayed(Duration(seconds:2), ()=> setState(()=> lastCode='')); return; }
    var activeSnap = await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('library_loans').where('studentId', isEqualTo: student.idNo).where('returned', isEqualTo: false).get();
    List<LibraryLoan> active = activeSnap.docs.map((d)=>LibraryLoan.fromMap(d.data())).toList();
    if(active.isNotEmpty){ showReturnDialog(student, active); setState(()=>{lastMsg='📚 ${student.name} has ${active.length} active item(s) - Return?', scanSuccess=false, isProcessing=false}); Future.delayed(Duration(seconds:2), ()=> setState(()=> lastCode='')); return; }
    showBorrowDialog(student); setState(()=>{lastMsg='✅ ${student.name} - Ready to borrow', scanSuccess=true, isProcessing=false}); Future.delayed(Duration(seconds:2), ()=> setState(()=> lastCode=''));
  }

  void showBorrowDialog(Student student){
    TextEditingController itemsCtrl = TextEditingController(); int dueDays=7; TextEditingController notesCtrl = TextEditingController(); List<String> addedItems=[];
    showDialog(context: context, builder: (ctx)=>StatefulBuilder(builder: (ctx2,setS){
      return AlertDialog(
        title: Text('📚 Borrow - ${student.name} ${student.cls} ${student.stream}'),
        content: SingleChildScrollView(child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children:[
          Text('ID: ${student.idNo}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12, color: Colors.grey[700])),
          Text('Time Taken: ${DateTime.now().toString().substring(0,16)}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          TextField(controller: itemsCtrl, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Add Book/Item', hintText:'e.g. Math Textbook', border: OutlineInputBorder(), suffixIcon: IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.add), onPressed: (){ if(itemsCtrl.text.trim().isNotEmpty){ setS(()=> addedItems.add(itemsCtrl.text.trim())); itemsCtrl.clear(); }})), onSubmitted: (v){ if(v.trim().isNotEmpty){ setS(()=> addedItems.add(v.trim())); itemsCtrl.clear(); }}),
          SizedBox(height:6),
          if(addedItems.isNotEmpty) Wrap(spacing:6, runSpacing:6, children: addedItems.map((e)=> Chip(label: Text(e), deleteIcon: Icon(Icons.close, size:16), onDeleted: ()=> setS(()=> addedItems.remove(e)))).toList()),
          SizedBox(height:10),
          Text('Return in days:', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:12)),
          DropdownButton<int>(value: dueDays, isExpanded:true, items: [3,7,14,21,30].map((d)=> DropdownMenuItem(value:d, child: Text('$d days - Due ${DateTime.now().add(Duration(days:d)).toString().split(' ')[0]}'))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=> setS(()=> dueDays=v!)),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          TextField(controller: notesCtrl, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Notes (optional)', border: OutlineInputBorder())),
        ])),
        actions: [
          TextButton(onPressed: ()=> Navigator.pop(ctx2), child: Text('Cancel')),
          ElevatedButton(onPressed: () async {
            if(addedItems.isEmpty){ ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Add at least one item'))); return; }
            DateTime now=DateTime.now(); DateTime due=now.add(Duration(days:dueDays));
            var safeId = student.idNo.replaceAll('/', '_').replaceAll(' ', '_').replaceAll('#', '_'); var loan=LibraryLoan(id: '${safeId}_${now.millisecondsSinceEpoch}', studentId: student.idNo, studentName: student.name, cls: student.cls, stream: student.stream, items: List.from(addedItems), borrowDate: now.toString().split(' ')[0], dueDate: due.toString().split(' ')[0], borrowTimestamp: now, dueTimestamp: due, returned:false, teacher: widget.curUser.name, teacherEmail: widget.curUser.email, notes: notesCtrl.text);
            await widget.onSync(loan); Navigator.pop(ctx2);
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('✅ Borrowed ${addedItems.length} item(s) for ${student.name} - Due in $dueDays days'), backgroundColor: Colors.green));
          }, child: Text('Confirm Borrow')),
        ],
      );
    }));
  }

  void showReturnDialog(Student student, List<LibraryLoan> active){
    showDialog(context: context, builder: (ctx)=>AlertDialog(
      title: Text('📚 Return - ${student.name}'),
      content: SingleChildScrollView(child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children:[
        Text('${active.length} active loan(s) - Overdue red', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)), SizedBox(height:8),
       ...active.map((loan)=> Card(color: loan.isOverdue? Colors.red[50] : Colors.green[50], child: ListTile(
          title: Text(loan.items.join(', '), style: TextStyle(fontSize:12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          subtitle: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children:[ Text('Borrowed: ${loan.borrowDate} Due: ${loan.dueDate} by ${loan.teacher}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)), if(loan.isOverdue) Text('⚠️ OVERDUE ${loan.overdueDays} days!', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, color: Colors.red, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), ]),
          trailing: ElevatedButton(style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.green), onPressed: () async {
            var updated=LibraryLoan(id: loan.id, studentId: loan.studentId, studentName: loan.studentName, cls: loan.cls, stream: loan.stream, items: loan.items, borrowDate: loan.borrowDate, dueDate: loan.dueDate, borrowTimestamp: loan.borrowTimestamp, dueTimestamp: loan.dueTimestamp, returned:true, returnDate: DateTime.now().toString().split(' ')[0], returnTimestamp: DateTime.now(), teacher: loan.teacher, teacherEmail: loan.teacherEmail, notes: loan.notes);
            await widget.onSync(updated); Navigator.pop(ctx); ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('✅ Returned - ${loan.items.join(', ')}'), backgroundColor: Colors.green));
          }, child: Text('Return', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10))),
        ))).toList(),
      ])),
      actions: [
        TextButton(onPressed: ()=> Navigator.pop(ctx), child: Text('Close')),
        if(active.length>1) ElevatedButton(onPressed: () async { for(var loan in active){ var updated=LibraryLoan(id: loan.id, studentId: loan.studentId, studentName: loan.studentName, cls: loan.cls, stream: loan.stream, items: loan.items, borrowDate: loan.borrowDate, dueDate: loan.dueDate, borrowTimestamp: loan.borrowTimestamp, dueTimestamp: loan.dueTimestamp, returned:true, returnDate: DateTime.now().toString().split(' ')[0], returnTimestamp: DateTime.now(), teacher: loan.teacher, teacherEmail: loan.teacherEmail, notes: loan.notes); await widget.onSync(updated); } Navigator.pop(ctx); }, child: Text('Return All')),
      ],
    ));
  }

  Future<void> exportPDF(List<LibraryLoan> loans) async {
    final pdf = pw.Document();
    pdf.addPage(pw.MultiPage(build: (c)=>[
      pw.Text('Busalamu SS - Library Report ${getTodayStr()}', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold, fontSize:16)),
      pw.SizedBox(height:6),
      pw.Text('Total: ${loans.length} | Borrowed: ${loans.where((l)=>!l.returned).length} | Overdue: ${loans.where((l)=>l.isOverdue).length} | Returned: ${loans.where((l)=>l.returned).length}', style: pw.TextStyle(fontSize:10)),
      pw.Divider(),
      pw.Table.fromTextArray(headers: ['Student','Class','Items','Borrowed','Due','Status','Teacher','Overdue'], data: loans.map((l)=>[l.studentName, '${l.cls} ${l.stream}', l.items.join(', '), l.borrowDate, l.dueDate, l.returned? 'Returned' : (l.isOverdue? 'OVERDUE' : 'Borrowed'), l.teacher, l.isOverdue? '${l.overdueDays}d' : '']).toList(), headerStyle: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold, fontSize:8), cellStyle: pw.TextStyle(fontSize:7)),
    ]));
    await Printing.layoutPdf(onLayout: (f)=> pdf.save());
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Library - Borrow & Return'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1),
        bottom: TabBar(controller: tabController, isScrollable:true, tabs: [Tab(text:'Scan'), Tab(text:'Borrowed'), Tab(text:'Overdue'), Tab(text:'All')]),
        actions: [IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.refresh), onPressed: ()=> setState((){}))],
      ),
      body: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
        stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('library_loans').orderBy('ts', descending: true).snapshots(),
        builder: (ctx,snap){
          List<LibraryLoan> allLoans = snap.hasData? snap.data!.docs.map((d)=> LibraryLoan.fromMap(d.data() as Map<String,dynamic>)).toList() : [];
          List<LibraryLoan> filtered = allLoans.where((l){ if(searchQuery.isEmpty) return true; var q=searchQuery.toLowerCase(); return l.studentName.toLowerCase().contains(q) || l.studentId.toLowerCase().contains(q) || l.items.any((it)=> it.toLowerCase().contains(q)); }).toList();
          List<LibraryLoan> borrowed = filtered.where((l)=>!l.returned).toList();
          List<LibraryLoan> overdue = filtered.where((l)=> l.isOverdue).toList();
          return TabBarView(controller: tabController, children: [
            Column(children:[
              Container(width: double.infinity, color: Colors.blue[50], padding: EdgeInsets.all(10), child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children:[
                Row(children:[Icon(Icons.local_library, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1)), SizedBox(width:6), Text('Library Scan - ${widget.curUser.name}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))]),
                SizedBox(height:4), Text('Scan student QR: active loan => Return pop-up, no active => Borrow form. Time taken = Now.', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)),
                SizedBox(height:6), Container(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: scanSuccess? Colors.green[100] : Colors.orange[100], borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)), child: Row(children:[Icon(scanSuccess? Icons.check_circle : Icons.qr_code, color: scanSuccess? Colors.green : Colors.orange[800]), SizedBox(width:8), Expanded(child: Text(lastMsg, style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))) ])),
              ])),
              Expanded(flex:3, child: Container(margin: EdgeInsets.all(8), decoration: BoxDecoration(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12), border: Border.all(color: Colors.brown, width:2)), child: ClipRRect(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10), child: Stack(children:[
                MobileScanner(controller: scannerController, fit: BoxFit.cover, onDetect: (cap){ var codes=cap.barcodes; for(var b in codes){ if(b.rawValue!=null) handleScan(b.rawValue!); } }),
                Center(child: Container(width: 220, height: 220, decoration: BoxDecoration(border: Border.all(color: Colors.brown, width:4), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)))),
                Align(alignment: Alignment.bottomCenter, child: Container(margin: EdgeInsets.all(16), padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:12, vertical:8), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black54, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)), child: Text('📚 Scan student = Borrow/Return', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize:11)))),
              ])))),
              Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:12, vertical:4), child: Row(children:[ Expanded(child: TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(hintText:'Search student/book', isDense:true, border: OutlineInputBorder(), contentPadding: EdgeInsets.all(8), prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search, size:16)), style: TextStyle(fontSize:12), onChanged: (v)=> setState(()=> searchQuery=v))), SizedBox(width:6), Chip(label: Text('${allLoans.length} Total'), backgroundColor: Colors.brown[50]), ])),
              Expanded(flex:2, child: borrowed.isEmpty? Center(child: Text('No active borrows')) : ListView.builder(itemCount: borrowed.length>5?5:borrowed.length, itemBuilder: (_,i){ var l=borrowed[i]; return Card(margin: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:8, vertical:2), color: l.isOverdue? Colors.red[50] : Colors.orange[50], child: ListTile(dense:true, leading: Icon(l.isOverdue? Icons.warning : Icons.book, color: l.isOverdue? Colors.red : Colors.orange), title: Text('${l.studentName} ${l.cls} ${l.stream} - ${l.items.join(', ')}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), subtitle: Text('Due: ${l.dueDate} ${l.isOverdue? 'OVERDUE ${l.overdueDays}d' : ''}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)))); })),
            ]),
            Column(children:[ Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Row(children:[ Expanded(child: Text('Active Borrowed: ${borrowed.length}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))), ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf, size:14), label: Text('PDF', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)), onPressed: ()=> exportPDF(borrowed)), ])), Expanded(child: borrowed.isEmpty? Center(child: Text('No active')) : ListView.builder(itemCount: borrowed.length, itemBuilder: (_,i){ var l=borrowed[i]; return Card(color: l.isOverdue? Colors.red[50] : Colors.white, child: ListTile(title: Text('${l.studentName} (${l.studentId}) ${l.cls} ${l.stream}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), subtitle: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children:[Text('Items: ${l.items.join(', ')}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)), Text('Borrowed: ${l.borrowDate} Due: ${l.dueDate} Teacher: ${l.teacher}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)), if(l.isOverdue) Text('⚠️ OVERDUE ${l.overdueDays} days!', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:11))]))); })), ]),
            Column(children:[ Container(width: double.infinity, color: Colors.red[50], padding: EdgeInsets.all(10), child: Text('⚠️ Overdue: ${overdue.length}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.red))), Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Row(children:[ Expanded(child: Text('Overdue: ${overdue.length}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.red))), ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf, size:14), label: Text('PDF Overdue'), style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.red), onPressed: ()=> exportPDF(overdue)), ])), Expanded(child: overdue.isEmpty? Center(child: Text('No overdue')) : ListView.builder(itemCount: overdue.length, itemBuilder: (_,i){ var l=overdue[i]; return Card(color: Colors.red[50], child: ListTile(leading: Icon(Icons.warning, color: Colors.red), title: Text('${l.studentName} ${l.cls} ${l.stream} - ${l.items.join(', ')}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), subtitle: Text('Due: ${l.dueDate} Overdue: ${l.overdueDays} days', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)))); })), ]),
            Column(children:[ Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Row(children:[ Expanded(child: TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(hintText:'Search student/book/class', isDense:true, border: OutlineInputBorder(), prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search)), onChanged: (v)=> setState(()=> searchQuery=v))), SizedBox(width:6), ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf), label: Text('Export All'), onPressed: ()=> exportPDF(filtered)), ])), Expanded(child: filtered.isEmpty? Center(child: Text('No records')) : ListView.builder(itemCount: filtered.length, itemBuilder: (_,i){ var l=filtered[i]; return Card(color: l.returned? Colors.green[50] : (l.isOverdue? Colors.red[50] : Colors.white), child: ListTile(dense:true, leading: Icon(l.returned? Icons.check_circle : (l.isOverdue? Icons.warning : Icons.book), color: l.returned? Colors.green : (l.isOverdue? Colors.red : Colors.orange)), title: Text('${l.studentName} ${l.cls} ${l.stream} - ${l.items.join(', ')}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), subtitle: Text('${l.returned? 'Returned ${l.returnDate}' : 'Borrowed ${l.borrowDate} Due ${l.dueDate}'} | ${l.teacher}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)))); })), ]),
          ]);
        },
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(icon: Icon(Icons.qr_code_scanner), label: Text('Resume Scan'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), onPressed: (){ scannerController.start(); setState(()=> lastCode=''); }),
    );
  }
}

// ================= TEACHER LESSON UPDATE PAGE - SCAN STUDENT QR = TEACHER IN CLASS =================
class TeacherLessonPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final List<Student> studs;
  final List<Timetable> timetable;
  final List<ClassTeacher> teachers;
  final AppUser curUser;
  final Function(LessonLog) onSyncLesson;
  TeacherLessonPage({required this.studs, required this.timetable, required this.teachers, required this.curUser, required this.onSyncLesson});
  @override _TeacherLessonPageState createState()=>_TeacherLessonPageState();
}
class _TeacherLessonPageState extends State<TeacherLessonPage> {
  MobileScannerController scannerController = MobileScannerController(detectionSpeed: DetectionSpeed.noDuplicates);
  bool isProcessing=false;
  String lastCode='';
  String lastMsg='Ready - Scan ANY student QR in class to mark TEACHER IN CLASS';
  bool scanSuccess=false;
  List<LessonLog> todayMyScans=[];
  String selectedPeriod='Auto';
  String selectedClass='S.4';
  String selectedStream='Blue';
  List<String> periods=['Auto','P1','P2','P3','P4','P5','P6','P7','P8'];
  List<String> classes=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4','S.5','S.6'];
  List<String> get streams => (selectedClass=='S.5'||selectedClass=='S.6')? ['Sciences','Arts'] : ['Blue','Red','Green','White'];

  @override void initState(){ super.initState(); scannerController.start(); loadToday(); }
  @override void dispose(){ scannerController.dispose(); super.dispose(); }

  Future<void> loadToday() async {
    var snap = await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('lesson_logs')
    .where('teacherEmail', isEqualTo: widget.curUser.email)
    .where('date', isEqualTo: DateTime.now().toString().split(' ')[0]).get();
    setState(()=> todayMyScans = snap.docs.map((d)=>LessonLog.fromMap(d.data())).toList());
  }

  String getCurrentDay(){
    var days=['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday'];
    return days[DateTime.now().weekday-1];
  }

  String getCurrentPeriodByTime(){
    int total=DateTime.now().hour*60+DateTime.now().minute;
    if(total>=480 && total<520) return 'P1';
    if(total>=520 && total<560) return 'P2';
    if(total>=560 && total<600) return 'P3';
    if(total>=600 && total<640) return 'P4';
    if(total>=840 && total<880) return 'P5';
    if(total>=880 && total<920) return 'P6';
    if(total>=920 && total<960) return 'P7';
    return 'P1';
  }

  Future<void> handleScan(String code) async {
    if(isProcessing) return;
    if(code==lastCode) return;
    setState(()=>isProcessing=true);
    lastCode=code;
    String id = code.split('|').length>1? code.split('|')[1].trim().toUpperCase() : code.trim().toUpperCase();
    var student = widget.studs.firstWhere((s)=> s.idNo.toUpperCase().trim()==id,
      orElse: ()=> Student(name:'Unknown',cls:selectedClass,stream:selectedStream,idNo:id,zones:{}));
    if(student.name=='Unknown'){
      setState(()=>{lastMsg='❌ Unknown QR: $id', scanSuccess=false, isProcessing=false});
      Future.delayed(Duration(seconds:2), ()=> setState(()=> lastCode=''));
      return;
    }
    String day=getCurrentDay();
    String autoPeriod = getCurrentPeriodByTime();
    String periodToCheck = selectedPeriod=='Auto'? autoPeriod : selectedPeriod;
    String todayStr = DateTime.now().toString().split(' ')[0];
    DateTime now = DateTime.now();
    int nowMins = now.hour*60 + now.minute;
    var dayTT = widget.timetable.where((t)=> t.day==day && t.cls==student.cls && t.stream==student.stream &&!t.isBreak).toList();
    if(dayTT.isEmpty){
      setState(()=>{lastMsg='❌ No timetable for ${student.cls} ${student.stream} $day', scanSuccess=false, isProcessing=false});
      Future.delayed(Duration(seconds:2), ()=> setState(()=> lastCode=''));
      return;
    }
    Timetable? currentLesson;
    var periodMatches = dayTT.where((t)=> t.period==periodToCheck).toList();
    if(periodMatches.isEmpty){
      currentLesson = dayTT.firstWhere((t)=> _isNowInSlot(nowMins, t.time), orElse: ()=> dayTT.firstWhere((t)=> t.period==autoPeriod, orElse: ()=> dayTT.first));
    } else {
      currentLesson = periodMatches.first;
    }
    String actualPeriod = currentLesson.period;
    String timeSlot = currentLesson.time;
    String subject = currentLesson.subject;
    // TIME + DURATION CHECK
    if(!_isNowInSlot(nowMins, timeSlot)){
      var parsed = _parseTimeRange(timeSlot);
      if(parsed!=null){
        int s = parsed[0]; int e = parsed[1];
        if(nowMins < s-10 || nowMins > e+15){
          setState(()=>{lastMsg='⏰ Time Mismatch! Now ${now.hour}:${now.minute.toString().padLeft(2,'0')} not in $actualPeriod ($timeSlot). Wait for period.', scanSuccess=false, isProcessing=false});
          Future.delayed(Duration(seconds:3), ()=> setState(()=> lastCode=''));
          return;
        }
      }
    }
    // ONE SCAN PER TEACHER PER PERIOD
    var existingTeacherPeriod = await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('lesson_logs').where('teacherEmail', isEqualTo: widget.curUser.email).where('date', isEqualTo: todayStr).where('cls', isEqualTo: student.cls).where('stream', isEqualTo: student.stream).where('period', isEqualTo: actualPeriod).limit(1).get();
    if(existingTeacherPeriod.docs.isNotEmpty){
      var ex = existingTeacherPeriod.docs.first.data();
      setState(()=>{lastMsg='⚠️ You already scanned ${student.cls} ${student.stream} $actualPeriod at ${ex['timeSlot']??''} - ${ex['scannedStudentName']??''}. One scan per period only!', scanSuccess=false, isProcessing=false});
      Future.delayed(Duration(seconds:2), ()=> setState(()=> lastCode=''));
      return;
    }
    // TWO TEACHERS CANNOT SCAN SAME PERIOD UNLESS OPTIONAL
    var existingClassPeriod = await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('lesson_logs').where('date', isEqualTo: todayStr).where('cls', isEqualTo: student.cls).where('stream', isEqualTo: student.stream).where('period', isEqualTo: actualPeriod).get();
    if(existingClassPeriod.docs.isNotEmpty){
      var sameSlotTT = dayTT.where((t)=> t.period==actualPeriod).toList();
      bool isOptionalSlot = sameSlotTT.length>1 && sameSlotTT.map((e)=>e.subject).toSet().length>1;
      if(!isOptionalSlot){
        var other = existingClassPeriod.docs.first.data();
        String otherTeacher = other['teacher']??'Another teacher';
        if(other['teacherEmail']!=widget.curUser.email){
          setState(()=>{lastMsg='🚫 $actualPeriod ${student.cls} ${student.stream} already taught by $otherTeacher (${other['subject']}). Duplicate blocked!', scanSuccess=false, isProcessing=false});
          Future.delayed(Duration(seconds:3), ()=> setState(()=> lastCode=''));
          return;
        }
      } else {
        bool sameSubjectAlready = existingClassPeriod.docs.any((d)=> (d.data() as Map)['subject']==subject);
        if(sameSubjectAlready){
          setState(()=>{lastMsg='🚫 Subject $subject already scanned for $actualPeriod ${student.cls} ${student.stream}. Optional: different subject allowed only.', scanSuccess=false, isProcessing=false});
          Future.delayed(Duration(seconds:2), ()=> setState(()=> lastCode=''));
          return;
        }
      }
    }
    var lessonLog = LessonLog(
      id: DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch.toString(),
      date: todayStr,
      day: day, period: actualPeriod, timeSlot: timeSlot,
      cls: student.cls, stream: student.stream,
