      subject: subject, teacher: widget.curUser.name, teacherEmail: widget.curUser.email,
      scannedStudentId: student.idNo, scannedStudentName: student.name,
      timestamp: DateTime.now(), status: 'TEACHER_IN_CLASS'
    );
    await widget.onSyncLesson(lessonLog);
    await loadToday();
    setState((){
      lastMsg='✅ ${student.name} (${student.cls} ${student.stream}) - $subject $actualPeriod $timeSlot - TEACHER IN CLASS';
      scanSuccess=true; isProcessing=false;
    });
    Future.delayed(Duration(seconds:3), ()=> setState(()=> lastCode=''));
  }

  List<int>? _parseTimeRange(String timeStr){
    try{
      var clean = timeStr.replaceAll(' ', '');
      if(!clean.contains('-')){
        var parts = clean.split(':');
        if(parts.length>=2){
          int h=int.parse(parts[0]); int m=int.parse(parts[1].replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^0-9]'), ''));
          int start=h*60+m; return [start, start+40];
        }
        return null;
      }
      var split = clean.split('-');
      String s1 = split[0]; String s2 = split[1];
      int parseOne(String s){
        var ap = s.toLowerCase();
        bool pm = ap.contains('pm');
        bool am = ap.contains('am');
        s = s.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^0-9:]'), '');
        var p = s.split(':');
        int h=int.parse(p[0]); int mm=p.length>1? int.parse(p[1]):0;
        if(pm && h<12) h+=12;
        if(am && h==12) h=0;
        return h*60+mm;
      }
      return [parseOne(s1), parseOne(s2)];
    }catch(e){ return null; }
  }

  bool _isNowInSlot(int nowMins, String timeSlot){
    var range = _parseTimeRange(timeSlot);
    if(range==null) return true;
    return nowMins>=range[0]-10 && nowMins<=range[1]+15;
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Lesson Update - Scan Student'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1),
        actions: [IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.refresh), onPressed: loadToday)]),
      body: Column(children: [
        Container(width: double.infinity, color: Colors.blue[50], padding: EdgeInsets.all(10), child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
          Row(children: [Icon(Icons.menu_book, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1)), SizedBox(width:6), Text('Teacher: ${widget.curUser.name}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))]),
          SizedBox(height:6),
          Text('Scan ANY student QR in class => marks TEACHER IN CLASS. If not scanned, HT sees Missing.', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          Row(children: [
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: selectedClass, isExpanded:true, items: classes.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e, child: Text('Class $e', style:TextStyle(fontSize:12)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>selectedClass=v!))),
            SizedBox(width:6),
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: streams.contains(selectedStream)? selectedStream : streams.first, isExpanded:true, items: streams.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e, child: Text('Stream $e', style:TextStyle(fontSize:12)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>selectedStream=v!))),
            SizedBox(width:6),
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: selectedPeriod, isExpanded:true, items: periods.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e, child: Text(e, style:TextStyle(fontSize:12)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>selectedPeriod=v!))),
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:6),
          Container(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: scanSuccess? Colors.green[100] : Colors.orange[100], borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
            child: Row(children: [Icon(scanSuccess? Icons.check_circle : Icons.qr_code, color: scanSuccess? Colors.green : Colors.orange[800]), SizedBox(width:8), Expanded(child: Text(lastMsg, style: TextStyle(fontSize:12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))) ]))
        ])),
        // ===== GREEN GRID FIX - SAME AS GATE CAM =====
        Expanded(flex: 3, child: Container(margin: EdgeInsets.all(8), decoration: BoxDecoration(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12), border: Border.all(color: Colors.green, width:2)), child: ClipRRect(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10), child: Stack(children: [
          MobileScanner(controller: scannerController, fit: BoxFit.cover, onDetect: (cap){ var codes=cap.barcodes; for(var b in codes){ if(b.rawValue!=null) handleScan(b.rawValue!); } }),
          Center(child: Container(width: 220, height: 220, decoration: BoxDecoration(border: Border.all(color: scanSuccess? Colors.green : Colors.green, width:4), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)))),
          if(isProcessing) Center(child: Container(padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:16,vertical:8), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black54, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)), child: Text('SCANNED! NEXT...',style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white,fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)))),
          Align(alignment: Alignment.bottomCenter, child: Container(margin: EdgeInsets.all(16), padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:12, vertical:8), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black54, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)), child: Text('📚 Scan student QR = Lesson Taught', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize:11)))),
        ])))),
        Expanded(flex: 2, child: Column(children: [
          Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children: [Text('Today My Scans (${todayMyScans.length})', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), Text(getCurrentDay(), style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)) ])),
          Expanded(child: todayMyScans.isEmpty? Center(child: Text('No scans today - Scan first student')) : ListView.builder(itemCount: todayMyScans.length, itemBuilder: (c,i){
            var l=todayMyScans[i];
            return Card(margin: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:8, vertical:2), color: Colors.green[50], child: ListTile(dense:true, leading: Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: Colors.green), title: Text('${l.cls} ${l.stream} ${l.subject} ${l.period}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), subtitle: Text('${l.scannedStudentName} at ${l.timestamp.hour}:${l.timestamp.minute.toString().padLeft(2,'0')} - ${l.date}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)), trailing: Text('IN CLASS', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10, color: Colors.green, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))));
          }))
        ]))
      ]),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(icon: Icon(Icons.qr_code_scanner), label: Text('Resume Scan'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), onPressed: (){ scannerController.start(); setState(()=>lastCode=''); }),
    );
  }
}

// ================= HT LESSON REPORT - TAUGHT vs MISSING =================
class HTLessonReportPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final List<LessonLog> lessonLogs;
  final List<Timetable> timetable;
  final List<ClassTeacher> teachers;
  HTLessonReportPage({required this.lessonLogs, required this.timetable, required this.teachers});
  @override _HTLessonReportPageState createState()=>_HTLessonReportPageState();
}
class _HTLessonReportPageState extends State<HTLessonReportPage> {
  String filterClass='All'; String filterStream='All'; String filterTeacher='All'; String filterDay='All'; String searchQuery='';
  DateTime selectedDate=DateTime.now();
  // DYNAMIC from Timetable Tab - Boss requested
  List<String> get classes {
    var set = <String>{}; for(var t in widget.timetable){ if(t.cls.trim().isNotEmpty) set.add(t.cls.trim()); }
    var list = set.toList()..sort(); return ['All', ...list.isEmpty? ['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4','S.5','S.6'] : list];
  }
  List<String> get days {
    var set = <String>{}; for(var t in widget.timetable){ if(t.day.trim().isNotEmpty) set.add(t.day.trim()); }
    var list = set.toList(); var order = ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday'];
    list.sort((a,b)=> order.indexOf(a).compareTo(order.indexOf(b)));
    return ['All', ...list.isEmpty? ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday'] : list];
  }
  List<String> get streams { 
    var set = <String>{}; 
    for(var t in widget.timetable){ 
      if(filterClass!='All' && t.cls!=filterClass) continue;
      if(t.stream.trim().isNotEmpty) set.add(t.stream.trim()); 
    } 
    var list = set.toList()..sort(); 
    return ['All', ...list.isEmpty? (filterClass=='S.5'||filterClass=='S.6'? ['Sciences','Arts'] : ['Blue','Red','Green','White']) : list]; 
  }
  List<String> get teacherList {
    var set = <String>{}; for(var t in widget.timetable){ if(t.teacher.trim().isNotEmpty) set.add(t.teacher.trim()); }
    var list = set.toList()..sort(); return ['All', ...list];
  }

  List<LessonLog> get filtered {
    var l=widget.lessonLogs;
    if(filterClass!='All') l=l.where((e)=>e.cls==filterClass).toList();
    if(filterStream!='All') l=l.where((e)=>e.stream==filterStream).toList();
    if(filterTeacher!='All') l=l.where((e)=>e.teacher==filterTeacher).toList();
    if(filterDay!='All') l=l.where((e)=>e.day==filterDay).toList();
    String dateStr = selectedDate.toString().split(' ')[0];
    l=l.where((e)=> e.date==dateStr).toList();
    if(searchQuery.isNotEmpty) l=l.where((e)=> e.subject.toLowerCase().contains(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) || e.teacher.toLowerCase().contains(searchQuery.toLowerCase())).toList();
    return l;
  }

  List<Timetable> get missingLessons {
    String dayName = ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday'][selectedDate.weekday-1];
    var todaysTT = widget.timetable.where((t)=> t.day==dayName &&!t.isBreak).toList();
    if(filterClass!='All') todaysTT=todaysTT.where((t)=> t.cls==filterClass).toList();
    if(filterStream!='All') todaysTT=todaysTT.where((t)=> t.stream==filterStream).toList();
    if(filterTeacher!='All') todaysTT=todaysTT.where((t)=> t.teacher==filterTeacher).toList();
    if(filterDay!='All') todaysTT=todaysTT.where((t)=> t.day==filterDay).toList();
    if(searchQuery.isNotEmpty){
      var q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      todaysTT=todaysTT.where((t)=> t.subject.toLowerCase().contains(q) || t.teacher.toLowerCase().contains(q) || t.cls.toLowerCase().contains(q)).toList();
    }
    List<Timetable> missing=[];
    for(var tt in todaysTT){
      bool hasLog = widget.lessonLogs.any((log)=> log.date==selectedDate.toString().split(' ')[0] && log.cls==tt.cls && log.stream==tt.stream && log.period==tt.period);
      if(!hasLog) missing.add(tt);
    }
    return missing;
  }

  Future<void> exportPDF() async {
    final pdf = pw.Document();
    pdf.addPage(pw.MultiPage(build: (c)=>[
      pw.Text('Busalamu SS - Lesson Report ${selectedDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}', style: pw.TextStyle(fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold, fontSize:16)),
      pw.SizedBox(height:8),
      pw.Text('Taught: ${filtered.length} | Missing: ${missingLessons.length}', style: pw.TextStyle(fontSize:10)),
      pw.Divider(),
      pw.Text('LESSONS TAUGHT (Teacher scanned student QR)'),
      pw.Table.fromTextArray(headers: ['Time','Class','Stream','Subject','Teacher','Student','Status'], data: filtered.map((l)=>[l.timeSlot, l.cls, l.stream, l.subject, l.teacher, l.scannedStudentName, l.status]).toList()),
      pw.SizedBox(height:16),
      pw.Text('LESSONS MISSING (No scan = Teacher not in class)', style: pw.TextStyle(color: PdfColors.red)),
      pw.Table.fromTextArray(headers: ['Day','Period','Time','Class','Stream','Subject','Teacher'], data: missingLessons.map((t)=>[t.day, t.period, t.time, t.cls, t.stream, t.subject, t.teacher]).toList()),
    ]));
    await Printing.layoutPdf(onLayout: (f)=> pdf.save());
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    String dayName = ['Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday','Sunday'][selectedDate.weekday-1];
    return DefaultTabController(length: 2, child: Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Lessons - HT Report'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1),
        bottom: TabBar(tabs: [Tab(text:'Taught ${filtered.length}', icon: Icon(Icons.check_circle)), Tab(text:'Missing ${missingLessons.length}', icon: Icon(Icons.warning))]),
        actions: [IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf), onPressed: exportPDF), IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.calendar_today), onPressed: () async { var d=await showDatePicker(context: context, initialDate: selectedDate, firstDate: DateTime(2025), lastDate: DateTime(2027)); if(d!=null) setState(()=>selectedDate=d); })]
      ),
      body: Column(children: [
        Card(margin: EdgeInsets.all(8), color: Colors.blue[50], child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Column(children: [
          Row(children: [Text('Date: ${selectedDate.toString().split(' ')[0]} ($dayName)', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:12)), Spacer(), ElevatedButton(onPressed: ()=>setState(()=>{filterClass='All', filterStream='All', filterTeacher='All', filterDay='All', searchQuery='', selectedDate=DateTime.now()}), child: Text('Reset', style:TextStyle(fontSize:10)))]),
          SizedBox(height:6),
          Row(children: [
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterClass, isExpanded:true, items: classes.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e, child: Text('Class: $e', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>filterClass=v!))),
            SizedBox(width:4),
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterStream, isExpanded:true, items: streams.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e, child: Text('Stream: $e', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>filterStream=v!))),
            SizedBox(width:4),
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: filterDay, isExpanded:true, items: days.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e, child: Text('Day: $e', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11)))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>filterDay=v!))),
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:4),
          Row(children: [
            Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: teacherList.contains(filterTeacher)? filterTeacher : 'All', isExpanded:true, items: teacherList.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e, child: Text(e.length>15? e.substring(0,15) : e, style:TextStyle(fontSize:11), overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>filterTeacher=v!))),
            SizedBox(width:4),
            Expanded(child: TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(hintText:'Search subject/teacher', isDense:true, border: OutlineInputBorder(), contentPadding: EdgeInsets.all(8), prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search, size:16)), style: TextStyle(fontSize:11), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>searchQuery=v))),
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:6),
          Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround, children: [
            Chip(label: Text('Taught: ${filtered.length}', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11)), backgroundColor: Colors.green[100]),
            Chip(label: Text('Missing: ${missingLessons.length}', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11, color: Colors.white)), backgroundColor: Colors.red),
            Chip(label: Text('TT Today: ${widget.timetable.where((t)=> t.day==dayName &&!t.isBreak).length}', style:TextStyle(fontSize:11)), backgroundColor: Colors.blue[100]),
          ])
        ]))),
        Expanded(child: TabBarView(children: [
          filtered.isEmpty? Center(child: Text('No lessons taught for ${selectedDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}\nTeacher must scan student QR', textAlign: TextAlign.center)) : ListView.builder(itemCount: filtered.length, itemBuilder: (c,i){
            var l=filtered[i];
            return Card(color: Colors.green[50], child: ListTile(dense:true, leading: CircleAvatar(backgroundColor: Colors.green, child: Icon(Icons.check, color: Colors.white, size:16)), title: Text('${l.cls} ${l.stream} - ${l.subject} ${l.period} @ ${l.timeSlot}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:12)), subtitle: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [Text('Teacher: ${l.teacher} | Student: ${l.scannedStudentName} (${l.scannedStudentId})', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)), Text('${l.day} ${l.date} ${l.timestamp.hour}:${l.timestamp.minute.toString().padLeft(2,'0')} - ${l.status}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:9, color: Colors.green[800]))]), trailing: Icon(Icons.verified, color: Colors.green)));
          }),
          missingLessons.isEmpty? Center(child: Column(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children: [Icon(Icons.celebration, size:60, color: Colors.green), Text('All lessons taught! 🎉', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))])) : ListView.builder(itemCount: missingLessons.length, itemBuilder: (c,i){
            var t=missingLessons[i];
            return Card(color: Colors.red[50], child: ListTile(dense:true, leading: CircleAvatar(backgroundColor: Colors.red, child: Icon(Icons.close, color: Colors.white, size:16)), title: Text('${t.cls} ${t.stream} - ${t.subject} ${t.period} @ ${t.time}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:12)), subtitle: Text('Teacher: ${t.teacher} | Day: ${t.day} | Class ${t.cls} ${t.stream}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)), trailing: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [Text('MISSING', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:10)), Text('No Scan', style: TextStyle(fontSize:9))])));
          }),
        ]))
      ]),
    ));
  }
}

class Setup extends StatefulWidget {
  final List<Student> studs;
  final Function(int,int) onGrantSingle;
  final Function(List<int>,int) onGrantMulti;
  Setup({required this.studs, required this.onGrantSingle, required this.onGrantMulti});
  @override _SetupState createState()=>_SetupState();
}

class _SetupState extends State<Setup> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  List<int> selected=[];
  DateTime from=DateTime.now();
  DateTime to=DateTime.now().add(Duration(days:7));
  String searchSetup='';
  String selClass='All';
  String selZone='All';
  List<String> selectedZones=['GATE','CLASS','MESS'];
  late TabController _tab;
  TermSettings? currentTerm;
  DateTime? termEndDate;
  bool termLoading=true;
  bool termExpired=false;
  int get rawDaysCount => to.difference(from).inDays+1;
  int get remainingDaysToEnd {
    if(termEndDate==null) return 9999;
    int diff = termEndDate!.difference(DateTime.now()).inDays;
    if(diff <0) return 0;
    return diff+1;
  }
  int get daysCount {
    int raw = rawDaysCount;
    int rem = remainingDaysToEnd;
    if(rem==9999) return raw;
    if(raw > rem) return rem;
    return raw;
  }
  int effectiveDays(int requested){
    int rem = remainingDaysToEnd;
    if(rem==9999) return requested;
    if(rem <=0) return 0;
    return requested > rem? rem : requested;
  }
  @override void initState(){
    _tab=TabController(length: 2, vsync: this);
    super.initState();
    loadTerm();
  }
  Future<void> loadTerm() async {
    try{
      var doc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('settings').doc('currentTerm').get();
      if(doc.exists){
        var t = TermSettings.fromMap(doc.data()!);
        setState((){
          currentTerm=t;
          termEndDate=t.endDate;
          termLoading=false;
          termExpired=DateTime.now().isAfter(t.endDate);
        });
        if(termExpired){
          WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_)=> _autoResetIfExpired());
        }
        if(termEndDate!=null && to.isAfter(termEndDate!)){
          setState(()=> to=termEndDate!);
        }
      } else {
        setState(()=> termLoading=false);
      }
    }catch(e){
      setState(()=> termLoading=false);
    }
  }
  Future<void> _autoResetIfExpired() async {
    if(termEndDate==null) return;
    if(!DateTime.now().isAfter(termEndDate!)) return;
    bool needsReset = widget.studs.any((s)=> (s.zones['GATE']?.left??0)>0 || (s.zones['CLASS']?.left??0)>0 || (s.zones['MESS']?.left??0)>0);
    if(!needsReset) return;
    bool? confirm = await showDialog<bool>(context: context, builder: (ctx)=> AlertDialog(
      title: Row(children:[Icon(Icons.warning, color: Colors.red), SizedBox(width:8), Text('Term Ended')]),
      content: Text('End of Term date (${termEndDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}) has passed. All Gate, Class and Mess days will be reset to 0.'),
      actions: [TextButton(onPressed: ()=>Navigator.pop(ctx,false), child: Text('Cancel')), ElevatedButton(style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.red), onPressed: ()=>Navigator.pop(ctx,true), child: Text('RESET ALL TO 0'))],
    ));
    if(confirm==true){
      await resetAllToZero();
    }
  }
  Future<void> resetAllToZero() async {
    for(var s in widget.studs){
      s.zones['GATE']=ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(), days:0);
      s.zones['CLASS']=ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(), days:0);
      s.zones['MESS']=ZoneAccess(issued:DateTime.now(), days:0);
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('students').doc(s.safeDocId).update({
        'zones': s.zones.map((k,v)=>MapEntry(k,v.toMap()))
      });
    }
    if(mounted) ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Term ended - All access reset to 0'), backgroundColor: Colors.red));
    setState((){});
  }
  List<Student> get defaulters {
    var l = widget.studs.where((s)=>
      (s.zones['GATE']?.left??0) <= 2 ||
      (s.zones['CLASS']?.left??0) <= 2 ||
      (s.zones['MESS']?.left??0) <= 2
    ).toList();
    if(selClass!='All') l=l.where((s)=>s.cls==selClass).toList();
    if(selZone!='All') l=l.where((s)=>(s.zones[selZone]?.left??0)<=2).toList();
    if(searchSetup.isNotEmpty) l=l.where((s)=>s.name.toLowerCase().contains(searchSetup.toLowerCase())||s.idNo.toLowerCase().contains(searchSetup.toLowerCase())||s.cls.toLowerCase().contains(searchSetup.toLowerCase())).toList();
    return l;
  }
  List<Student> get allFiltered {
    var l = List<Student>.from(widget.studs);
    if(selClass!='All') l=l.where((s)=>s.cls==selClass).toList();
    if(searchSetup.isNotEmpty) l=l.where((s)=>s.name.toLowerCase().contains(searchSetup.toLowerCase())||s.idNo.toLowerCase().contains(searchSetup.toLowerCase())).toList();
    return l;
  }
  Widget zoneChip(String z, int left){
    Color c = left<=0?Colors.red: left<=3?Colors.orange: Colors.green;
    return Container(padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:6,vertical:2), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: c.withOpacity(0.15), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), border: Border.all(color: c)), child: Text('$z: ${left}d', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: c)));
  }
  void _grantWithTermCheck(int realIndex, int requestedDays){
    int eff = effectiveDays(requestedDays);
    if(eff<=0 && remainingDaysToEnd==0){
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Term has ended - Cannot grant. Reset to 0'), backgroundColor: Colors.red));
      widget.onGrantSingle(realIndex, 0);
      return;
    }
    widget.onGrantSingle(realIndex, eff);
    if(eff < requestedDays){
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Capped to $eff days - Term ends ${termEndDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}'), backgroundColor: Colors.orange));
    }
  }
  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Access Control'),backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1),
        bottom: TabBar(controller: _tab, tabs: [Tab(text: 'Defaulters (${defaulters.length})'), Tab(text: 'All Students')])
      ),
      body: Column(children: [
        if(termLoading) LinearProgressIndicator(),
        if(currentTerm!=null) Card(
          color: termExpired? Colors.red[50] : Colors.amber[50],
          margin: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(8,8,8,0),
          child: ListTile(
            dense: true,
            leading: Icon(termExpired? Icons.event_busy : Icons.event, color: termExpired? Colors.red : Colors.orange.shade800),
            title: Text('${currentTerm!.termName} | End: ${termEndDate.toString().split(' ')[0]}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:12)),
            subtitle: Text(termExpired? 'TERM ENDED - Access reset to 0' : 'Remaining: $remainingDaysToEnd days | Grant will not exceed end date', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, color: termExpired? Colors.red : Colors.black87)),
            trailing: termExpired? ElevatedButton(style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.red), onPressed: resetAllToZero, child: Text('RESET ALL 0', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10))) : null,
          ),
        ),
        Card(color: Colors.blue[50], margin: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
          Row(children:[
            Icon(Icons.shield, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1)), SizedBox(width:6),
            Text('Grant Access', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:16, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1))),
            Spacer(),
            Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end, children:[
              Text('$daysCount Days', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:16, color: remainingDaysToEnd==0? Colors.red : Colors.green[700])),
              if(rawDaysCount!= daysCount) Text('(capped from $rawDaysCount)', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10, color: Colors.orange)),
            ])
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          Wrap(spacing:6, children: ['GATE','CLASS','MESS'].map((z){
            bool sel = selectedZones.contains(z);
            return FilterChip(label: Text(z), selected: sel, onSelected: (v){ setState((){ if(v) selectedZones.add(z); else selectedZones.remove(z); }); }, selectedColor: Colors.green[200]);
          }).toList()),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          Row(children: [
            Expanded(child: OutlinedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.calendar_today, size:16), label: Text('From ${from.toString().split(' ')[0]}'), onPressed: () async { var d=await showDatePicker(context: context, initialDate: from, firstDate: DateTime(2025), lastDate: termEndDate?? DateTime(3000)); if(d!=null) setState(()=>from=d!); })),
            SizedBox(width:6),
            Expanded(child: OutlinedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.event, size:16), label: Text('To ${to.toString().split(' ')[0]}'), onPressed: () async { var d=await showDatePicker(context: context, initialDate: to, firstDate: from, lastDate: termEndDate?? DateTime(3000)); if(d!=null){ if(termEndDate!=null && d.isAfter(termEndDate!)) d=termEndDate!; setState(()=>to=d!);} })),
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          Row(children:[
            Expanded(child: ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.select_all), label: Text('Select All ${ _tab.index==0? defaulters.length : allFiltered.length}'), onPressed: (){
              setState((){
                var list = _tab.index==0? defaulters : allFiltered;
                selected = list.map((s)=> widget.studs.indexOf(s)).toList();
              });
            })),
            SizedBox(width:8),
            Expanded(child: ElevatedButton.icon(style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: termExpired? Colors.grey : Colors.green), icon: Icon(Icons.check_circle), label: Text('Grant ${selected.length}'), onPressed: selected.isEmpty?null:(){
              if(termExpired && remainingDaysToEnd==0){
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Term ended - Cannot grant beyond end date'), backgroundColor: Colors.red));
                return;
              }
              for(int idx in selected){
                for(String z in selectedZones){
                  widget.studs[idx].zones[z] = ZoneAccess(issued: from, days: daysCount);
                  FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('students').doc(widget.studs[idx].safeDocId).update({
                    'zones': widget.studs[idx].zones.map((k,v)=>MapEntry(k,v.toMap()))
                  });
                }
              }
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Granted $daysCount days (capped to term end ${termEndDate?.toString().split(' ')[0]})'), backgroundColor: Colors.green));
              setState(()=>selected=[]);
            })),
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:4),
          Row(children: [
            TextButton(onPressed: (){ setState((){ from=DateTime.now(); var nd=DateTime.now().add(Duration(days:1)); if(termEndDate!=null && nd.isAfter(termEndDate!)) nd=termEndDate!; to=nd; }); }, child: Text('1 Day')),
            TextButton(onPressed: (){ setState((){ from=DateTime.now(); var nd=DateTime.now().add(Duration(days:7)); if(termEndDate!=null && nd.isAfter(termEndDate!)) nd=termEndDate!; to=nd; }); }, child: Text('Week')),
            TextButton(onPressed: (){ setState((){ from=DateTime.now(); var nd=DateTime.now().add(Duration(days:30)); if(termEndDate!=null && nd.isAfter(termEndDate!)) nd=termEndDate!; to=nd; }); }, child: Text('Month')),
            TextButton(onPressed: (){ setState((){ from=DateTime.now(); var nd=termEndDate?? DateTime.now().add(Duration(days:90)); to=nd; }); }, child: Text('Term (${remainingDaysToEnd}d)')),
          ])
        ]))),
        Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:8), child: Row(children:[
          Expanded(child: TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search), hintText: 'Search', border: OutlineInputBorder(), isDense: true), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>searchSetup=v))),
          SizedBox(width:6),
          DropdownButton<String>(value: selClass, items: ['All','S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4','S.5','S.6'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e, child: Text(e))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>selClass=v!)),
          SizedBox(width:6),
          DropdownButton<String>(value: selZone, items: ['All','GATE','CLASS','MESS'].map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e, child: Text(e))).toList(), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>selZone=v!)),
        ])),
        Expanded(child: TabBarView(controller: _tab, children: [
          ListView.builder(itemCount: defaulters.length, itemBuilder: (c,i){
            var s=defaulters[i]; int real=widget.studs.indexOf(s); bool isSel=selected.contains(real);
            return Card(margin: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:8,vertical:3), child: CheckboxListTile(value: isSel, onChanged: (v){ setState((){ if(v==true) selected.add(real); else selected.remove(real); }); },
              title: Text(s.name, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), subtitle: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                Text('${s.cls} ${s.stream} | ${s.idNo}'),
                SizedBox(height:4), Row(children: [zoneChip('GATE', s.zones['GATE']?.left??0), SizedBox(width:4), zoneChip('CLASS', s.zones['CLASS']?.left??0), SizedBox(width:4), zoneChip('MESS', s.zones['MESS']?.left??0)])
              ]),
              secondary: PopupMenuButton<String>(onSelected: (v){
                int d=v=='day'?1:v=='week'?7:v=='month'?30: remainingDaysToEnd;
                _grantWithTermCheck(real,d);
              }, itemBuilder: (c)=>[
                PopupMenuItem(value:'day',child: Text('Grant 1 Day (max ${effectiveDays(1)}d)')),
                PopupMenuItem(value:'week',child: Text('Grant Week (max ${effectiveDays(7)}d)')),
                PopupMenuItem(value:'month',child: Text('Grant Month (max ${effectiveDays(30)}d)')),
                PopupMenuItem(value:'term',child: Text('Grant Term (${remainingDaysToEnd}d left)'))
              ]),
            ));
          }),
          ListView.builder(itemCount: allFiltered.length, itemBuilder: (c,i){
            var s=allFiltered[i]; int real=widget.studs.indexOf(s); bool isSel=selected.contains(real);
            return Card(margin: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:8,vertical:3), child: CheckboxListTile(value: isSel, onChanged: (v){ setState((){ if(v==true) selected.add(real); else selected.remove(real); }); },
              title: Text(s.name), subtitle: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                Text('${s.cls} ${s.stream} | ${s.idNo}'),
                SizedBox(height:4), Row(children: [zoneChip('GATE', s.zones['GATE']?.left??0), SizedBox(width:4), zoneChip('CLASS', s.zones['CLASS']?.left??0), SizedBox(width:4), zoneChip('MESS', s.zones['MESS']?.left??0)])
              ]),
              secondary: IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.edit, color: Colors.blue), onPressed: (){ _grantWithTermCheck(real, effectiveDays(7)); }),
            ));
          }),
        ]))
      ]));
  }
}

class AttendanceConfig {
  String htOverrideCode; String defaultTeacherPin;
  double schoolLat; double schoolLng; double radiusM;
  int lateHour; int lateMinute;
  
  AttendanceConfig({
    this.htOverrideCode='BSS2025',
    this.defaultTeacherPin='1234',
    this.schoolLat=0.0640,
    this.schoolLng=32.0705,
    this.radiusM=300,
    this.lateHour=8,
    this.lateMinute=0
  });
  
  Map<String,dynamic> toMap()=> {
    'htOverrideCode':htOverrideCode,
    'defaultTeacherPin':defaultTeacherPin,
    'schoolLat':schoolLat,
    'schoolLng':schoolLng,
    'radiusM':radiusM,
    'lateHour':lateHour,
    'lateMinute':lateMinute,
    'updatedAt':DateTime.now().toIso8601String()
  };
  
  static AttendanceConfig fromMap(Map m)=> AttendanceConfig(
    htOverrideCode:m['htOverrideCode']??'BSS2025',
    defaultTeacherPin:m['defaultTeacherPin']??'1234',
    schoolLat:(m['schoolLat']??0.0640).toDouble(),
    schoolLng:(m['schoolLng']??32.0705).toDouble(),
    radiusM:(m['radiusM']??300).toDouble(),
    lateHour: (m['lateHour']??8) is int ? m['lateHour'] : int.tryParse(m['lateHour'].toString())??8,
    lateMinute: (m['lateMinute']??0) is int ? m['lateMinute'] : int.tryParse(m['lateMinute'].toString())??0
  );

  String get lateTimeStr {
    int h12 = lateHour % 12;
    if(h12==0) h12=12;
    String ampm = lateHour>=12 ? 'PM' : 'AM';
    return '${h12.toString()}:${lateMinute.toString().padLeft(2,'0')} $ampm';
  }
  
  TimeOfDay get lateTimeOfDay => TimeOfDay(hour: lateHour, minute: lateMinute);
}

class ClassAccessConfig {
  bool enforceTime; bool blockOutside;
  String globalOpen; String globalClose;
  Map<String,String> classOpen;
  Map<String,String> classClose;
  Map<String,List<String>> classStreams;
  Map<String,bool> classEnabled;
  ClassAccessConfig({
    this.enforceTime=true, this.blockOutside=true,
    this.globalOpen='07:00', this.globalClose='18:00',
    Map<String,String>? classOpen, Map<String,String>? classClose,
    Map<String,List<String>>? classStreams, Map<String,bool>? classEnabled,
  }):
    classOpen=classOpen??{'S.1':'07:00','S.2':'07:00','S.3':'07:00','S.4':'07:00','S.5':'07:00','S.6':'07:00'},
    classClose=classClose??{'S.1':'17:00','S.2':'17:00','S.3':'17:00','S.4':'18:00','S.5':'18:00','S.6':'18:00'},
    classStreams=classStreams??{
      'S.1':['Blue','Red','Green','White'],
      'S.2':['Blue','Red','Green','White'],
      'S.3':['Blue','Red','Green','White'],
      'S.4':['Blue','Red','Green','White'],
      'S.5':['Sciences','Arts'],
      'S.6':['Sciences','Arts'],
    },
    classEnabled=classEnabled??{'S.1':true,'S.2':true,'S.3':true,'S.4':true,'S.5':true,'S.6':true};
  Map<String,dynamic> toMap()=> {
    'enforceTime':enforceTime,'blockOutside':blockOutside,
    'globalOpen':globalOpen,'globalClose':globalClose,
    'classOpen':classOpen,'classClose':classClose,
    'classStreams':classStreams,'classEnabled':classEnabled,
    'updatedAt':DateTime.now().toIso8601String()
  };
  static ClassAccessConfig fromMap(Map m){
    Map<String,String> co={}; Map<String,String> cc={}; Map<String,List<String>> cs={}; Map<String,bool> ce={};
    if(m['classOpen']!=null) (m['classOpen'] as Map).forEach((k,v){ co[k.toString()]=v.toString(); });
    if(m['classClose']!=null) (m['classClose'] as Map).forEach((k,v){ cc[k.toString()]=v.toString(); });
    if(m['classStreams']!=null) (m['classStreams'] as Map).forEach((k,v){ cs[k.toString()]=List<String>.from(v); });
    if(m['classEnabled']!=null) (m['classEnabled'] as Map).forEach((k,v){ ce[k.toString()]=v as bool; });
    var def=ClassAccessConfig();
    return ClassAccessConfig(
      enforceTime:m['enforceTime']??true, blockOutside:m['blockOutside']??true,
      globalOpen:m['globalOpen']??'07:00', globalClose:m['globalClose']??'18:00',
      classOpen: co.isEmpty? def.classOpen : co, classClose: cc.isEmpty? def.classClose : cc,
      classStreams: cs.isEmpty? def.classStreams : cs, classEnabled: ce.isEmpty? def.classEnabled : ce,
    );
  }
  bool isTimeAllowedForClass(String cls, String stream, DateTime now){
    if(!enforceTime) return true;
    if(!(classEnabled[cls]??true)) return false;
    var allowed=classStreams[cls];
    if(allowed!=null &&!allowed.contains(stream)) return false;
    int curMin = now.hour*60 + now.minute;
    int gOpen = _parseMin(globalOpen); int gClose = _parseMin(globalClose);
    if(blockOutside && (curMin<gOpen || curMin>gClose)) return false;
    int cOpen = _parseMin(classOpen[cls]??globalOpen);
    int cClose = _parseMin(classClose[cls]??globalClose);
    return curMin>=cOpen && curMin<=cClose;
  }
  static int _parseMin(String t){ try{ var p=t.split(':'); return int.parse(p[0])*60 + int.parse(p[1]); }catch(_){ return 0; } }
  String timeReason(String cls, String stream){ return 'School $globalOpen-$globalClose | $cls ${classEnabled[cls]==false? "DISABLED" : "${classOpen[cls]}-${classClose[cls]}"} Streams:${(classStreams[cls]??[]).join(",")}'; }
}

// === SETTINGS PAGE ===
class AttendanceSettingsPage extends StatefulWidget { @override _AttendanceSettingsPageState createState()=>_AttendanceSettingsPageState(); }
class _AttendanceSettingsPageState extends State<AttendanceSettingsPage> {
  bool loading=true;
  final overrideC=TextEditingController(); final defaultPinC=TextEditingController();
  final latC=TextEditingController(); final lngC=TextEditingController(); final radiusC=TextEditingController();
  String curOverride='BSS2025'; String curPin='1234';
  TimeOfDay lateTime = TimeOfDay(hour: 8, minute: 0); // NEW
  @override void initState(){ super.initState(); loadConfig(); }
  Future<void> loadConfig() async {
    var doc = await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('app_settings').doc('attendance_config').get();
    if(doc.exists){
      var cfg=AttendanceConfig.fromMap(doc.data()!);
      setState((){
        curOverride=cfg.htOverrideCode; curPin=cfg.defaultTeacherPin;
        overrideC.text=cfg.htOverrideCode; defaultPinC.text=cfg.defaultTeacherPin;
        latC.text=cfg.schoolLat.toString(); lngC.text=cfg.schoolLng.toString(); radiusC.text=cfg.radiusM.toString();
        lateTime = cfg.lateTimeOfDay;
        loading=false;
      });
    } else { setState(()=>loading=false); overrideC.text=curOverride; defaultPinC.text=curPin; latC.text='0.0640'; lngC.text='32.0705'; radiusC.text='300'; }
  }
  Future<void> saveConfig() async {
    var cfg=AttendanceConfig(
      htOverrideCode: overrideC.text.trim(),
      defaultTeacherPin: defaultPinC.text.trim(),
      schoolLat: double.tryParse(latC.text)??0.0640,
      schoolLng: double.tryParse(lngC.text)??32.0705,
      radiusM: double.tryParse(radiusC.text)??300,
      lateHour: lateTime.hour,
      lateMinute: lateTime.minute,
    );
    await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('app_settings').doc('attendance_config').set(cfg.toMap());
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('✅ Saved Override:${cfg.htOverrideCode} PIN:${cfg.defaultTeacherPin} Late:${cfg.lateTimeStr} GPS:${cfg.schoolLat},${cfg.schoolLng}')));
    setState((){ curOverride=cfg.htOverrideCode; curPin=cfg.defaultTeacherPin; });
  }
    Future<void> pickLateTime() async {
    var t = await showTimePicker(context: context, initialTime: lateTime);
    if(t!=null) setState(()=> lateTime = t);
  }
  Future<void> useCurrentLocationAsSchool() async {
    bool service=await Geolocator.isLocationServiceEnabled(); if(!service){ ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Turn on GPS'))); return; }
    LocationPermission p=await Geolocator.checkPermission(); if(p==LocationPermission.denied){ p=await Geolocator.requestPermission(); }
    var pos=await Geolocator.getCurrentPosition(desiredAccuracy: LocationAccuracy.high);
    setState(()=>{ latC.text=pos.latitude.toString(), lngC.text=pos.longitude.toString() });
  }
  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    return Scaffold(appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Attendance Settings'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1)),
      body: loading? Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()) : ListView(padding: EdgeInsets.all(16), children: [
        Card(child: ListTile(title: Text('HT Override'), subtitle: Text(curOverride, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.red, fontSize:20)))),
        Card(child: ListTile(title: Text('Default PIN'), subtitle: Text(curPin, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:20)))),
        TextField(controller: overrideC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'NEW HT Override Code', border: OutlineInputBorder())),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextField(controller: defaultPinC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'NEW Default PIN', border: OutlineInputBorder())),
        Divider(height:30),
        Text('GPS Geofence', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        Row(children: [Expanded(child: TextField(controller: latC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'School Lat', border: OutlineInputBorder()))), SizedBox(width:8), Expanded(child: TextField(controller: lngC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'School Lng', border: OutlineInputBorder())))]),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextField(controller: radiusC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Radius Meters', border: OutlineInputBorder())),
        SizedBox(height:12),
        Card(
          color: Colors.orange[50],
          child: ListTile(
            leading: Icon(Icons.access_time, color: Colors.orange[800]),
            title: Text('Late Coming Time'),
            subtitle: Text('${lateTime.hour.toString().padLeft(2,'0')}:${lateTime.minute.toString().padLeft(2,'0')} ${lateTime.hour>=12?'PM':'AM'}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:18)),
            trailing: ElevatedButton(onPressed: pickLateTime, child: Text('SET TIME')),
          ),
        ),
        TextButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.my_location), label: Text('Use My Current Location as School'), onPressed: useCurrentLocationAsSchool),
        SizedBox(height:20),
        ElevatedButton(onPressed: saveConfig, child: Text('UPDATE NOW'), style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.green.shade800, minimumSize: Size(double.infinity, 50))),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// === CLASS ACCESS TIME SETTINGS - NEW ===
class ClassAccessSettingsPage extends StatefulWidget { @override _ClassAccessSettingsPageState createState()=>_ClassAccessSettingsPageState(); }
class _ClassAccessSettingsPageState extends State<ClassAccessSettingsPage> {
  bool loading=true;
  ClassAccessConfig config=ClassAccessConfig();
  List<String> classes=['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4','S.5','S.6'];

  @override void initState(){ super.initState(); loadConfig(); }

  Future<void> loadConfig() async {
    var doc=await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('app_settings').doc('class_access_config').get();
    if(doc.exists){ config=ClassAccessConfig.fromMap(doc.data()!); }
    setState(()=>loading=false);
  }

  Future<void> saveConfig() async {
    await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('app_settings').doc('class_access_config').set(config.toMap());
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('✅ Class Access Saved'), backgroundColor: Colors.green));
  }

  Future<void> pickTime(bool isGlobal, bool isOpen, String? cls) async {
    String curStr = isGlobal? (isOpen? config.globalOpen : config.globalClose) : (isOpen? (config.classOpen[cls]??'07:00') : (config.classClose[cls]??'18:00'));
    var parts=curStr.split(':');
    TimeOfDay init=TimeOfDay(hour:int.tryParse(parts[0])??7, minute:int.tryParse(parts[1])??0);
    var t=await showTimePicker(context: context, initialTime: init);
    if(t!=null){
      String fmt='${t.hour.toString().padLeft(2,'0')}:${t.minute.toString().padLeft(2,'0')}';
      setState((){
        if(isGlobal){ if(isOpen) config.globalOpen=fmt; else config.globalClose=fmt; }
        else { if(isOpen) config.classOpen[cls!]=fmt; else config.classClose[cls!]=fmt; }
      });
    }
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Class Access Time Settings'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), actions:[IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.save), onPressed: saveConfig)]),
      body: loading? Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()) : ListView(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), children:[
        Card(color: Colors.blue[50], child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children:[
          Text('Global School Access', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:16)),
          SwitchListTile(title: Text('Enforce Time Restriction'), subtitle: Text('If OFF, any time allowed'), value: config.enforceTime, onChanged:(v)=>setState(()=>config.enforceTime=v)),
          SwitchListTile(title: Text('Block Outside Global Hours'), value: config.blockOutside, onChanged:(v)=>setState(()=>config.blockOutside=v)),
          Row(children:[
            Expanded(child: ListTile(title: Text('Open'), subtitle: Text(config.globalOpen, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:18)), onTap:()=>pickTime(true,true,null), trailing: Icon(Icons.access_time))),
            Expanded(child: ListTile(title: Text('Close'), subtitle: Text(config.globalClose, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:18)), onTap:()=>pickTime(true,false,null), trailing: Icon(Icons.access_time))),
          ]),
        ]))),
        SizedBox(height:10),
        Text('Per Class & Stream Validity', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
       ...classes.map((cls){
          bool enabled=config.classEnabled[cls]??true;
          List<String> allowed=config.classStreams[cls]??[];
          return Card(child: ExpansionTile(
            title: Text('$cls ${enabled? "✅":"❌"} - ${config.classOpen[cls]}-${config.classClose[cls]}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:13)),
            subtitle: Text('Streams: ${allowed.join(", ")}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)),
            children:[
              SwitchListTile(title: Text('Enable $cls'), value: enabled, onChanged:(v)=>setState(()=>config.classEnabled[cls!]=v)),
              Row(children:[
                Expanded(child: ListTile(dense:true, title: Text('Open'), subtitle: Text(config.classOpen[cls]??'07:00'), onTap:()=>pickTime(false,true,cls))),
                Expanded(child: ListTile(dense:true, title: Text('Close'), subtitle: Text(config.classClose[cls]??'18:00'), onTap:()=>pickTime(false,false,cls))),
              ]),
              Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Wrap(spacing:6, children: ['Blue','Red','Green','White','Sciences','Arts'].map((st){
                bool sel=allowed.contains(st);
                return FilterChip(label: Text(st, style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)), selected: sel, onSelected:(v){ setState((){ if(v) allowed.add(st); else allowed.remove(st); config.classStreams[cls]=allowed; }); });
              }).toList())),
            ],
          ));
        }).toList(),
        SizedBox(height:15),
        ElevatedButton(onPressed: saveConfig, child: Text('SAVE ALL SETTINGS'), style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.green.shade800, minimumSize: Size(double.infinity,50))),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        Text('Gate CAM CLASS scan will check: zone expiry + class enabled + stream allowed + time window.', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, color: Colors.grey)),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// === STUDENT PROMOTION SYSTEM - END OF YEAR ===
class StudentPromotionPage extends StatefulWidget { @override _StudentPromotionPageState createState()=>_StudentPromotionPageState(); }
class _StudentPromotionPageState extends State<StudentPromotionPage> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  bool loading=true;
  List<Student> allStudents=[];
  List<Student> oldStudents=[];
  late TabController tabController;
  String currentAcademicYear=DateTime.now().year.toString();
  String nextAcademicYear=(DateTime.now().year+1).toString();
  bool isPromoting=false;
  String searchQuery='';
  String filterClass='All';
  List<String> classes=['All','S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4','S.5','S.6'];
  @override void initState(){ super.initState(); tabController=TabController(length:2, vsync:this); loadData(); }
  Future<void> loadData() async {
    setState(()=>loading=true);
    try{
      var snap=await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('students').get();
      allStudents=snap.docs.map((d)=>Student.fromMap(d.data())).toList();
      var oldSnap=await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('old_students').get();
      oldStudents=oldSnap.docs.map((d)=>Student.fromMap(d.data())).toList();
      var oldInMain=allStudents.where((s)=>s.isOldStudent || s.status!='active').toList();
      for(var s in oldInMain){ if(!oldStudents.any((o)=>o.idNo==s.idNo)) oldStudents.add(s); }
      allStudents=allStudents.where((s)=>s.isActive).toList();
    }catch(e){ print(e); }
    setState(()=>loading=false);
  }
  Map<String,int> get countByClass { Map<String,int> m={}; for(var c in ['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4','S.5','S.6']){ m[c]=allStudents.where((s)=>s.cls==c).length; } return m; }
  Future<void> promoteAll() async {
    bool? confirm=await showDialog<bool>(context: context, builder:(ctx)=>AlertDialog(
      title: Text('PROMOTE ALL STUDENTS?'),
      content: Text('This will:\n\n• S.1 → S.2\n• S.2 → S.3\n• S.3 → S.4\n• S.4 → OLD (Leavers $currentAcademicYear)\n• S.5 → S.6\n• S.6 → OLD (Graduated $currentAcademicYear)\n\nIDs kept. Continue?\n\n$currentAcademicYear → $nextAcademicYear'),
      actions:[TextButton(onPressed:()=>Navigator.pop(ctx,false), child: Text('Cancel')), ElevatedButton(onPressed:()=>Navigator.pop(ctx,true), child: Text('YES PROMOTE'), style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.green))],
    ));
    if(confirm!=true) return;
    setState(()=>isPromoting=true);
    int promoted=0, archived=0;
    try{
      WriteBatch batch=FirebaseFirestore.instance.batch();
      int batchCount=0;
      for(var s in allStudents){
        String prev=s.cls; String newCls=''; bool toOld=false; String newStatus='active';
        if(s.cls=='S.1') newCls='S.2'; else if(s.cls=='S.2') newCls='S.3'; else if(s.cls=='S.3') newCls='S.4'; else if(s.cls=='S.4'){ toOld=true; newStatus='leaver'; } else if(s.cls=='S.5') newCls='S.6'; else if(s.cls=='S.6'){ toOld=true; newStatus='graduated'; }
        String history=s.promotionHistory.isEmpty? '${s.cls}($currentAcademicYear)' : '${s.promotionHistory} -> ${s.cls}($currentAcademicYear)';
        if(toOld){
          Student oldStu=Student(name:s.name, cls:s.cls, stream:s.stream, idNo:s.idNo, parentPhone:s.parentPhone, gender:s.gender, studentType:s.studentType, hostelBlock:s.hostelBlock, zones:s.zones, status:newStatus, prevCls:prev, academicYear:currentAcademicYear, graduatedYear:currentAcademicYear, promotionHistory:history, isOldStudent:true);
          var ref=FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('old_students').doc(oldStu.safeDocId);
          batch.set(ref, oldStu.toMap());
          var mainRef=FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('students').doc(s.safeDocId);
          batch.update(mainRef, {'status':newStatus, 'prevCls':prev, 'graduatedYear':currentAcademicYear, 'promotionHistory':history, 'isOldStudent':true});
          archived++;
        } else {
          var mainRef=FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('students').doc(s.safeDocId);
          batch.update(mainRef, {'cls':newCls, 'prevCls':prev, 'academicYear':nextAcademicYear, 'promotionHistory':history, 'status':'active', 'isOldStudent':false});
          promoted++;
        }
        batchCount++; if(batchCount>=400){ await batch.commit(); batch=FirebaseFirestore.instance.batch(); batchCount=0; }
      }
      if(batchCount>0) await batch.commit();
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('promotion_logs').add({'date':DateTime.now().toIso8601String(), 'fromYear':currentAcademicYear, 'toYear':nextAcademicYear, 'promoted':promoted, 'archived':archived, 'by':'HT'});
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('✅ Promoted $promoted, Archived $archived'), backgroundColor: Colors.green));
      await loadData();
    }catch(e){ ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Error: $e'), backgroundColor: Colors.red)); }
    setState(()=>isPromoting=false);
  }
  Future<void> promoteSingleClass(String fromCls) async {
    String toCls=''; bool toOld=false;
    if(fromCls=='S.1') toCls='S.2'; else if(fromCls=='S.2') toCls='S.3'; else if(fromCls=='S.3') toCls='S.4'; else if(fromCls=='S.4') toOld=true; else if(fromCls=='S.5') toCls='S.6'; else if(fromCls=='S.6') toOld=true;
    var list=allStudents.where((s)=>s.cls==fromCls).toList();
    if(list.isEmpty){ ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('No students in $fromCls'))); return; }
    bool? confirm=await showDialog<bool>(context: context, builder:(ctx)=>AlertDialog(title: Text('Promote $fromCls?'), content: Text(toOld? '$fromCls (${list.length}) → OLD' : '$fromCls (${list.length}) → $toCls'), actions:[TextButton(onPressed:()=>Navigator.pop(ctx,false), child: Text('Cancel')), ElevatedButton(onPressed:()=>Navigator.pop(ctx,true), child: Text('Promote'))]));
    if(confirm!=true) return;
    setState(()=>isPromoting=true);
    WriteBatch batch=FirebaseFirestore.instance.batch();
    for(var s in list){
      String history=s.promotionHistory.isEmpty? '${s.cls}($currentAcademicYear)' : '${s.promotionHistory} -> ${s.cls}($currentAcademicYear)';
      if(toOld){
        String st=s.cls=='S.4'?'leaver':'graduated';
        Student oldStu=Student(name:s.name, cls:s.cls, stream:s.stream, idNo:s.idNo, parentPhone:s.parentPhone, gender:s.gender, studentType:s.studentType, hostelBlock:s.hostelBlock, zones:s.zones, status:st, prevCls:s.cls, academicYear:currentAcademicYear, graduatedYear:currentAcademicYear, promotionHistory:history, isOldStudent:true);
        batch.set(FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('old_students').doc(oldStu.safeDocId), oldStu.toMap());
        batch.update(FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('students').doc(s.safeDocId), {'status':st, 'prevCls':s.cls, 'graduatedYear':currentAcademicYear, 'promotionHistory':history, 'isOldStudent':true});
      } else {
        batch.update(FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('students').doc(s.safeDocId), {'cls':toCls, 'prevCls':s.cls, 'academicYear':nextAcademicYear, 'promotionHistory':history});
      }
    }
    await batch.commit();
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('✅ $fromCls promoted'))); await loadData(); setState(()=>isPromoting=false);
  }
  Future<void> restoreStudent(Student s) async {
    await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('students').doc(s.safeDocId).update({'isOldStudent':false, 'status':'active', 'academicYear':nextAcademicYear});
    await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('old_students').doc(s.safeDocId).update({'isOldStudent':false, 'status':'active'});
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Restored ${s.name}'))); await loadData();
  }
  List<Student> get filteredActive { var list=allStudents; if(filterClass!='All') list=list.where((s)=>s.cls==filterClass).toList(); if(searchQuery.isNotEmpty) list=list.where((s)=>s.name.toLowerCase().contains(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) || s.idNo.toLowerCase().contains(searchQuery.toLowerCase())).toList(); return list; }
  List<Student> get filteredOld { var list=oldStudents; if(filterClass!='All') list=list.where((s)=>s.cls==filterClass).toList(); if(searchQuery.isNotEmpty) list=list.where((s)=>s.name.toLowerCase().contains(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) || s.idNo.toLowerCase().contains(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) || s.graduatedYear.contains(searchQuery)).toList(); return list; }
  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    var counts=countByClass;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Promotion $currentAcademicYear → $nextAcademicYear'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), bottom: TabBar(controller: tabController, tabs:[Tab(text:'ACTIVE (${allStudents.length})'), Tab(text:'OLD (${oldStudents.length})')]), actions:[IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.refresh), onPressed: loadData)]),
      body: loading? Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()) : Column(children:[
        if(isPromoting) LinearProgressIndicator(color: Colors.green),
        Container(color: Colors.amber[100], padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Column(children:[
          Row(children:[
            Expanded(child: TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: 'Current Year', border: OutlineInputBorder()), controller: TextEditingController(text: currentAcademicYear), onChanged:(v){ currentAcademicYear=v; nextAcademicYear=(int.tryParse(v)??2026+1).toString(); setState((){}); })),
            SizedBox(width:8),
            Expanded(child: TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: 'Next Year', border: OutlineInputBorder()), controller: TextEditingController(text: nextAcademicYear), onChanged:(v){ nextAcademicYear=v; setState((){}); })),
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          SingleChildScrollView(scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal, child: Row(children: counts.entries.map((e)=>Container(margin: EdgeInsets.only(right:6), padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:10,vertical:6), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.white, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20), border: Border.all(color: Colors.blue)), child: Text('${e.key}: ${e.value}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:12)))).toList())),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          Row(children:[ Expanded(child: ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.upgrade), label: Text('PROMOTE ALL - END OF YEAR'), onPressed: isPromoting? null : promoteAll, style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.green.shade700, minimumSize: Size(double.infinity,45))))]),
        ])),
        Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Row(children:[
          Expanded(child: TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search), hintText: 'Search name/ID/year', border: OutlineInputBorder(), isDense:true), onChanged:(v)=>setState(()=>searchQuery=v))),
          SizedBox(width:8),
          DropdownButton<String>(value: filterClass, items: classes.map((e)=>DropdownMenuItem(value:e, child: Text(e))).toList(), onChanged:(v)=>setState(()=>filterClass=v!)),
        ])),
        Expanded(child: TabBarView(controller: tabController, children:[
          ListView(children:[
           ...['S.1','S.2','S.3','S.4','S.5','S.6'].map((cls){
              var list=filteredActive.where((s)=>s.cls==cls).toList();
              if(filterClass!='All' && filterClass!=cls) return SizedBox.shrink();
              return Card(child: ExpansionTile(title: Text('$cls - ${list.length}'), subtitle: Text('Promote $cls → ${cls=='S.1'?'S.2':cls=='S.2'?'S.3':cls=='S.3'?'S.4':cls=='S.4'?'OLD':cls=='S.5'?'S.6':'OLD'}'), trailing: ElevatedButton(onPressed:()=>promoteSingleClass(cls), child: Text('Promote')), children: list.take(20).map((s)=>ListTile(dense:true, title: Text('${s.name} - ${s.stream} - ${s.idNo}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12)), subtitle: Text('Hist: ${s.promotionHistory.isEmpty?'New':s.promotionHistory}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)))).toList()));
            }).toList(),
          ]),
          ListView.builder(itemCount: filteredOld.length, itemBuilder:(ctx,i){
            var s=filteredOld[i];
            return Card(color: s.status=='graduated'? Colors.green[50] : Colors.orange[50], child: ListTile(
              title: Text('${s.name} - ${s.cls} ${s.stream}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:13)),
              subtitle: Text('ID: ${s.idNo} | ${s.status.toUpperCase()} ${s.graduatedYear} | Hist: ${s.promotionHistory}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)),
              trailing: IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.restore, color: Colors.green), onPressed:()=>restoreStudent(s)),
            ));
          }),
        ])),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// === ENROLLMENT - FIXED WITH ALL FIELDS ===
class TeacherEnrollmentPage extends StatefulWidget { @override _TeacherEnrollmentPageState createState()=>_TeacherEnrollmentPageState(); }
class _TeacherEnrollmentPageState extends State<TeacherEnrollmentPage> {
  final _formKey=GlobalKey<FormState>();
  String name='', email='', phone='', gender='Male', staffId='', nationalId='', role='Subject Teacher', department='Science', pin='1234';
  List<String> subjects=[], classesAssigned=[];
  bool isEnrolling=false; String defaultPinFromSettings='1234';

  List<String> genders=['Male','Female'];
  List<String> roles=['Subject Teacher','HOD','Head of Department','Class Teacher','Lab Technician','Head Teacher','Deputy Head Teacher'];
  List<String> departments=['Science','Arts','Sports','Library','Administration','Boarding','ICT','General'];

  @override void initState(){ super.initState(); loadDefaultPin(); }
  Future<void> loadDefaultPin() async {
    var doc=await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('app_settings').doc('attendance_config').get();
    if(doc.exists){
      setState(()=>defaultPinFromSettings=doc.data()!['defaultTeacherPin']??'1234');
      pin=defaultPinFromSettings;
    }
  }

  Future<void> saveTeacher() async {
    if(!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
    _formKey.currentState!.save();
    setState(()=>isEnrolling=true);
    try{
      String cleanStaffId = staffId.trim().replaceAll('/', '_').replaceAll(' ', '_');
      if(cleanStaffId.isEmpty) cleanStaffId = 'BSS_T_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}';
      
      var profile=TeacherProfile(
        staffId: staffId.trim(), // keep original for display, but docId is clean
        name: name.trim(),
        email: email.toLowerCase().trim(),
        phone: phone.trim(),
        gender: gender,
        nationalId: nationalId.trim(),
        role: role,
        department: department,
        subjects: subjects,
        classesAssigned: classesAssigned,
        pinCode: pin.isEmpty? defaultPinFromSettings : pin,
        dateJoined: DateTime.now()
      );
      
      // Use clean docId - no slash
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('teacher_profiles').doc(cleanStaffId).set(profile.toMap());
      
      // Also use clean doc for users collection (email as doc id is okay)
      await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('users').doc(email.toLowerCase().trim()).set({
        'email':email.toLowerCase().trim(),
        'name':name.trim(),
        'role': role.toUpperCase().contains('HEAD TEACHER') || role=='HT' ? 'HT' : 'TEACHER',
        'password':'bss123',
        'mustChange':true,
        'department': department,
        'staffId': staffId.trim(),
        'cleanStaffId': cleanStaffId,
        'phone': phone.trim(),
        'gender': gender,
        'nationalId': nationalId.trim(),
        'actualRole': role,
      }, SetOptions(merge: true));
      
      if(!mounted) return;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('✅ $name enrolled! $role - $department | PIN:${profile.pinCode}'), backgroundColor: Colors.green));
      _formKey.currentState!.reset();
      setState((){
        gender='Male'; role='Subject Teacher'; department='Science';
        subjects=[]; classesAssigned=[]; phone=''; name=''; email=''; staffId=''; nationalId='';
      });
    }catch(e){
      print('Enroll error: $e');
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('❌ Failed: $e'), backgroundColor: Colors.red));
    }finally{
      if(mounted) setState(()=>isEnrolling=false);
    }
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('Enroll Teacher - PIN: $defaultPinFromSettings'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1)),
      body: Form(key: _formKey, child: ListView(padding: EdgeInsets.all(16), children: [
        TextFormField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Full Name *', border: OutlineInputBorder()), onSaved: (v)=>name=v!.trim(), validator: (v)=>v!.isEmpty?'Required':null),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextFormField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Staff ID * BSS/T/012', border: OutlineInputBorder()), onSaved: (v)=>staffId=v!.trim(), validator: (v)=>v!.isEmpty?'Required':null),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextFormField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Email * same as login', border: OutlineInputBorder()), onSaved: (v)=>email=v!.trim(), validator: (v)=>v!.isEmpty?'Required':null),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextFormField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Phone * 07...', border: OutlineInputBorder(), prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.phone)), onSaved: (v)=>phone=v!.trim(), validator: (v)=>v!.isEmpty?'Required':null),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
          value: gender,
          items: genders.map((g)=> DropdownMenuItem(value:g, child: Text(g))).toList(),
          onChanged:(v)=>setState(()=>gender=v!),
          onSaved:(v)=>gender=v??'Male',
          decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Gender *', border: OutlineInputBorder(), prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.person)),
        ),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextFormField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'National ID * NIN', border: OutlineInputBorder(), prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.badge)), onSaved: (v)=>nationalId=v!.trim(), validator: (v)=>v!.isEmpty?'Required':null),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
          value: department,
          items: departments.map((d)=> DropdownMenuItem(value:d, child: Text(d))).toList(),
          onChanged:(v)=>setState(()=>department=v!),
          onSaved:(v)=>department=v??'General',
          decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Department *', border: OutlineInputBorder(), prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.business)),
        ),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
          value: role,
          items: roles.map((r)=> DropdownMenuItem(value:r, child: Text(r))).toList(),
          onChanged:(v)=>setState(()=>role=v!),
          onSaved:(v)=>role=v??'Subject Teacher',
          decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Role * e.g HOD', border: OutlineInputBorder(), prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.work)),
          isExpanded:true,
        ),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextFormField(initialValue: defaultPinFromSettings, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Teacher PIN', border: OutlineInputBorder(), prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.pin)), onSaved: (v)=>pin=v!.trim()),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextFormField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Subjects Math,Physics', border: OutlineInputBorder(), prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.book)), onSaved: (v)=>subjects=v!.split(',').map((e)=>e.trim()).where((e)=>e.isNotEmpty).toList()),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextFormField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Classes S.4 Blue, S.3 Red', border: OutlineInputBorder(), prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.class_)), onSaved: (v)=>classesAssigned=v!.split(',').map((e)=>e.trim()).where((e)=>e.isNotEmpty).toList()),
        SizedBox(height:20),
        Card(color: Colors.blue[50], child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(10), child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
          Text('Preview:', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          Text('Phone: $phone | Gender: $gender | NIN: $nationalId'),
          Text('Role: $role | Dept: $department', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1))),
        ]))),
        SizedBox(height:12),
        ElevatedButton(onPressed: isEnrolling? null : saveTeacher, child: Text(isEnrolling? 'Saving...' : 'SAVE TEACHER'), style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), minimumSize: Size(double.infinity, 50))),
      ])),
    );
  }
}

// === ATTENDANCE FIXED ===
class TeacherFingerprintAttendancePage extends StatefulWidget {
  final dynamic curUser;
  TeacherFingerprintAttendancePage({required this.curUser});
  @override _TeacherFingerprintAttendancePageState createState()=>_TeacherFingerprintAttendancePageState();
}
class _TeacherFingerprintAttendancePageState extends State<TeacherFingerprintAttendancePage> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  final LocalAuthentication auth = LocalAuthentication();
  String msg='Loading...'; bool success=false;
  AttendanceConfig config=AttendanceConfig();
  List<TeacherProfile> allTeachers=[]; TeacherProfile? selectedTeacher;
  String search=''; Position? currentPos; late TabController tabCtrl;
  String currentOverrideCode='BSS2025';

  @override void initState(){ super.initState(); tabCtrl=TabController(length: 2, vsync: this); loadAll(); }

  Future<void> loadAll() async {
    var cfgDoc=await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('app_settings').doc('attendance_config').get();
    if(cfgDoc.exists){ config=AttendanceConfig.fromMap(cfgDoc.data()!); currentOverrideCode=config.htOverrideCode; }
    await getCurrentLocation();
    // LIVE teachers
    FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('teacher_profiles').snapshots().listen((snap){
      if(!mounted) return;
      setState((){
        allTeachers=snap.docs.map((e)=>TeacherProfile.fromMap(e.data())).toList();
        if(widget.curUser.role!='HT'){
          try{
            selectedTeacher=allTeachers.firstWhere((t)=> t.email.toLowerCase().trim()==widget.curUser.email.toString().toLowerCase().trim() || t.name.toLowerCase().trim()==widget.curUser.name.toString().toLowerCase().trim());
            msg='Welcome ${selectedTeacher!.name} - Tap MARK';
          }catch(_){
            selectedTeacher=null;
            msg='Your profile not found for ${widget.curUser.email} - Tap FIX below';
          }
        } else {
          msg='HT Mode: Select teacher to mark';
        }
      });
    });
  }

  Future<void> getCurrentLocation() async {
    try{
      if(!await Geolocator.isLocationServiceEnabled()) return;
      LocationPermission perm=await Geolocator.checkPermission();
      if(perm==LocationPermission.denied){ perm=await Geolocator.requestPermission(); }
      var pos=await Geolocator.getCurrentPosition(desiredAccuracy: LocationAccuracy.high);
      setState(()=>currentPos=pos);
    }catch(_){}
  }

  Future<void> createMissingProfile() async {
    var email = widget.curUser.email.toString().toLowerCase().trim();
    var name = widget.curUser.name.toString();
    var id = 'BSS/T/${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch.toString().substring(8)}';
    var p = TeacherProfile(staffId: id, name: name, email: email, phone: '', gender: 'Male', nationalId: '', role: 'Subject Teacher', subjects: [], classesAssigned: [], pinCode: config.defaultTeacherPin, dateJoined: DateTime.now());
    await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('teacher_profiles').doc(id).set(p.toMap());
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('✅ Created profile $id with PIN ${p.pinCode}'), backgroundColor: Colors.green));
  }

  void showPinFlow(){
    if(selectedTeacher==null) return;
    String pinInput='', htInput='';
    showDialog(context: context, builder: (ctx)=>AlertDialog(
      title: Text('Verify ${selectedTeacher!.name}'),
      content: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
        Text('Staff: ${selectedTeacher!.staffId} | PIN: ${selectedTeacher!.pinCode}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11)),
        if(currentPos!=null) Text('GPS: ${currentPos!.latitude.toStringAsFixed(5)}, ${currentPos!.longitude.toStringAsFixed(5)}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10, color: Colors.blue)),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Teacher PIN', border: OutlineInputBorder()), obscureText:true, onChanged: (v)=>pinInput=v),
        SizedBox(height:8),
        TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'OR HT Override $currentOverrideCode', border: OutlineInputBorder()), onChanged: (v)=>htInput=v),
      ]),
      actions: [
        TextButton(onPressed: ()=>Navigator.pop(ctx), child: Text('Cancel')),
        ElevatedButton(onPressed: () async {
          Navigator.pop(ctx);
          if(htInput==currentOverrideCode){
            await doAuthAndSave(selectedTeacher!, 'HT_OVERRIDE');
          } else if(pinInput==selectedTeacher!.pinCode){
            await doAuthAndSave(selectedTeacher!, 'PIN+GPS+BIO');
          } else {
            setState(()=>msg='❌ Wrong PIN');
          }
        }, child: Text('Verify'))
      ],
    ));
  }

  Future<void> doAuthAndSave(TeacherProfile teacher, String method) async {
    try{
      bool canCheck=await auth.canCheckBiometrics;
      bool didAuth=true;
      if(canCheck){
        didAuth = await auth.authenticate(
  localizedReason: 'Confirm ${teacher.name}',
  biometricOnly: false,
);
      }
      if(didAuth) await saveAttendance(teacher, method);
      else setState(()=>msg='❌ Fingerprint failed');
    }catch(e){ await saveAttendance(teacher, 'PIN_FALLBACK'); }
  }

  Future<void> saveAttendance(TeacherProfile teacher, String method) async {
    await getCurrentLocation();
    var now=DateTime.now();
    double lat=currentPos?.latitude??0; double lng=currentPos?.longitude??0;
    double dist=0; bool within=true;
    if(lat!=0){ dist=Geolocator.distanceBetween(config.schoolLat, config.schoolLng, lat, lng); within=dist <= config.radiusM; }
    // LATE CHECK vs 08:00 AM setting
    int lateTotalMinutes = config.lateHour*60 + config.lateMinute;
    int nowTotalMinutes = now.hour*60 + now.minute;
    bool isLate = nowTotalMinutes > lateTotalMinutes;
    int lateMins = isLate? nowTotalMinutes - lateTotalMinutes : 0;
    String status = isLate? 'LATE' : 'PRESENT';
    var att=TeacherAttendance(
      id: now.millisecondsSinceEpoch.toString(),
      staffId: teacher.staffId,
      name: teacher.name,
      photoUrl: teacher.photoUrl,
      date: now.toString().split(' ')[0],
      time: '${now.hour.toString().padLeft(2,'0')}:${now.minute.toString().padLeft(2,'0')}',
      timestamp: now,
      method: method,
      verifiedBy: widget.curUser.role=='HT'? 'HT:${widget.curUser.name}' : 'SELF',
      lat: lat, lng: lng, distanceM: dist, withinFence: within,
      isLate: isLate, lateMinutes: lateMins, status: status
    );
    await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('teacher_attendance').doc(att.id).set(att.toMap());
    setState(()=>{ msg='✅ ${teacher.name} $status at ${att.time} ${within? 'Inside' : 'Outside ${dist.toStringAsFixed(0)}m'} ${isLate? 'LATE by ${lateMins}m (after ${config.lateTimeStr})':''}', success=true });
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    bool isHT=widget.curUser.role=='HT';
    var filtered=allTeachers.where((t)=> search.isEmpty || t.name.toLowerCase().contains(search.toLowerCase())).toList();
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('T-Attend + GPS'), backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1), bottom: TabBar(controller: tabCtrl, tabs: [Tab(text:'MARK'), Tab(text:'TODAY LIST (${allTeachers.length} Teachers)')])),
      body: TabBarView(controller: tabCtrl, children: [
        // TAB 1 MARK
        Column(children: [
          Container(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), color: success? Colors.green[50] : Colors.blue[50], width: double.infinity, child: Column(children: [
            Icon(Icons.fingerprint, size:60, color: success? Colors.green : Color(0xFF0D47A1)),
            Text(msg, textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, decoration: TextDecoration.none)),
            if(currentPos!=null) Text('GPS: ${currentPos!.latitude.toStringAsFixed(5)},${currentPos!.longitude.toStringAsFixed(5)} | School ${config.schoolLat},${config.schoolLng} | ${config.radiusM}m', style: TextStyle(fontSize:9), textAlign: TextAlign.center),
            if(selectedTeacher==null &&!isHT) ElevatedButton(onPressed: createMissingProfile, child: Text('FIX: Create My Profile Now'), style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.orange)),
            if(selectedTeacher!=null) Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.only(top:10), child: ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.verified_user), label: Text('MARK ${selectedTeacher!.name}'), onPressed: showPinFlow, style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.green.shade700, minimumSize: Size(double.infinity, 48)))),
          ])),
          if(isHT)...[
            Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Search teacher', prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search), border: OutlineInputBorder(), isDense:true), onChanged: (v)=>setState(()=>search=v))),
            Expanded(child: ListView.builder(itemCount: filtered.length, itemBuilder: (_,i){ var t=filtered[i]; bool sel=selectedTeacher?.staffId==t.staffId; return ListTile(leading: CircleAvatar(child: Text(t.name.isNotEmpty? t.name[0] : '?')), title: Text(t.name, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, decoration: TextDecoration.none)), subtitle: Text('${t.staffId} | PIN:${t.pinCode}', style: TextStyle(decoration: TextDecoration.none)), selected: sel, selectedTileColor: Colors.green[50], trailing: sel? Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: Colors.green):null, onTap: ()=>setState(()=>selectedTeacher=t)); })),
          ] else...[
            if(allTeachers.isNotEmpty) Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: Card(child: ListTile(leading: CircleAvatar(child: Text(selectedTeacher?.name[0]??'?')), title: Text(selectedTeacher?.name??'Loading...'), subtitle: Text('PIN: ${selectedTeacher?.pinCode??''}')))),
          ]
        ]),
        // TAB 2 TODAY + ENROLLED
        Column(children: [
          Container(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), color: Colors.indigo[50], child: Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround, children: [Text('Enrolled: ${allTeachers.length}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), Text('GPS Radius: ${config.radiusM}m'), IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.refresh), onPressed: loadAll)])),
          Expanded(child: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
            stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('teacher_attendance').orderBy('timestamp', descending: true).limit(100).snapshots(),
            builder: (ctx, snap){
              if(!snap.hasData) return Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
              if(snap.data!.docs.isEmpty){
                return Center(child: Column(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children: [
                  Icon(Icons.list_alt, size:60, color: Colors.grey),
                  Text('No attendance yet', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, decoration: TextDecoration.none)),
                  Text('Enrolled teachers: ${allTeachers.length}\nThey must tap MARK to appear here', textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: TextStyle(decoration: TextDecoration.none)),
                  SizedBox(height:10),
                 ...allTeachers.map((t)=> Card(child: ListTile(title: Text(t.name, style: TextStyle(decoration: TextDecoration.none)), subtitle: Text('${t.staffId} | ${t.email} | PIN ${t.pinCode}', style: TextStyle(decoration: TextDecoration.none)), trailing: Text('Not Marked Today', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.red, decoration: TextDecoration.none))))).toList(),
                ]));
              }
              var docs=snap.data!.docs;
              return ListView.builder(itemCount: docs.length, itemBuilder: (_,i){
                var m=docs[i].data() as Map;
                bool inside=m['withinFence']??true;
                return Card(child: ListTile(
                  leading: CircleAvatar(backgroundColor: inside? Colors.green: Colors.orange, child: Icon(Icons.person, color: Colors.white)),
                  title: Text('${m['name']} - ${m['time']} ${m['date']}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:13, decoration: TextDecoration.none)),
