                  subtitle: Text('${m['staffId']} | ${m['method']} | ${m['distanceM']?.toStringAsFixed(0)??'0'}m ${inside? 'INSIDE':'OUTSIDE'} | ${m['lat']?.toStringAsFixed(4)},${m['lng']?.toStringAsFixed(4)}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, decoration: TextDecoration.none)),
                  trailing: Icon(inside? Icons.check_circle : Icons.warning, color: inside? Colors.green : Colors.red),
                ));
              });
            },
          )),
        ]),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// ===== INVENTORY & REPORTS PAGE - HOD + HT =====
class InventoryPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final AppUser curUser;
  InventoryPage({required this.curUser});
  @override _InventoryPageState createState()=>_InventoryPageState();
}
class _InventoryPageState extends State<InventoryPage> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late TabController tabCtrl;
  String selectedDept = 'All';
  String searchQ = '';
  List<String> departments = ['All','Science','Arts','Sports','Library','Administration','Boarding','ICT','General'];
  List<String> categories = ['Lab Equipment','Furniture','Sports Equipment','Books','Stationery','Electronics','General'];
  List<String> conditions = ['New','Good','Fair','Damaged','Lost'];

  @override void initState(){ super.initState(); tabCtrl=TabController(length: 3, vsync:this); }

  // Get current teacher profile to know department
  Future<TeacherProfile?> getMyProfile() async {
    try{
      var snap = await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('teacher_profiles')
       .where('email', isEqualTo: widget.curUser.email.toLowerCase().trim()).limit(1).get();
      if(snap.docs.isNotEmpty) return TeacherProfile.fromMap(snap.docs.first.data());
      // fallback by name
      var snap2 = await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('teacher_profiles')
       .where('name', isEqualTo: widget.curUser.name).limit(1).get();
      if(snap2.docs.isNotEmpty) return TeacherProfile.fromMap(snap2.docs.first.data());
    }catch(_){}
    return null;
  }

  void showAddEdit({InventoryItem? edit, required TeacherProfile myProfile, required bool isHT}){
    var nameC = TextEditingController(text: edit?.name??'');
    var qtyC = TextEditingController(text: (edit?.quantity??1).toString());
    var locC = TextEditingController(text: edit?.location??'');
    var priceC = TextEditingController(text: (edit?.price??0).toString());
    var notesC = TextEditingController(text: edit?.notes??'');
    String dept = edit?.department?? (isHT? 'Science' : myProfile.department);
    String cat = edit?.category?? 'General';
    String cond = edit?.condition?? 'Good';
    String status = edit?.status?? 'Available';

    showDialog(context: context, builder: (ctx)=> StatefulBuilder(builder: (ctx2,setS){
      return AlertDialog(
        title: Text(edit==null? '➕ Add Inventory - $dept' : '✏️ Edit ${edit!.name}'),
        content: SingleChildScrollView(child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
          TextField(controller: nameC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Item Name * e.g Microscope', border: OutlineInputBorder())),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          Row(children:[
            Expanded(child: DropdownButtonFormField<String>(value: isHT? dept : dept, items: (isHT? departments.where((d)=>d!='All').toList() : [myProfile.department]).map((d)=> DropdownMenuItem(value:d, child: Text(d))).toList(), onChanged: isHT? (v)=>setS(()=>dept=v!): null, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Department', border: OutlineInputBorder()))),
            SizedBox(width:8),
            Expanded(child: DropdownButtonFormField<String>(value: cat, items: categories.map((e)=> DropdownMenuItem(value:e, child: Text(e))).toList(), onChanged:(v)=>setS(()=>cat=v!), decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Category', border: OutlineInputBorder()))),
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          Row(children:[
            Expanded(child: TextField(controller: qtyC, keyboardType: TextInputType.number, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Qty', border: OutlineInputBorder()))),
            SizedBox(width:8),
            Expanded(child: DropdownButtonFormField<String>(value: cond, items: conditions.map((e)=> DropdownMenuItem(value:e, child: Text(e))).toList(), onChanged:(v)=>setS(()=>cond=v!), decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Condition', border: OutlineInputBorder()))),
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          Row(children:[
            Expanded(child: TextField(controller: locC, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Location e.g Lab 1', border: OutlineInputBorder()))),
            SizedBox(width:8),
            Expanded(child: TextField(controller: priceC, keyboardType: TextInputType.number, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Price UGX', border: OutlineInputBorder()))),
          ]),
          SizedBox(height:8),
          TextField(controller: notesC, maxLines:2, decoration: InputDecoration(labelText:'Notes', border: OutlineInputBorder())),
        ])),
        actions:[
          TextButton(onPressed: ()=> Navigator.pop(ctx), child: Text('Cancel')),
          ElevatedButton(style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1)), onPressed: () async {
            if(nameC.text.trim().isEmpty) return;
            var now = DateTime.now();
            var item = InventoryItem(
              id: edit?.id?? now.millisecondsSinceEpoch.toString(),
              name: nameC.text.trim(),
              category: cat,
              department: dept,
              quantity: int.tryParse(qtyC.text)??1,
              condition: cond,
              location: locC.text.trim(),
              price: double.tryParse(priceC.text)??0,
              purchaseDate: now.toString().split(' ')[0],
              recordedBy: myProfile.staffId,
              recordedByName: myProfile.name,
              timestamp: now,
              status: status,
              notes: notesC.text.trim(),
            );
            await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('inventories').doc(item.id).set(item.toMap());
            Navigator.pop(ctx);
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(edit==null? '✅ ${item.name} added to $dept' : '✅ Updated'), backgroundColor: Colors.green));
          }, child: Text(edit==null? 'SAVE' : 'UPDATE')),
        ],
      );
    }));
  }

  @override Widget build(BuildContext c){
    bool isHT = widget.curUser.role=='HT';
    return FutureBuilder<TeacherProfile?>(
      future: getMyProfile(),
      builder: (ctx, profSnap){
        TeacherProfile? myProfile = profSnap.data;
        bool isHOD = myProfile?.isHOD?? false;
        String myDept = myProfile?.department?? 'General';
        bool canRecord = isHT || isHOD; // HOD can record all for his dept, HT all

        return Scaffold(
          appBar: AppBar(
            title: Text(isHT? 'Inventories & Reports - All Depts' : isHOD? 'Inventory - $myDept Dept (HOD)' : 'Inventory - $myDept'),
            backgroundColor: Color(0xFF0D47A1),
            bottom: TabBar(controller: tabCtrl, tabs: [
              Tab(text: isHT? 'All Items' : 'My Dept', icon: Icon(Icons.inventory)),
              Tab(text: 'Reports', icon: Icon(Icons.analytics)),
              Tab(text: 'By Dept', icon: Icon(Icons.category)),
            ]),
          ),
          floatingActionButton: canRecord? FloatingActionButton.extended(
            icon: Icon(Icons.add),
            label: Text(isHT? 'Add Item' : 'Add to $myDept'),
            backgroundColor: Colors.green.shade700,
            onPressed: (){
              if(myProfile==null &&!isHT){
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Profile not found - contact HT'), backgroundColor: Colors.red));
                return;
              }
              showAddEdit(myProfile: myProfile?? TeacherProfile(staffId:'HT', name: widget.curUser.name, email: widget.curUser.email, department: 'Administration', dateJoined: DateTime.now(), role: 'HT'), isHT: isHT);
            },
          ) : null,
          body: StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot>(
            stream: FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('inventories').orderBy('timestamp', descending: true).snapshots(),
            builder: (ctx, snap){
              if(!snap.hasData) return Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
              var all = snap.data!.docs.map((d)=> InventoryItem.fromMap(d.data() as Map)).toList();

              // Filter logic: HT sees all, HOD sees own dept, teacher sees own dept read-only
              List<InventoryItem> filtered;
              if(isHT){
                filtered = selectedDept=='All'? all : all.where((e)=> e.department==selectedDept).toList();
              } else {
                filtered = all.where((e)=> e.department==myDept).toList();
              }
              if(searchQ.isNotEmpty){
                filtered = filtered.where((e)=> e.name.toLowerCase().contains(searchQ.toLowerCase()) || e.category.toLowerCase().contains(searchQ.toLowerCase())).toList();
              }

              // Reports data
              Map<String,int> byDeptCount = {};
              Map<String,double> byDeptValue = {};
              Map<String,int> byCond = {};
              for(var it in (isHT? all : filtered)){
                byDeptCount[it.department] = (byDeptCount[it.department]??0) + it.quantity;
                byDeptValue[it.department] = (byDeptValue[it.department]??0) + (it.price*it.quantity);
                byCond[it.condition] = (byCond[it.condition]??0) + 1;
              }

              return TabBarView(controller: tabCtrl, children: [
                // TAB 1 LIST
                Column(children:[
                  Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Row(children:[
                    if(isHT) Expanded(child: DropdownButton<String>(value: selectedDept, isExpanded:true, items: departments.map((d)=> DropdownMenuItem(value:d, child: Text(d))).toList(), onChanged:(v)=> setState(()=> selectedDept=v!))),
                    if(isHT) SizedBox(width:8),
                    Expanded(child: TextField(decoration: InputDecoration(prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search), hintText: 'Search item', border: OutlineInputBorder(), isDense:true), onChanged:(v)=> setState(()=> searchQ=v))),
                  ])),
                  if(!isHT) Container(width: double.infinity, color: isHOD? Colors.green[50] : Colors.blue[50], padding: EdgeInsets.all(8), child: Text(isHOD? '✅ HOD $myDept: You can ADD/EDIT all items in $myDept' : '👁️ $myDept: View only - Only HOD can record', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))),
                  Expanded(child: filtered.isEmpty? Center(child: Text('No items in ${isHT? selectedDept : myDept}')) : ListView.builder(itemCount: filtered.length, itemBuilder: (_,i){
                    var it = filtered[i];
                    return Card(child: ListTile(
                      leading: CircleAvatar(backgroundColor: it.condition=='Damaged'? Colors.red : Colors.green, child: Icon(Icons.inventory_2, color: Colors.white)),
                      title: Text('${it.name} x${it.quantity} - ${it.department}', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:13)),
                      subtitle: Text('${it.category} | ${it.condition} | ${it.location} | UGX ${it.price} | By ${it.recordedByName}\n${it.timestamp.toString().split(' ')[0]}', style: TextStyle(fontSize:10)),
                      trailing: canRecord? IconButton(icon: Icon(Icons.edit, color: Colors.blue), onPressed: ()=> showAddEdit(edit: it, myProfile: myProfile!, isHT: isHT)) : null,
                      onLongPress: canRecord? () async {
                        bool ok = await showDialog(context: context, builder: (ctx)=> AlertDialog(title: Text('Delete ${it.name}?'), actions: [TextButton(onPressed: ()=> Navigator.pop(ctx,false), child: Text('Cancel')), ElevatedButton(onPressed: ()=> Navigator.pop(ctx,true), child: Text('Delete'), style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: Colors.red))]))??false;
                        if(ok) await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('inventories').doc(it.id).delete();
                      } : null,
                    ));
                  })),
                ]),
                // TAB 2 REPORTS
                ListView(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), children:[
                  Card(color: Colors.indigo[50], child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children:[
                    Text('📊 Reports Summary', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize:16)),
                    SizedBox(height:8),
                    Text('Total Items: ${isHT? all.length : filtered.length} | Total Qty: ${byDeptCount.values.fold(0,(a,b)=>a+b)}'),
                    Divider(),
                   ...byDeptCount.entries.map((e)=> ListTile(dense:true, title: Text('${e.key} Dept'), subtitle: Text('Qty: ${e.value} | Value: UGX ${byDeptValue[e.key]?.toStringAsFixed(0)}'), leading: Icon(Icons.business))),
                    Divider(),
                    Text('By Condition:', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                   ...byCond.entries.map((e)=> Chip(label: Text('${e.key}: ${e.value}'))),
                  ]))),
                  SizedBox(height:10),
                  ElevatedButton.icon(icon: Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf), label: Text('Export Summary'), onPressed: (){
                    // Simple print/share via dialog for now
                    String report = 'BUSALAMU SS INVENTORY REPORT ${DateTime.now().toString().split(' ')[0]}\n\n';
                    byDeptCount.forEach((k,v)=> report+='$k: Qty $v - Value UGX ${byDeptValue[k]}\n');
                    showDialog(context: context, builder: (ctx)=> AlertDialog(title: Text('Report'), content: SingleChildScrollView(child: Text(report)), actions: [TextButton(onPressed: ()=> Navigator.pop(ctx), child: Text('Close'))]));
                  }),
                ]),
                // TAB 3 BY DEPT - HT sees all, HOD sees own
                GridView.builder(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.all(12),
                  gridDelegate: SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(crossAxisCount:2, childAspectRatio:1.2, crossAxisSpacing:8, mainAxisSpacing:8),
                  itemCount: isHT? departments.where((d)=>d!='All').length : 1,
                  itemBuilder: (_,i){
                    String dept = isHT? departments.where((d)=>d!='All').toList()[i] : myDept;
                    int count = all.where((e)=> e.department==dept).length;
                    int qty = all.where((e)=> e.department==dept).fold(0,(a,b)=> a + b.quantity);
                    return Card(elevation:3, child: InkWell(onTap: ()=> setState(()=> {selectedDept=dept, tabCtrl.animateTo(0)}), child: Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(12), child: Column(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children:[
                      Icon(Icons.category, size:40, color: Color(0xFF0D47A1)),
                      SizedBox(height:8),
                      Text(dept, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      Text('$count items', style: TextStyle(fontSize:12)),
                      Text('Qty: $qty', style: TextStyle(fontSize:11, color: Colors.grey[700])),
                    ]))));
                  },
                ),
              ]);
            },
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

class _ModuleItem {
  final String title;
  final IconData icon;
  final Color color;
  final Widget page;
  _ModuleItem(this.title, this.icon, this.color, this.page);
}
